Additionally, ACML introduces a difference-aware attention calibration strategy that adaptively adjusts attention regularization based on feature magnitude and confidence differences across modalities, thereby mitigating biases caused by unreasonable fusion constraints. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal benchmark datasets demonstrate that ACML consistently achieves superior performance and robustness over recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [69] [CCDF: A Benchmark Dataset for Deepfake Detection in Real-World Surveillance Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07939)
*Baptiste Chopin,Thomas Swearingen,Arun Ross,Antitza Dantcheva,Christian Rathgeb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CCtv DeepFakes (CCDF)数据集，包含1840个视频（460真实、1380生成），覆盖16类犯罪与事故场景，由Grok Imagine、Google VEO 3.1和OpenAI Sora 2生成，用于评估深伪检测模型在潜在恶意监控视频场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用视频生成工具可低成本伪造监控画面，可能被用于犯罪举报、选举等高风险场景，误导应急响应或扭曲公共讨论。现有深伪数据集存在两点不足：（1）偏重良性网络内容，而非可能涉及恶意活动的影像；（2）依赖较旧或开源生成器，未反映最新生成系统的能力。

Method: 构建CCDF视频深伪数据集，含1840个视频（460真实、1380生成），涵盖16个犯罪与事故类别，生成内容由三个领先商业系统（Grok Imagine、Google VEO 3.1、OpenAI Sora 2）产出。数据集为高真实度、小规模、人工标注，面向检测模型评估。发布三个版本：原始生成数据、清理版（统一真实与合成样本的视频元数据，防止检测器利用简单线索）、以及模拟低成本后处理攻击的篡改版。使用十种近期先进检测器进行评测，覆盖不同检测方法。

Result: 十种先进检测方法在CCDF上难以可靠区分生成视频与真实视频，尽管它们在现有数据集上报告了强劲性能。结果进一步证实现有数据集不适合评估某些特定威胁。

Conclusion: CCDF揭示了当前深伪检测器在面对最新商业生成系统产出的、涉及高风险场景的监控类伪造视频时存在明显不足，说明需要更贴近真实威胁的数据集来推动检测研究。

Abstract: Due to rapid advances in Generative AI, commercial video generation tools can be used to produce fabricated surveillance footage that can fool both human viewers and automated synthetic video detectors. Since these tools are so widely accessible, a malicious user can create a harmful video clip at minimal cost. The production and dissemination of such videos in high-stakes settings, such as crime reporting and elections, can misdirect emergency response efforts or distort political discourse. Existing deepfake video datasets, used by the research community to develop deepfake detection algorithms, exhibit two limitations: (1) they emphasize benign web content rather than footage of possibly malicious activity, and (2) they rely on older or open-source generators that do not represent recent advances in generative systems. We assemble CCtv DeepFakes (CCDF), a video deepfake dataset, to address both gaps. CCDF contains 1840 videos (460 real and 1380 generated) spanning 16 crime and accident categories, with generated content produced using three leading commercial systems: Grok Imagine, Google VEO 3.1, and OpenAI Sora 2. CCDF is a highly realistic, small-scale, manually annotated dataset targeting evaluation of detection models. We release three versions of the dataset: the raw generated data, a cleaned version in which video metadata are standardized between real and synthetic samples to prevent detectors from exploiting trivial cues, and an altered version simulating low-effort post-processing attacks. We evaluate CCDF with ten recent state-of-the-art detectors covering different detection approaches. Our results suggest that these approaches do not reliably distinguish CCDF's generated videos from real ones, despite their strong reported performance on existing datasets. These results further confirm that existing datasets are not well-suited to evaluating certain threats.

</details>


### [70] [Revisiting Numerical Forecasting Models for Language-Based Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07954)
*JunGyu Lee,Inhwan Bae,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoRE框架，通过强化学习把五个冻结的数值预测器的坐标级先验迁移到基于语言的轨迹预测器中，仅用高熵样本进行PPO微调，在ETH-UCY、SDD、NBA上降低ADE/FDE且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言的轨迹预测器把坐标离散化为token，并学习目的地、群体推理等辅助任务，能捕捉行为意图与社会情境，但token级目标只能间接指导连续坐标空间的动力学，缺乏坐标级反馈。

Method: MoRE（Mixture of Reward Experts）：用五个冻结的数值预测器提供互补的运动与交互坐标级知识，其预测转为专家奖励，通过不确定性加权共识（惩罚分歧）并与真值奖励结合；用预测熵排名前1%的训练样本做PPO精调；专家预测一次性计算并缓存，训练更新与推理阶段均不运行专家。

Result: ETH-UCY上ADE由0.22降至0.20 m，FDE由0.32降至0.29 m；相对基础策略，SDD上ADE降低17.9%，NBA上降低12.7%；ETH-UCY上碰撞率下降，行人间距更接近真值；推理内存与延迟无明显增加。

Conclusion: MoRE将语言模型的上下文建模能力与数值专家的坐标级反馈相结合，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升轨迹预测精度与社交合理性，为跨范式融合提供了可行的精调方案。

Abstract: Language-based trajectory predictors represent coordinates as discrete tokens and learn auxiliary tasks such as destination and group reasoning. This formulation enables the model to capture behavioral intent and social context beyond coordinate dynamics alone. However, token-level objectives provide only indirect guidance for continuous coordinate-space dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce MoRE (Mixture of Reward Experts), a refinement framework that transfers numerical forecasting priors into a pretrained language-based predictor through reinforcement learning. Five frozen numerical predictors provide complementary coordinate-level knowledge of motion and interactions. Their predictions are converted into expert rewards and combined through an uncertainty-weighted consensus that penalizes disagreement. A ground-truth reward anchors the prediction to the target trajectory. To focus refinement on difficult cases, MoRE refines the policy using the top 1% of training samples ranked by predictive entropy. Expert predictions are computed once and cached before PPO training, so the experts are not run during policy updates or inference. In this way, MoRE combines the contextual modeling of the language-based predictor with coordinate-level feedback from numerical experts. On ETH-UCY, MoRE reduces ADE from 0.22 to 0.20 m and FDE from 0.32 to 0.29 m. Relative to the base policy, ADE decreases by 17.9% on SDD and 12.7% on NBA. On ETH-UCY, MoRE also reduces collision rates and better matches ground-truth pedestrian spacing, without increasing measured inference memory or latency. The project page is available at https://jungyu0413.github.io/MoRE/.

</details>


### [71] [DensiTok: Making Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting See More Views Than It Is Given](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07958)
*Minhyeok Lee,Jungho Lee,Minseok Kang,Heeseung Choi,Ig-Jae Kim,Sangyoun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用模块DensiTok，通过直接稠密化前馈3DGS模型的内部几何token，在低维潜空间中用单步流匹配补全未观测视角，无需图像合成即可显著提升稀疏视角重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）虽能单次前向重建场景，但随着输入图像数量减少，质量急剧下降。瓶颈在于重建头之前的内部表示：从少量无位姿视角出发，该表示缺乏未观测区域的证据，导致空洞、漂浮物和模糊。常见的补救方法是用图像或视频生成器合成额外视角像素并重新编码，但这代价高昂且本质上不具备3D一致性。

Method: DensiTok是一个针对预训练前馈3DGS模型的即插即用模块，直接稠密化其内部几何token，使冻结的主干网络表现得如同观测到更多视角。具体做法：将token压缩到紧凑的潜空间，在相机几何条件下通过单步流匹配完成未观测视角的潜变量补全，再解码回token供原重建头使用。该模块可集成到不同预训练预测器中，主干与重建头均保持冻结。潜空间补全无需图像合成或额外编码器前向传播。

Result: 在三个预训练主干网络和两个基准测试上，DensiTok一致地改善了稀疏视角重建效果，并弥合了与稠密视角重建之间的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 通过稠密化内部几何证据（而非合成像素），DensiTok以低成本、3D一致的即插即用方式有效提升了前馈3DGS在稀疏视角下的重建质量，具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructs a scene in a single forward pass, replacing per-scene optimization with a network trained across many scenes. Its quality, however, degrades sharply as the number of input images drops. The bottleneck is upstream of the reconstruction heads: from a few unposed views, the internal representation they read carries no evidence for unobserved regions, leaving holes, floaters, and blur. The common remedy supplies that evidence as pixels, synthesizing extra views with an image or video generator and re-encoding them, which is costly and not 3D-consistent by construction. We instead densify the evidence itself. We present DensiTok, a plug-in module for pretrained feed-forward 3DGS models that densifies their internal geometry tokens directly, making a frozen backbone behave as though it had observed many more views than it was given. DensiTok compresses those tokens into a compact latent space, completes the latents of the unobserved viewpoints in a single flow-matching step conditioned on camera geometry, and decodes them back into tokens that the original reconstruction heads. The same module design can be integrated into different pretrained predictors while keeping each backbone and its reconstruction heads frozen. Completion in a low-dimensional latent space requires no image synthesis or additional encoder passes. Across three pretrained backbones and two benchmarks, DensiTok consistently improves sparse-view reconstruction and recovers much of the gap to dense-view reconstruction.

</details>


### [72] [EmbodiedSmith: Scaling Embodied Data through Recursive Self-Improvement Flywheel in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07969)
*Yikai Qin,Yifei Deng,Mingjian Liang,Wenxuan Song,Zepeng Lin,Zhiyi Jiang,Jiajun Fu,Qiao Sun,Huashuo Lei,Xicheng Gong,Jiayi Chen,Han Zhao,Shuanghao Bai,Pengxiang Ding,Pengwei Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmbodiedSmith 是一个通过递归自我改进实现可扩展具身数据生成的框架，统一资产、场景与任务生成，支持复杂本体与物理现象，提升数据质量、多样性及下游泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展机器人基础模型需要多样化训练数据与可靠评估环境。仿真虽可扩展，但现有生成流程受限于预定义资产与技能、场景与任务生成脱节，以及对复杂本体和物理支持不足。

Method: 提出 EmbodiedSmith 框架，将资产、场景、任务生成统一于一条支持自主创建与语言驱动定制的流程中。核心是智能体式精炼循环：场景生成预判下游任务需求，任务生成指导针对性的场景编辑，使场景与任务相互迭代改进。框架还支持移动操作器、人形机器人和灵巧手，以及涉及可变形物体与流体的交互。

Result: 联合精炼提升了任务生成成功率，包括长时程任务；框架扩展了生成数据所涵盖的行为范围与物理现象；大量实验验证了所生成数据的质量、多样性与生成效率，下游策略实验表明数据多样性增加可提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: EmbodiedSmith 为机器人预训练与评估提供了灵活的仿真引擎，通过递归自我改进实现可扩展的具身数据生成。

Abstract: Scaling robotic foundation models requires diverse training data and reliable evaluation environments. Simulation offers a scalable solution, yet existing generation pipelines remain constrained by predefined assets and skills, a disconnect between scene generation and task generation, and limited support for complex embodiments and physics. We introduce EmbodiedSmith, a framework for scalable embodied data generation through recursive self-improvement (RSI). EmbodiedSmith unifies asset, scene, and task generation in a pipeline that supports autonomous creation and language-driven customization. Its core is an agentic refinement loop: scene generation anticipates downstream task requirements, while task generation guides targeted scene edits, allowing scenes and tasks to iteratively improve one another. This joint refinement improves task generation success, including for long-horizon tasks. The framework further supports mobile manipulators, humanoids, and dexterous hands, as well as interactions involving deformable objects and fluids, broadening the range of behaviors and physical phenomena represented in generated data. Together, these capabilities provide a flexible simulation engine for both robot pretraining and evaluation. Extensive experiments validate the quality, diversity, and generation efficiency of the resulting data, while downstream policy experiments demonstrate that increased data diversity improves generalization.

</details>


### [73] [M3SunAgent: Monocular 3D Spatial Understanding Agent for Metric Depth Estimation and 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07982)
*Jinsong Zhang,Kejun Wu,Ming Zhu,Renjie Qiao,Chengtao Cai,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M3SunAgent，用LLM做任务规划器统一单目度量深度估计与3D视觉定位，并构建M3SI基准，性能优于对比模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目度量深度估计与3D视觉定位是单目3D空间理解的两大互补基石，但通常由分离框架完成，导致面向具身智能的空间信息获取不灵活、不对齐。

Method: 以LLM作为任务规划器进行空间视觉编程，灵活生成结构化程序并协调工具。实例级深度估计：调用目标检测器定位目标，用深度估计工具在选取点估计深度并聚合为实例级深度。3D视觉定位：用VLM工具定位目标并输出基本空间属性，再结合反投影工具与维度提升工具预测3D边界框。同时构建2910样本的M3SI评测基准。

Result: 实例级单目度量深度估计中，M3SunAgent在所有对比模型中表现最佳，52.61%的预测实例深度误差低于0.25（δ<0.25）。单目3D视觉定位中整体具竞争力，3D mIoU达41.73%，超过SOTA的MonoVLM 3.62%。

Conclusion: M3SunAgent通过LLM驱动的统一智能体框架，有效整合互补的单目3D空间理解任务，验证了统一规划与工具协调的有效性。

Abstract: Monocular metric depth estimation and 3D visual grounding represent the two complementary cornerstones of monocular 3D spatial understanding (M3Sun), from which the fundamental 3D spatial information required by M3Sun can be acquired. However, these complementary tasks are generally conducted by separate frameworks, which pose challenges of inflexible and unaligned spatial information access for embodied intelligence systems. In this paper, we propose a unified agent for monocular 3D spatial understanding (M3SunAgent) that leverages a large language model (LLM) as a task planner for spatial visual programming, which flexibly generate structured programs and coordinate tools. For instance-level metric depth estimation task, M3SunAgent invokes an object detector tool to locate the target, estimates depth at selected points with a depth estimation tool, and aggregates these predictions into an instance-level depth estimate. We also construct the M3Sun Instance (M3SI) dataset, a benchmark with 2,910 samples for evaluation. For monocular 3D visual grounding task, M3SunAgent uses a vision-language model (VLM) tool to locate the target and output basic spatial attributes, then combines back-projection tool with a dimension-lifting tool to predict its 3D bounding box. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of M3SunAgent. Specifically, in evaluations of instance-level monocular metric depth estimation, M3SunAgent achieves the best performance among all compared models, 52.61% of predicted instances are distributed below depth error 0.25 ($δ< 0.25$). In evaluations of monocular 3D visual grounding, M3SunAgent demonstrates overall competitive performance than vision and VLM models, reaching a 3D mean intersection over union (mIoU) of 41.73% and exceeding the state-of-the-art MonoVLM model by 3.62%.

</details>


### [74] [Decide Before You Look: Learning Which Retrieved Memories Deserve Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07984)
*Youxing LI*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PixelTriage：检索后接入的插件，用小型无生成模型读取对话、短注与缩略图，预测每张检索图像像素对回答的边际贡献，从而按其价值决定是否以全分辨率像素送入作答模型，大幅削减视觉 token 且精度基本无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态助手需从含图像的长时记忆中回答问答。检索到的每张图像若以像素形式输入，约需上千视觉 token；若以已存文本代理形式输入，又常丢失问题所问的细节。作者发现像素带来的收益通常只来自一两张检索记忆，且该收益可在不读取任何全分辨率图像的前提下提前预测，据此可按需分配像素预算。

Method: PixelTriage 置于检索之后。一个小模型（不生成文本）读取对话、一条短注以及每张检索记忆的缩略图，预测该记忆的像素会带来多少增益。训练数据为合成记忆回合，标签由冻结的 27B 模型对每个问题分别在含/不含该记忆像素的条件下作答得到。

Result: 搭配 7B 作答模型时，PixelTriage 位于 M³Exam、DMV 与 MemEye 的精度—成本前沿上，仅用 11–23% 的视觉 token 而无显著精度损失；在 DMV 上比打开全部图像的作答速度快 2.9 倍；在相同预算下优于按检索顺序选择和统一降采样两种基线，并可迁移到其他记忆系统以及 397B 的作答模型。

Conclusion: 像素收益高度稀疏且可在作答前预测，据此以轻量插件对检索记忆进行"分诊"、按需加载全像素，是一种高效、可迁移的长时多模态记忆问答方案。

Abstract: Multimodal assistants answer questions from long-term memories that contain images. After retrieval, each retrieved image reaches the answering model either as pixels, at about a thousand visual tokens per image, or as a stored text proxy that often misses the detail the question asks about. We find that the benefit of pixels usually comes from one or two retrieved memories, and that it can be predicted before the answering model runs, without reading any full-resolution image. In PixelTriage, a plug-in placed after retrieval, a small model that does not generate text reads the dialogue, a short note and a thumbnail of each retrieved memory and predicts how much its pixels would add. It is trained on synthetic memory episodes labeled by a frozen 27B model that answers each question with and without each memory's pixels. With a 7B answering model, PixelTriage lies on the accuracy--cost frontier of M$^3$Exam, DMV and MemEye and uses 11--23\% of the visual tokens without a significant loss of accuracy. On DMV it answers 2.9 times faster than opening all images. It outperforms retrieval order and uniform down-sizing at equal budgets and transfers to other memory systems and to a 397B answering model.

</details>


### [75] [VisionWeave: Weaving Elastic Visual Representations as a Native Capability of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07987)
*Yuan Feng,Qize Yang,Ruizhe Chen,Sibo Song,Haolin He,Muzhi Zhu,Zihan Liu,Yunfei Chu,Xize Cheng,Yuxuan Wang,Jin Xu,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionWeave 提出"弹性视觉表示编织"能力，让多模态大模型端到端学习在何处、以何种粒度分配视觉表示，在保留 98.9% 原生性能的同时平均节省 43.0% token，并在 SGLang 上实现 2.3 倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型将输入编码为稠密固定尺寸的 patch token，成本高昂。视觉信息分布不均：部分区域需细粒度细节，部分区域可用紧凑表示。降采样损失细节，而现有 token 剪枝与自适应方法在内容自适应粒度、任务泛化性及与现代 MLLM 和服务基础设施的集成上仍受限。作者认为需让基础模型端到端学习"在哪里、以何种粒度"分配视觉表示。

Method: 提出 VisionWeave，通过大规模训练在前沿 MLLM 中建立该能力，包含两个组件：(1) 门控空间池化器（gated spatial pooler），在共享 MRoPE 坐标下构建粗粒度表示与原生细粒度表示；(2) 粒度路由器（granularity router），学习内容自适应的表示分配。仅通过自蒸馏即可实现该能力，先在 Qwen3.5-4B 上验证，再扩展至 Qwen3.8-27B，训练量超 30K A100 GPU 小时。

Result: 基于 Qwen3.8-27B，VisionWeave 根据视觉内容自适应调整 token 节省比例，在八个基准上平均节省 43.0% token 同时保留 98.9% 原生性能；而固定 50% 节省目标的 token 剪枝基线仅保留 88% 性能。在多样任务、分辨率和视频帧上均验证了稳健的效率-质量权衡。部署于 SGLang 推理引擎时，实现 2.3 倍吞吐提升，平均 TTFT 降低 54.4%，平均 TPOT 降低 60.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明弹性视觉编织是下一代多模态模型的有前景的能力方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have become the dominant paradigm for visual understanding, but incur substantial costs by encoding inputs into dense, fixed-size patch tokens. However, visual information is unevenly distributed: some regions require fine-grained detail, while others admit compact representations. Downsampling sacrifices this detail, while existing token pruning and adaptive approaches remain limited in content-adaptive granularity, task generalization, and integration with modern MLLMs and serving infrastructure. Overcoming these limitations calls for foundation models that learn, end to end, where-and at what granularity-to allocate visual representations, a native capability we term elastic visual representation weaving. We introduce VisionWeave, establishing this capability in frontier-level MLLMs through large-scale training. It combines two components: a gated spatial pooler constructs coarse-grained representations alongside native fine-grained representations within a shared MRoPE coordinate, while a granularity router learns their content-adaptive allocation. Through self-distillation alone, we validate this capability on Qwen3.5-4B and scale to Qwen3.8-27B with over 30K A100 GPU-hours. Based on Qwen3.8-27B, VisionWeave adaptively adjusts token savings to visual content, saving 43.0% tokens on average while retaining 98.9% native performance across eight benchmarks, versus only 88% performance preserved for token pruning baselines with a fixed 50% savings target. Extensive evaluations confirm robust efficiency-quality trade-offs across diverse tasks, resolutions and video frames. When deployed on SGLang serving engine, our method achieves a 2.3x throughput gain while reducing mean TTFT by 54.4% and mean TPOT by 60.6%. Together, we believe these results position elastic visual weaving as a promising capability for next-generation multimodal models.

</details>


### [76] [PhysTacGen: Physics-Aware Visual-Tactile Sensor Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08068)
*Guo Tang,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysTacGen是一个视觉到光学触觉图像生成框架，通过整合材料感知描述与几何条件，生成光学触觉图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实物理交互是具身智能的基础，但采集配对的视觉-触觉数据成本高昂。视觉到触觉合成可增强数据，但视觉外观与接触相关材料属性之间的差距、配对观察中的空间错位使映射学习困难。

Method: 1) 提出组触觉策略优化(GTPO)，一种强化学习策略，通过任务特定奖励优化视觉-语言模型生成结构化材料描述；2) 结合基于DINOv2的配对筛选与单目相对深度估计，选择训练对并提供几何先验；3) 使用SDXL ControlNet，以RGB、相对深度和GTPO生成文本为条件合成光学触觉图像。

Result: 在筛选的SSVTP数据上，结构相似性优于对比基线；盲测用户研究偏好GTPO生成的描述；生成的触觉输入提升了基于属性推导的力系数预测代理任务性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明PhysTacGen在光学触觉图像合成方面有效，并在所评估的下游任务中具有实用性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Realistic physical interaction is a cornerstone of embodied intelligence, yet collecting paired visual--tactile data remains costly. Visual-to-tactile synthesis offers a promising approach to augmenting such data, but learning this mapping is complicated by the gap between visual appearance and contact-related material properties, as well as spatial misalignment in paired observations. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{PhysTacGen}, a visual-to-optical-tactile image generation framework that integrates material-aware descriptions with geometric conditioning. First, we introduce Group Tactile Policy Optimization (GTPO), a reinforcement learning strategy that refines a vision--language model to generate structured material descriptions using task-specific rewards. Second, we combine DINOv2-based pair curation with monocular relative-depth estimation to select training pairs and provide geometric priors. Finally, an SDXL ControlNet synthesizes optical tactile images conditioned on RGB, relative depth, and GTPO-generated text. Experiments on curated SSVTP data demonstrate improved structural similarity over the compared baselines, while a blinded user study shows a preference for GTPO-generated descriptions. Generated tactile inputs also improve performance on an attribute-derived force-coefficient prediction proxy. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of PhysTacGen for optical tactile image synthesis and its utility in the evaluated downstream task.The code will be available at https://github.com/VDIGPKU/PhysTacGen.

</details>


### [77] [Two Halves are More than One: Phase-wise Velocity Distillation for Fast and High-Quality Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08070)
*Zhen Guo,Rongyuan Wu,Qiaosi Yi,Chenxi Xie,Xinyu Wei,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Phase-wise Velocity Distillation (PVD)，将生成过程分为粗、细两阶段，各用半规模专家模型建模平均速度，在单次全骨干前向计算预算内实现高质量单步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型规模增大导致推理成本高；现有蒸馏方法虽能减少步数，但在单次全骨干前向计算预算下难以保证质量。单步方法把预算分配给单一整体学生模型，难以拟合从粗到细的异构传输，易产生过度平滑的输出。

Method: PVD 将生成时间线划分为粗阶段和细阶段，用平均速度刻画各阶段内的转换；为每个阶段分配一个半规模的专用专家模型，从而解耦结构组成与细节精修，同时保持累计计算量等同于一次全骨干前向。

Result: 在类别条件生成上，ImageNet 256×256 达到 FID 1.48；在文本到图像任务上，蒸馏后的 Stable Diffusion 3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-dev、Qwen-Image 结果与多步教师相当，显著优于先前蒸馏方法；活跃参数减少 49.10–50.89%，峰值显存降低 45.76–48.36%。

Conclusion: 两个半规模阶段专家优于单一全规模整体学生，PVD 在单步预算下实现高质量生成并大幅降低计算与显存开销，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based image generation backbones have grown substantially in scale, making the network inference cost increase rapidly. While diffusion distillation techniques can reduce the number of inference steps, high-quality image generation within a single full-backbone-forward compute budget remains challenging. Existing one-step methods typically allocate this budget to a single evaluation of a monolithic student. However, approximating the heterogeneous coarse-to-fine transport with a single monolithic mapping is difficult and often leads to over-smoothed outputs. To address this issue, we propose Phase-wise Velocity Distillation (PVD), which partitions the generation timeline into a coarse and a fine phase, and models the transition within each phase via the average velocity. A dedicated half-sized expert is assigned to each phase, decoupling structural composition from detail refinement while keeping the cumulative computation equivalent to one full-backbone forward pass. We show that the use of two half-sized phase-specific experts outperforms a single full-size monolithic student. On class-conditional image generation, PVD achieves an FID of 1.48 on ImageNet 256 x 256. On more complex text-to-image (T2I) tasks, PVD-distilled models (Stable Diffusion 3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-dev, Qwen-Image) produce results competitive with their multi-step teachers, significantly outperforming prior distillation methods. Moreover, across the evaluated T2I backbones, PVD reduces active parameters by 49.10-50.89% and peak VRAM by 45.76-48.36% compared to the corresponding teachers. Source code and distilled models are available at https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/PVD.

</details>


### [78] [Multi-Dataset Diagnostic Utility of Clinical Visual Concepts in AI Systems for Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08086)
*Linda Wermelinger,Simone Lionetti,Fabian Gröger,Nipun Ranasekara,Philippe Gottfrois,Ludovic Amruthalingam,Labelling Consortium,Marc Pouly,Alexander A. Navarini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkinLex，一个整合四个公开数据集、涵盖48个临床形态属性、共20,411条记录的统一皮肤科数据集，用于研究可解释视觉概念与诊断准确性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在数字皮肤科中的临床应用高度依赖人类信任，而临床可解释的视觉概念可作为中间表示来增强信任与可靠性。但该领域研究受限于分散、异构的数据集标注。

Method: 构建SkinLex统一数据集，整合SkinCon、DermaCon-IN、MM-Skin和PASSION四个公开数据集，涵盖48个临床形态属性。进行监督式九分类皮肤状况诊断实验，比较仅使用特定视觉特征组（如形状或颜色）的效果；并采用自助法（bootstrapped）后向消除分析48个视觉概念的冗余性。

Result: 将特征限制于特定视觉组（如仅形状或仅颜色）会降低诊断准确率；后向消除显示48个视觉概念在九分类诊断任务中存在一定冗余，说明粗粒度诊断只需相对少量但多样化的临床概念组合。

Conclusion: 该研究支持改进概念分类体系，并可转化为临床收益：减少基于概念模型的输入、提升标注与建模效率、进一步增强可解释性。代码与提示模板已开源。

Abstract: The clinical integration of AI systems in digital dermatology relies heavily on human trust. Clinically interpretable visual concepts can act as intermediate representations enhancing trust and reliability. However, research in this domain is currently limited by scattered, heterogeneous dataset annotations. In this work, we introduce SkinLex, a harmonized dataset of 48 clinical morphological attributes across four public datasets (SkinCon, DermaCon-IN, MM-Skin, and PASSION) for a total of 20,411 records. Supervised nine-partition classification of skin conditions shows that limiting features to specific visual groups, like shapes or colors alone, reduces diagnostic accuracy. Bootstrapped backward elimination reveals that the set of 48 visual concepts has some degree of redundancy for algorithmic nine-partition diagnosis on the examined dataset. This demonstrates that coarse diagnosis on the selected dataset requires a relatively small but varied combination of clinical concepts, and motivates further research to improve concept taxonomy. Results can be translated into clinical benefits by reducing inputs for concept-based models, improving efficiency for annotation and modeling, and further enhancing interpretability. Code and prompt templates are available at https://github.com/Digital-Dermatology/SkinLex.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Training from Scratch: Foundation Models for Data-Efficient and Generalizable Cardiac MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08109)
*Anam Hashmi,Mayug Maniparambil,Julia Dietlmeier,Kathleen M. Curran,Noel E. O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索将预训练视觉基础模型（CLIP、BiomedCLIP、DINOv2）作为先验，集成到基于Transformer的心脏MRI重建框架中，证明其比从头训练的方法更高效、泛化性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏MRI重建方法通常从头训练，需要大量任务特定数据，在数据稀缺和分布偏移情况下鲁棒性不足。作者希望探究预训练视觉基础模型能否作为加速心脏MRI重建的有效先验。

Method: 提出一个重建框架，将冻结及参数高效适配（LoRA）的视觉编码器（CLIP、BiomedCLIP、DINOv2）集成到Transformer重建架构中。

Result: 在CMRxRecon2023和CMRxRecon2024基准上，预训练表示在多个加速倍数下均优于从头训练的Transformer；在有限监督和跨数据集迁移下数据效率与泛化性更优。冻结表示在极端低数据场景下有效，LoRA在中等数据量下带来额外增益，其中DINOv2整体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 视觉基础模型可作为心脏MRI重建的稳健且可迁移的先验，具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging reconstruction aims to recover high-quality images from undersampled acquisitions, enabling faster scans while preserving diagnostic fidelity. Recent reconstruction methods are typically trained from scratch and often require large amounts of task-specific data, limiting their robustness under data scarcity and distribution shifts. In this work, we investigate whether pretrained vision foundation models can serve as effective priors for accelerated cardiac MRI reconstruction. We propose a reconstruction framework that integrates frozen and parameter-efficiently adapted visual encoders, including CLIP, BiomedCLIP, and DINOv2, within a transformer-based reconstruction architecture. Extensive experiments on the CMRxRecon2023 and CMRxRecon2024 benchmarks demonstrate that pretrained representations consistently outperform a transformer trained from scratch across multiple acceleration factors. We further evaluate performance under limited supervision and cross-dataset transfer, showing that foundation models provide superior data efficiency and generalization. While frozen representations are particularly effective in extreme low-data regimes, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) yields additional gains when moderate amounts of training data are available. Among the evaluated backbones, DINOv2 achieves the strongest overall performance. These findings highlight the potential of vision foundation models as robust and transferable priors for cardiac MRI reconstruction.

</details>


### [80] [Supermarket Product Detection and Recognition: Utilizing Deep Learning with Rectified Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08126)
*Mayank Sah,Jimson Mathew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究超市货架商品识别中的角度变化问题，提出用霍夫变换与单应性估计对倾斜图像进行矫正，以提升目标检测模型精度，并分析了矫正方法的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售自动化与工业5.0背景下，自动库存管理和商品目录创建至关重要。目标检测模型虽有较高识别与定位精度，但超市货架密集且拍摄角度多变，单张图像含大量商品且视角倾斜，使模型性能下降，因此需要引入图像矫正手段加以补充。

Method: 将传统霍夫变换（HT）与单应性估计（homography estimation）引入目标检测流程，对倾斜拍摄的图像进行几何矫正；提出构建新数据集以评估矫正效果，并在不同拍摄角度变化和单图商品密度场景下，对多种目标检测模型开展实验与对比分析。

Result: 实验表明，对存在角度倾斜的图像进行矫正可以提升超市商品在图像中的检测精度；同时结果也揭示了图像矫正方法在拍摄角度和图像中物体密度方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 图像矫正可作为目标检测模型的有效补充手段，改善倾斜视角下密集商品图像的识别效果，但其收益受拍摄角度与物体密度限制，未来可基于新数据集进一步研究该边界问题。

Abstract: Product Identification has sprung up to become one of the most challenging problems in the automation of the retail industry. With the new industry 5.0 standards, automated inventory management, and catalog creation tasks are vitally important. Object identification models have emerged as a viable answer with their unprecedented identification and localization accuracy. However, the close-knit rack design of supermarkets generates the problem of angle variation in capturing images. The angle-variant densely packed images(a single image contains many objects) become overwhelming for these models alone. In this paper, we try to supplement object detection models with traditional Hough transform (HT) and homogeneous estimation concepts. We study the effect of rectified images using homography estimation and hough transform and their limitations on the problem of grocery identification. We make a case for creating a new dataset to test the effects of such rectification and produce analytical results on different scenarios of angle variation and object densities per image. Extensive experiments on different object detection models suggest that image rectification of angled images improves the detection accuracy of grocery products in images. The results also highlight the limitation of rectification on the angle of image capture and the object density of the image.

</details>


### [81] [The Failure Is in the Readout: Fine-Grained Emotion Recognition Benchmarks Measure Elicitation, Not Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08162)
*Tobias Hallmen,Fabian Deuser,Robin-Nico Kampa,Norbert Oswald,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出，EmoNet-Face-HQ基准的结论（细粒度情绪识别必须用微调模型EIF）源于其生成式评测协议。改用logits逐类别二分类读取答案后，11个现成VLM均显著超过专家一致性锚点，3个还显著优于EIF；增益来自分级概率而非问答形式，真实照片上的复现结果较弱且混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度情绪识别对治疗工具和社交机器人有价值，但依赖面部数据，带来隐私与数据保护问题。EmoNet-Face-HQ用生成式人像和40类专家标注的细粒度分类体系来规避这一问题，但其自带协议下VLM表现很差，并据此得出结论：必须使用专门微调的模型（EIF）。作者质疑这一结论是否真实反映模型能力，还是评测读取方式造成的假象。

Method: 保留基准的图像、分类体系和专家评分，只改变答案的读取方式：不生成答案，而是针对每个类别做一次二分类查询，从logits中读取答案。以专家一致性（κ_w）为锚点，比较11个开放权重VLM的生成式与验证式（verification）评测表现，并与微调模型EIF（Small/Large）对比。设置控制实验：将同样的概率阈值化为yes/no，以检验增益来源是分级概率还是提问形式。另在真实照片数据集FACES上进行复现。

Result: 专家在其最可靠的五个类别上一致性为κ_w=0.468。生成式下，11个VLM的置信区间均未完全高于该锚点（κ_w=0.268–0.486）。验证式下全部11个模型均超过锚点，κ_w=0.507–0.586，且各自显著更优；其中3个还显著超过EIF（Small κ_w=0.551；Large κ_w=0.534）。增益来自分级概率而非yes/no提问：将概率阈值化会损失平均增益的142%，并使二值化表现低于生成式（κ_w=0.254–0.423）。FACES真实照片复现结果较弱且混合：通过有效性门槛的10个模型中，6个有增益、3个中性偏正、1个为负。

Conclusion: 基准因协议设计而得出的"必须微调专用模型"的结论并不成立：仅改变答案读取方式，现成VLM即可匹配甚至超越微调模型，说明原结论很大程度上是评测方法造成的假象。增益核心在于利用模型的分级概率信息，而非是否以问答形式提问。合成数据上的效应明确，真实照片上效应较弱且不一致，说明该效应并非仅存在于合成数据，但泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Fine-grained emotion recognition supports therapy tools and social robots, but it needs facial data, which raises privacy and data-protection concerns. EmoNet-Face-HQ answers that with generated portraits, expert-rated over a $40$-category taxonomy far finer than the usual six to eight basic emotions. Under the protocol it ships with, vision-language models (VLMs) score poorly on that taxonomy, and the benchmark concludes that a dedicated fine-tuned model is necessary: Empathic-Insight-Face (EIF; Small/Large). We show that off-the-shelf VLMs match or beat that fine-tuned model when the answer is not generated but read from the logits, as one binary query per category. We keep the benchmark's images, taxonomy and ratings, and change only how the answer is read. Experts agree at $κ_w = 0.468$ on the five categories they measure most reliably. Generatively, no interval among eleven open-weight VLMs lies entirely above that anchor ($κ_w=0.268$-$0.486$). Under verification all eleven clear it, each of them significantly better at $κ_w=0.507$-$0.586$. Three also significantly beat EIF sitting at $κ_w = 0.551$ (Small; $0.534$ Large). The gain comes from the graded probability and not from asking a yes/no question: as a control, thresholding those same probabilities to yes/no costs 142% of the average gains and drops binarization below generative elicitation to $κ_w=0.254$-$0.423$. A replication on real photographs (FACES) is weaker and mixed: of the ten models that pass a validity gate, six gain, three are neutral to positive and one is negative, so the effect is not confined to synthetic data.

</details>


### [82] [View Matters: Keyframe-Guided Text-Driven 3D Gaussian Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08179)
*Kaizhe Zhang,Yijie Zhou,Weizhan Zhang,Xuanyu Wang,Feng Lei,Sha Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 View Matters 框架，通过视图重要性估计与关键帧引导编辑，让可靠视角主导文本驱动的 3D 高斯编辑，提升编辑效果与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动 3D 高斯编辑通常不区分各渲染视角的编辑可靠性，而不同视角提供的监督质量差异显著。清晰且与编辑指令匹配的视角能提供可靠引导，信息量低的视角若被同等对待则会削弱编辑效果。

Method: 框架以可靠关键帧为核心：关键帧重要性估计（KIE）结合几何可见性、语义显著性和编辑相关性来识别可靠视角；关键帧引导编辑（KGE）将这些视角的编辑信号非对称地传播到非关键帧，避免噪声反向影响；重要性感知优化（IAO）在 3DGS 优化中保持这种可靠性偏好。

Result: 在 23 组场景-提示对上，该方法取得最高的平均 CLIP 文本-图像相似度 0.2822 和方向相似度 0.2564，编辑耗时约四分钟；相邻视角分析表明，面向保真度的编辑过程保持了跨视角一致性。

Conclusion: 区分并优先利用可靠视角进行 3D 高斯编辑，可有效增强文本-图像对齐与跨视角连贯性，在保持较低编辑耗时的同时优于对比方法。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D Gaussian editing commonly does not distinguish the editing reliability of rendered views, although different viewpoints provide supervision of substantially different quality. Views that clearly show the scene and match the edit instruction provide reliable guidance, while less informative views may weaken the edit when all views are treated equally. We present View Matters, a view-importance-aware framework that conducts editing around reliable keyframes. Keyframe Importance Estimation (KIE) identifies reliable views using geometric visibility, semantic distinctiveness, and edit relevance. Keyframe-Guided Editing (KGE) then propagates their editing signals asymmetrically to non-keyframes without noisy reverse influence, while Importance-Aware Optimization (IAO) preserves this reliability preference during 3DGS optimization. Across 23 scene-prompt pairs, View Matters achieves the highest average CLIP text-image similarity of 0.2822 and directional similarity of 0.2564 among the evaluated methods, with a four-minute editing time. Additional adjacent-view analysis indicates that the fidelity-oriented editing process maintains cross-view coherence.

</details>


### [83] [PIE-PS: Photometric Stereo from Physical Irradiance Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08188)
*Xiangze Meng,Guangyu Li,Jing Li,Di Mei,Songchen Ma,Mingkun Xu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIE-PS是一个基于学习的框架，从原始事件流和已知光照中重建稠密表面法线，通过构建物理辐照事件（PIEs）及其特征（PIEF）绕开未知对比度阈值，并用图神经网络与可靠性评分注意力实现稠密法线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级延迟和高动态范围，适合运动光照下的光度立体。但原始事件稀疏，且依赖未知的对比度阈值，限制了直接物理求解器的应用。

Method: 从事件触发模型推导相邻事件、光照运动与表面法线之间的物理关系，得到纯物理求解器；针对其需阈值、事件量不足及逐像素独立优化的局限，提出PIE-PS：将同像素相邻两事件与其光照方向配对构成物理辐照事件（PIE），定义符号化事件率PIEF（无需阈值）；引入PIE-GNN将每个PIE作为图节点并编码光照对几何以共享时空上下文；用可靠性评分注意力（RGA）预测可靠性权重以降低不可靠PIE的影响，最终经像素聚合得到稠密法线。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上的实验表明，PIE-PS优于先前基于事件的光度立体方法以及直接求解器基线。

Conclusion: PIE-PS通过物理驱动的特征设计与学习式图建模，有效克服了未知阈值与事件稀疏问题，实现了从原始事件流的稠密表面法线重建，优于现有方法与物理基线。

Abstract: Event cameras record asynchronous log-image-irradiance changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range. These properties are useful for photometric stereo under moving illumination, but raw events are sparse and depend on an unknown contrast threshold. We start from the event trigger model and derive a physical relation between adjacent events, light motion, and surface normals. This relation gives a direct physics-only solver, but the solver needs the threshold, enough events at each pixel, and independent per-pixel optimization. To address these limits, we introduce PIE-PS, a learning-based framework for dense surface normal reconstruction from raw event streams and known lighting. We form Physical Irradiance Events (PIEs) by pairing two adjacent events at the same pixel with their corresponding light directions. Each PIE provides a Physical Irradiance Event Feature (PIEF), defined as the signed event rate. PIEF does not require the unknown contrast threshold. To share spatial and temporal context across nearby PIEs, we introduce PIE-GNN, which treats each PIE as a graph node and encodes it with its light-pair geometry. Since the reliability of PIE observations can vary with local appearance, illumination geometry, and sensor noise, Reliability-Grading Attention (RGA) predicts reliability weights to down-weight unreliable PIEs. Pixel aggregation then produces dense normals. Experiments on synthetic and real data show that PIE-PS outperforms prior event-based photometric stereo methods and the direct solver baseline.

</details>


### [84] [MacJEPA: Missingness-Robust Audio-Visual Recognition from Untrimmed Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08192)
*Souptik Sen,Zahra Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MacJEPA将自我中心模态缺失重新定义为未裁剪多事件视频中的局部时序传感器中断，通过窗口级模态dropout模拟与掩码鲁棒性目标，在单一模型/单一阶段训练下同时实现强全输入识别与时序缺失模态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频模型利用互补线索提升自我中心动作识别，但通常假设推理时两条流都可用。现有缺失模态方法仅针对裁剪好的单事件片段，流要么完全存在要么完全缺失；而真实传感器会在长时间、未裁剪观测中发生失效与恢复。因此需要一个面向时序局部中断、且不依赖测试时自适应的统一模型。

Method: 提出MacJEPA（掩码上下文查询JEPA），基于供给的区间查询在音视频上下文中识别视觉动作与声学事件。训练时用窗口局部模态dropout模拟传感器中断；将JEPA中的掩码从自监督 pretext 改写为有监督鲁棒性目标，对齐多模态内容token与任务条件查询的掩码/干净潜表示。所有目标与识别任务在单阶段联合优化，无需测试时适应。

Result: 在Epic-Kitchens-100与Epic-Sounds上，单一checkpoint在完整输入下保持竞争力，并在移除主导流或辅助流时持续超越已发表的缺失模态基线。

Conclusion: MacJEPA在未裁剪多事件视频上，将强全输入识别与时序缺失模态鲁棒性统一于单一模型，把整段缺失作为极限情形，验证了该问题新定义的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Audio-visual models improve egocentric action recognition by exploiting complementary cues, yet typically assume that both streams remain available at inference. Existing missing-modality methods operate on trimmed, single-event clips in which a stream is entirely present or absent, whereas real sensors fail and recover within long, untrimmed observations. We redefine egocentric modality missingness as temporally localized sensor outages within untrimmed, multi-event observations, with whole-clip absence as the limiting case. We introduce \textbf{MacJEPA}, a missing-modality-robust \textbf{Ma}sked-\textbf{c}ontext query \textbf{JEPA} that recognizes visual actions and acoustic events from supplied interval queries over audio-visual context. Window-local modality dropout simulates these sensor outages during training. MacJEPA further repurposes masking in JEPA from a self-supervised pretext into a supervised robustness objective, aligning masked and clean latent representations of both multimodal content tokens and the task-conditioned queries. All objectives are optimized jointly with recognition in a single stage, requiring no test-time adaptation. Across Epic-Kitchens-100 and Epic-Sounds, a single checkpoint remains competitive under complete input and consistently surpasses published missing-modality baselines when either the dominant or auxiliary stream is removed. MacJEPA thus unifies strong full-input recognition with temporal missing-modality robustness in a single model operating on untrimmed multi-event videos.

</details>


### [85] [RACE-FPP: A Robust AI-assisted Characterisation Enhancement for Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08213)
*Osman Ali,Xiangjun Kong,Tibebe Yalew,Waiel Elmadih,Samanta Piano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套完整的条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）标定流程，将深度学习角点检测融入相机标定，并通过采样白方块中心相位标定投影仪，系统分析角点定位误差在整条标定链中的传播。在混合（清晰+退化）数据上，相机重投影误差从1.237像素降至0.259像素，投影仪重投影误差约降低50%，重建件几何精度优于传统流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPP实现可靠三维重建依赖精确的系统标定，而标定精度又高度依赖稳健的棋盘格特征定位；镜头模糊、标定靶姿态等成像条件会使其退化。现有深度学习角点检测器通常仅用检测指标和相机重投影误差评估，未考虑其对投影仪标定、相机-投影仪立体标定一致性及整体测量精度的影响。

Method: 提出完整FPP标定流程：将深度学习角点检测纳入标准相机标定工作流；投影仪标定则通过采样标定靶白色方块中心的相位值来实现。该框架不把角点检测当作孤立任务，而是显式分析定位误差如何沿整条FPP标定链传播。评估采用检测指标（精确率、召回率）、相机与投影仪重投影误差以及相机-投影仪立体标定结果。

Result: 在清晰与退化图像混合数据集上，相机重投影误差由1.237像素降至0.259像素，投影仪重投影误差约降低50%；对重建件的尺寸评估显示，相比传统流程其几何精度得到提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在具有挑战性的成像条件下，系统级标定的鲁棒性得到增强，从而可实现更可靠的工业FPP测量。

Abstract: Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) requires precise system characterisation to achieve reliable three-dimensional (3D) reconstructions; however, characterisation accuracy strongly depends on robust checkerboard feature localisation, which can deteriorate under challenging imaging conditions such as lens blur and characterisation target orientations. Existing deep learning-based corner detectors are typically assessed using detection metrics and camera reprojection error alone, without considering their wider impact on projector characterisation, camera-projector stereo characterisation consistency, or overall measurement accuracy. In this work, we introduce a complete FPP characterisation pipeline that incorporates deep learning-based corner detection into the standard camera characterisation workflow. We also characterise the projector by sampling phase values at the centres of the white squares in the characterisation target. Rather than treating corner detection as an isolated task, the proposed framework explicitly analyses how localisation errors propagate throughout the entire FPP characterisation chain. Performance is evaluated using detection metrics (e.g., precision and recall), camera and projector reprojection errors, and the camera and projector stereo characterisation. Across a mixed dataset of clean and degraded images, the camera reprojection error is reduced from 1.237 pixels to 0.259 pixels, while the projector reprojection error is reduced by roughly 50%. Dimensional evaluation of reconstructed artefacts shows improved geometric accuracy compared with those resulting from the conventional pipeline. Overall, the findings indicate increased robustness of system-level characterisation under challenging imaging conditions, thereby enabling more reliable industrial FPP measurements.

</details>


### [86] [TSRN-RTVD: Real-Time Video Deblurring System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08230)
*Nikita Alutis,Danila Evsyukov,Egor Chistov,Mikhail Voronin,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出高效视频去模糊系统 TSRN-RTVD，通过显式重建曝光期间相机轨迹来引导复原，在单块消费级 GPU 上以 30 FPS 实时处理，GoPro 数据集上 PSNR 达 30.08 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频采集向手持与边缘设备转移，相机抖动引起的运动模糊变得普遍，降低感知质量并影响下游视觉任务。现有最强去模糊网络虽细节恢复出色，但计算量大、结构复杂，消费级硬件难以实时运行，复原质量与设备端速度之间存在明显鸿沟。

Method: 开发并实现 TSRN-RTVD 系统，在曝光期间显式重建底层相机轨迹，并利用恢复出的运动信息引导复原，将模糊的物理成因转化为驱动锐化的信号。

Result: 系统在单块消费级 GPU 上以 30 FPS 复原视频，在 GoPro 数据集上达到 30.08 dB PSNR；并在消费设备上展示交互式对比可视化（模糊输入与去模糊输出）、实时吞吐量以及恢复的相机轨迹屏显视图。

Conclusion: TSRN-RTVD 通过在复原流程中引入相机轨迹重建，兼顾了去模糊质量与设备端实时性，为消费级硬件上的实时视频去模糊提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As video capture moves to handheld and edge devices, motion blur from camera shake has become a pervasive degradation that lowers perceptual quality and harms downstream vision tasks. The strongest deblurring networks recover impressive detail, yet they remain computationally heavy and overwhelmingly complex, so their quality comes at a cost that consumer hardware cannot pay in real time. This gap between restoration quality and on-device speed is exactly what makes real-time deblurring difficult.
  We developed and implemented TSRN-RTVD, an efficient video deblurring system that explicitly reconstructs the underlying camera trajectory during exposure and uses the recovered motion to guide restoration. This approach turns the physical cause of blur into a signal that drives sharpening. Our system runs on a single consumer GPU and restores the video at 30 FPS while reaching 30.08 dB PSNR on the GoPro dataset. We demonstrate TSRN-RTVD on consumer devices with interactive side-by-side visualization of the blurry input and the deblurred output, live throughput, and an on-screen view of the recovered camera trajectory. Demo video is available at https://youtu.be/3alMwVrVALU.

</details>


### [87] [Whose Face Is It Anyway? A Multi-Model Audit of Facial Affect Recognition on Children, and Why the Gap Is the Head, Not the Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08279)
*Tobias Hallmen,Robin-Nico Kampa,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对五个基于AffectNet预训练的表情模型在儿童群体上进行了统一的多模型审计，发现儿童面部表情识别存在模型无关的性能差距，该差距集中在特定表情形态和特定人群上，且主要源于分类头而非特征表示，仅重校准分类头即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部情感模型几乎全部在成人数据上训练，却日益被应用于儿童的教育、健康和发展研究，存在潜在风险，因此需要对儿童群体的模型表现进行系统、受控的审计。

Method: 通过统一测试框架，对五个AffectNet预训练的表情模型（EmoNet、EmotiEffLib、DDAMFN++、OpenFace 3.0、LibreFace）在四个儿童图像数据集、AffectNet-8验证集和两个自然情境儿童视频数据集上进行受控审计；并使用冻结特征上的线性探针与维度效价/唤醒回归进行分析，以及仅重校准分类头的实验。

Result: 儿童差距具有模型无关性——所有架构从摆拍人脸到自然人脸均性能下降，并共有"恐惧→惊讶"混淆；差距集中在张嘴人脸（被误读为惊讶，与AU26下颌张开相关）和南亚儿童，存在较小的回避凝视惩罚，而闭嘴人脸、白人和黑人儿童、直视凝视则无此问题，偏差跟随表情形态和特定人群而非肤色；线性探针在未见儿童上达0.75–0.91，零样本仅0.48–0.66，说明差距主要在分类头而非表示，而维度效价/唤醒回归在域偏移下急剧退化；仅针对分类头在少量目标数据上重校准，可在两个最大儿童集上为全部五个模型带来+0.13至+0.28的提升，而对成人性能代价可忽略，但增益为分布内且无法跨儿童集合迁移。

Conclusion: 儿童面部表情识别差距是系统性的、可诊断的且部分可修复的：差距主要位于分类头，通过少量目标数据重校准即可显著改善儿童集上的表现而几乎不影响成人性能。研究将发布测试框架、逐样本预测和分析代码，儿童人脸数据因许可限制不对外重新分发。

Abstract: Facial affect models are trained almost entirely on adults, yet are increasingly applied to children in education, health, and developmental research. We present a controlled, multi-model audit of five AffectNet-pretrained expression models (EmoNet, EmotiEffLib, DDAMFN++, OpenFace 3.0, LibreFace) on children, across four child image datasets, the AffectNet-8 validation set, and two spontaneous child video datasets, through one shared harness. Three findings emerge. First, the child gap is model-agnostic: every architecture degrades from posed to naturalistic faces and shares the fear$\rightarrow$surprise confusion. Second, it is concentrated and corroborated across all five models: open-mouth faces (read as surprise, correlating with the AU26 jaw drop) and South-Asian children degrade systematically, with a smaller averted-gaze penalty, while closed-mouth faces, White and Black children, and direct gaze do not; the bias tracks expression morphology and specific populations, not skin tone. Third, the gap is diagnosable: a linear probe on frozen features reaches 0.75-0.91 on unseen children versus 0.48-0.66 zero-shot, so it lies largely in the classifier head, not the representation, whereas dimensional valence/arousal regression degrades sharply under domain shift. Building on this, recalibrating only the head on a little target data recovers $+0.13$ to $+0.28$ on the two largest child sets across all five models at negligible adult cost, though the gain is in-distribution and does not transfer across child collections. We will release the harness, per-sample predictions, and analysis code; the child face data stays license-locked and is never redistributed.

</details>


### [88] [Event Detection in Table Tennis Videos using 2D Keypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08286)
*Rainer Lienhart,Daniel Kienzle,Shin'ichi Satoh,Anastasiia Bilinska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EventNet两阶段流程，仅用2D关键点实现乒乓球视频帧级事件检测，无需预先标注关键事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D球轨迹与旋转估计方法需预先识别球拍触球等关键事件，难以应用于长时未剪辑视频。

Method: 1）提取双方上半身姿态、球台角点与球心的2D关键点，用小型关键点Transformer融合为紧凑表示，对视角/光照/背景干扰鲁棒；2）用Transformer编码器处理时序表示，逐帧预测到前一次与后一次触球的时间距离，提出类余弦时间目标信号；并引入3D重投影视角增强与帧率增强。

Result: Latte-MV数据集F1为91.16%、平均帧偏差0.42；TTHQ数据集为73.08%/1.16；消融实验验证了各架构与训练要素的重要性。

Conclusion: 基于2D关键点的时序建模配合EventNet是乒乓球视频自动事件检测的实用且有前景的方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of automatic, frame-accurate event detection in table tennis videos. Current methods for estimating 3d ball trajectories and ball spin typically require that key events, such as ball-racket contacts, have already been identified in advance. This requirement makes it difficult to apply these methods to longer, unedited video recordings. To overcome this limitation, we propose EventNet, a two-stage pipeline to detect key events: (1) 2d keypoints are extracted of the upper-body poses for both players, table corners and ball center. A small keypoint transformer combines them into a compact representation that is robust to changes in viewpoint, lighting, and background clutter. (2) The temporal sequences of these frame-based representations are processed by a transformer encoder that predicts two time-to-event values for each frame, indicating how close the current frame is to the next and previous ball-racket contact. One novelty is a new, temporal cosine-like target signal. Furthermore, we introduce viewpoint augmentation via 3D reprojection and frame-rate augmentation to improve robustness and generalization. Our extensive ablation study gives deeper insights into the importance of various architectural and training aspects. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves an F1 score of 91.16% and a mean frame deviation between ground truth and predicted frame of 0.42 on the Latte-MV dataset and 73.08% / 1.16 on the challenging TTHQ dataset. Overall, our work demonstrates that 2d keypoint-based temporal modeling with our EventNet architecture is a promising and practical approach for automatic event detection in table tennis videos.

</details>


### [89] [Catastrophic Forgetting in Sequential Thermal Anti-UAV Detection: The Role of Scale-Conditioned Gradient Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08315)
*Khac Duc Giang Nguyen,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在热红外反无人机检测的持续学习场景下，量化了YOLOMG的顺序微调灾难性遗忘，并比较知识蒸馏与尺度分层回放两种缓解手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于热红外的反无人机系统需随任务数据演进保持精度，但顺序微调会导致对先前任务的灾难性遗忘，而该领域的这一问题尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 以基于YOLOv5的检测器YOLOMG为对象，关闭运动通道、仅用单路热红外流，在Anti-UAV-RGBT、Anti-UAV410、CST Anti-UAV三个难度递增的反无人机基准上顺序训练；采用遗忘度量（FM）评估稳定性-可塑性权衡，测试冻结教师知识蒸馏（KD）与尺度分层牧群（SSH，300样本、跨四个尺寸层平衡）及其随机分层消融。

Result: 朴素微调在CST上相对第一阶段上限的FM为-0.605（约90%能力损失），其中-0.572发生于第三阶段；KD在三个随机种子下FM为-0.033±0.004（约95%保留），但缺少第二阶段无KD对照，故仅能说明KD训练下的保留而非因果效应。分层分析显示大目标检测在首个epoch内近乎归零，而梯度更新权重的阶段间余弦相似度达0.987，提示机制候选为尺度条件的梯度失衡而非权重漂移。SSH将遗忘大致减半（FM -0.605→-0.311）并保持大目标检测非零；消融显示增益主要来自尺度分层而非牧群，随机分层回放表现至少相当（FM -0.221 vs -0.311），但回放结果为单种子，属初步结论。

Conclusion: 热红外反无人机检测的顺序微调存在严重遗忘且集中于大目标尺度；知识蒸馏与尺度分层回放均能缓解遗忘，后者增益主要归因于尺度分层，但因果性证据与回放结果的稳健性仍受实验设计限制。

Abstract: Counter-UAV systems based on thermal infrared detection must stay accurate as operational datasets evolve, yet sequential fine-tuning causes catastrophic forgetting of prior tasks, a problem that remains insufficiently characterized in this domain. This continual-learning study measures the stability-plasticity trade-off in YOLOMG, a YOLOv5-based detector run as a single thermal-infrared stream with the motion channel disabled, trained sequentially across three anti-UAV benchmarks of rising scale difficulty: Anti-UAV-RGBT, Anti-UAV410, and CST Anti-UAV. Naive fine-tuning on CST yields a Forgetting Measure of -0.605 against the Stage 1 ceiling, corresponding to a 90% capability loss, with -0.572 occurring in Stage 3 alone. In contrast, knowledge distillation from a frozen teacher is associated with FM = -0.033 +/- 0.004 across three seeds, corresponding to 95% retention. Because no Stage 2 no-KD control is included, this result establishes retention under KD training rather than a causal KD effect. Per-stratum analysis shows large-target detection collapsing to near zero within the first epoch, despite an inter-stage cosine similarity of 0.987 over the gradient-updated weights, pointing to scale-conditioned gradient imbalance, rather than weight drift, as a candidate mechanism. Scale-Stratified Herding (SSH), a 300-exemplar buffer balanced across four UAV size strata, roughly halves the forgetting (FM = -0.605 to -0.311) and keeps large-target detection non-zero. An ablation attributes the gain primarily to scale stratification rather than herding: random-stratified replay performs at least as well (FM = -0.221 versus -0.311 for SSH). These replay results are single-seed and should therefore be treated as preliminary.

</details>


### [90] [Transferable Spatial Temporal Coherence Adversarial Attack on Black-Box Vision Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08331)
*Heyam Bin Jahlan Areej Alhothali Abeer Alhothali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间时序一致性对抗攻击（STCA），针对自动驾驶场景中的视频视觉语言模型，通过字幕引导选帧、空间攻击与时序一致性攻击三阶段，在黑盒迁移设置下实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）日益集成到敏感系统中，但针对视频、特别是自动驾驶场景下的时序对抗攻击研究仍十分匮乏，存在未被探索的安全漏洞。

Method: STCA 包含三阶段：1）模态扩展，提出字幕引导的帧选择方法，定位语义最重要的帧；2）空间攻击，生成保持高相似度的扰动；3）STCA 攻击，利用运动引导掩码破坏跨帧时序一致性。方法在黑盒威胁模型下运行，仅依赖白盒替代模型的迁移性。

Result: 在 BDD100K 和 nuScenes 数据集上，对 Video LLaVA-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Dolphin 三个 VLM 模型进行实验，空间攻击实现了较高 ASR 且保持高 SSIM。

Conclusion: 现有视频语言模型在自动驾驶场景下仍高度易受对抗攻击，凸显出对 VLM 模型鲁棒防御的迫切需求。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Vision Language Models (VLMs) into sensitive systems introduces critical safety vulnerabilities that remain unexplored in exist studies. While adversarial attack robustness has been extensively studied for image-based models, the susceptibility of VLMs to temporally-aware adversarial attacks against video in driving context poses a distinct and under examined threat. In this paper, we introduce novel adversarial attack against video targeting VLM models used for autonomous driving scenes named Spatial Temporal Coherence Adversarial Attack (STCA). Our attack comprise from three stages: modalities expansion, Spatial attack, and STCA attack. In modalities expansion, we propose caption-guided frame selection method in order to ensure that adversarial perturbation target the most semantically significant frames. Secondly.In spatial attack, we craft effective perturbation and preserve high similarity. Then the perturbed video generated fed into STCA stage that disrupt cross-frame temporal coherence using motion guided mask. Our method operate under black box threat model against victim target VLMs, relying solely on transferability from white-box surrogate model.We conduct our experiments on the BDD100K and nuScenes autonomous driving datasets across three VLM models: Video LLaVA-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and Dolphin. Experimental results demonstrate spatial attack achieves an ASR with high SSIM. Our finding reveal that existing video language model, remain highly susceptible to adversarial attack in autonomous driving scenarios, underscoring the urgent need for robust defense for VLM models.

</details>


### [91] [Digital Twin-Driven Real2Sim2Real: Simulator-Conditioned Generation via Paired Driving-Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08339)
*Hojun Lim,Hyeongseok Jeon,Donghyun Kim,Soonyoung Jung,Heecheol Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究用数字孪生驱动的Real2Sim2Real流程（DT-R2S2R）替代目标区域真实数据，通过数字孪生重建驾驶片段并以几何对齐的仿真渲染图条件化扩散模型，生成逼真的驾驶图像用于3D感知训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机3D感知高度依赖大规模标注数据，部署到新区域通常需重新采集标注。现有生成式增强存在两难：标签条件方法需消耗本要替代的标注，仿真条件方法标注免费但缺乏对真实环境的视觉落地。

Method: 构建数字孪生（DT-R2S）重建录制的驾驶片段，以几何对齐的仿真渲染为条件训练扩散模型，得到数字孪生落地的Sim2Real模型（DT-S2R），在数字孪生覆盖范围内的重建及新仿真场景中合成逼真驾驶图像。

Result: 在多种3D检测器上验证生成数据有效性。DETR3D在未使用目标图像训练的情况下达到目标区域真实数据oracle的93.18% mAP；与现有域外真实数据简单协同训练可超越oracle。

Conclusion: DT-R2S2R可显著降低数字孪生覆盖区域的人工实地采集与标注成本，为3D感知规模化提供实用基础。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D perception for autonomous driving relies heavily on large annotated datasets, and deploying such a system to a new target region typically requires data collection and annotation. Generative augmentation has been proposed to reduce this cost, but existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: label-conditioned methods consume the very annotations they aim to replace, while simulator-conditioned methods offer free annotations but lack visual grounding to specific real environments. This work investigates the extent to which a digital-twin-driven Real2Sim2Real pipeline (DT-R2S2R) can substitute for target-region real data. By reconstructing recorded driving clips inside a georeferenced digital twin (DT-R2S), we condition a diffusion model on geometrically aligned simulator renderings, establishing a digital twin-grounded Sim2Real model (DT-S2R). As a result, DT-S2R synthesizes photorealistic driving images given low-cost yet georeferenced simulator data across both reconstructed and novel simulator scenes within digital-twin coverage. The efficacy of generated data is verified on diverse 3D detectors. DETR3D, especially, reports 93.18% of mAP obtained by a target-region real-data oracle, without employing target images for detector training. Furthermore, simple co-training with existing out-of-target real data outperforms the oracle. Thus, DT-R2S2R can substantially reduce the cost of manual on-site data collection and annotation in digital twin-available districts, providing a practical foundation for scaling 3D perception.

</details>


### [92] [DIPrune: Task-Aware Token Pruning with Dual Importance for Efficient Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08341)
*Shuo Yang,Changbai Li,Linlin Yang,Huobin Tan,Rongyu Chen,Tongfei Chen,Tian Wang,Sheng Xu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPrune，一种面向多模态大模型的无训练剪枝框架，通过任务导向的损失失真最小化与双层重要性评分，缓解语义退化并取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型无训练剪枝方法依赖视觉冗余或文本-视觉注意力，因任务无关设计或注意力估计不可靠而出现语义退化。作者经实证分析发现，浅层显著token会通过数值惯性持续压制新出现的语义token，导致深层推理所需信号被过早丢弃。

Method: 从任务导向角度，将无训练剪枝重新表述为最终任务损失的失真最小化，推导出可处理的token级上界作为代理目标；该表述揭示了以往被忽略的跨层梯度层间项。据此提出DIPrune：基于排序的框架，采用双重重要性评分机制，联合优化层内静态特征显著性与层间动态语义演化。

Result: 在LLaVA与Qwen-VL上的大量实验表明，DIPrune持续取得最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过将剪枝与任务损失关联并建模层间语义演化，DIPrune有效缓解了语义退化问题，验证了任务导向与双层重要性联合建模在无训练多模态剪枝中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent training-free pruning approaches for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) effectively cut computational overhead by exploiting visual redundancy or text-vision attention. However, they frequently suffer from semantic degradation due to their task-agnostic design or unreliable attention estimates. Based on our empirical analysis, we have found that this issue arises because salient tokens in shallow layers persistently suppress emerging semantic ones through numerical inertia, leading to premature discarding of signals crucial for deep reasoning. To address the aforementioned issue, from the task-oriented aspects, we first reformulate training-free pruning as a minimization of the distortion in the final task loss and derive a tractable, token-wise upper bound to serve as a surrogate objective. Specifically, this formulation inherently reveals a previously neglected inter-layer term that accounts for gradients across layers. Accordingly, for the implementation, we propose DIPrune, a rank-based framework that employs a dual importance scoring mechanism to jointly optimize intra-layer static feature saliency and inter-layer dynamic semantic evolution. Extensive experiments on LLaVA and Qwen-VL demonstrate that DIPrune consistently achieves state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [93] [PolarScale: A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Radiometrically Consistent RGB-to-Stokes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08346)
*Beibei Lin,Tingting Chen,Xin Zhang,Wenhao Zhao,Dongjun Li,Zifeng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PolarScale 基准，将全斯托克斯偏振重建中缺失的辐射尺度作为显式预测与评估目标，并系统评估多种模型在该任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从类 RGB 输入推断偏振的方法只能预测归一化斯托克斯分量或相对描述符，缺失了完整斯托克斯重建所需的辐射尺度，且该尺度在现有方法中被剔除，无法显式评估。

Method: 基于已有的三色全斯托克斯测量构建 PolarScale 基准，输入每场景归一化总强度图像 s0（场景参照线性图像），要求模型预测归一化斯托克斯分量、AoLP/DoLP/DoCP 及每场景尺度；由于尺度从输入中剔除而不可物理识别，因此采用数据集条件语义尺度估计并对照常数尺度控制，结合角度、自一致性和物理边界指标进行评估。

Result: 在七种基于复原和生成的主干及三种预测策略中，最强复原模型尺度估计平均相对误差为 3.6-4.3%，优于常数控制的 5.7%，物理边界违反像素少于 0.25%，而两个生成基线尺度坍缩至近零；显式描述符监督提升描述符精度（MAE 下 PSNR 23.66 vs. 18.88 dB）。预测的全斯托克斯表示改善了漫反射/镜面分离、材质分割和眩光分类，但在漫反射/镜面分离中学习尺度仅与常数控制持平。

Conclusion: PolarScale 将尺度估计确立为可评估的显式目标，表明复原模型在语义尺度估计上可行并优于常数控制，但下游漫反射/镜面分离等任务中学习尺度优势有限，揭示尺度估计的实用价值仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Polarization imaging provides physical cues beyond intensity imaging but typically requires specialized hardware. Recent methods infer polarization from RGB-like inputs, yet predict only normalized Stokes components or relative descriptors, from which the radiometric scale needed for full Stokes reconstruction has been divided out. We introduce PolarScale, a benchmark that makes this scale an explicit prediction and evaluation target. Built on existing trichromatic full-Stokes measurements, PolarScale takes the per-scene normalized total-intensity image $s_0$ (a scene-referred linear image, not a consumer sRGB photograph) and asks models to predict normalized Stokes components, AoLP/DoLP/DoCP, and a per-scene scale. Because the scale is divided out of the input, it is not physically identifiable; PolarScale therefore evaluates dataset-conditioned semantic scale estimation against a constant-scale control, together with angular, self-consistency, and physical-bound metrics. Across seven restoration-based and generative backbones and three prediction strategies, the strongest restoration models estimate the scale with 3.6-4.3% mean relative error versus 5.7% for the constant control and violate physical bounds on fewer than 0.25% of pixels, whereas two generative baselines collapse to a near-zero scale; explicit descriptor supervision improves descriptor accuracy (23.66 vs. 18.88 dB PSNR for MAE). Predicted full-Stokes representations improve diffuse/specular separation, material segmentation, and glare classification, although in diffuse/specular separation the learned scale performs only on par with the constant control.

</details>


### [94] [Test-Time Adaptation of Quantized ViTs via Single-Pass Quantizer-Aligned Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08358)
*Hyeongheon Cha,Young D. Kwon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuAR，一种面向量化ViT的单次前向、无需反向传播和参数更新的测试时适应方法，通过将激活重新对齐到冻结量化器的源校准分布来恢复分布偏移下的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化使ViT在分布偏移下特别脆弱。现有TTA方法多依赖反向传播或额外前向/参数更新，开销大；轻量的特征或logit方法只恢复部分损失。同时未针对一个量化特有的失效模式：偏移下激活落在冻结量化器校准范围的方式改变，导致码分布失真。

Method: QuAR在冻结量化器输入端对激活进行重校准，使用测试流的滑动逐通道统计量将其映射回源校准分布。方法为单次前向，不反向传播、不更新任何模型参数，仅引入极小的逐通道统计与映射开销。

Result: 在ImageNet-C与ViT-B上，QuAR在3、4、6、8比特权重/激活精度下取得无反向TTA方法中最高的平均精度，8比特超最强基线2.28点、3比特超4.00点，延迟低46%，内存开销仅0.17 MB（峰值推理内存的0.01%）。在连续流、非i.i.d.标签偏移、七个OOD测试集及三种其他骨干网络上，单一固定配置仍保持领先。

Conclusion: 诊断分析表明增益来自量化器处逐通道失配的降低，从而恢复被基线保持或进一步扭曲的码分布。QuAR以极低开销显著提升量化ViT在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，且配置通用性强。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is a standard route to fitting vision transformers (ViTs) into edge compute and memory budgets, yet quantized models become especially brittle under distribution shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) addresses such shifts without labels, but most existing approaches are poorly aligned with the constraints of quantized inference. Prevailing TTA methods recover accuracy through backpropagation, while backprop-free methods often still incur overhead from extra forward passes or parameter updates, and lightweight feature- or logit-level methods recover only part of the loss. Across these approaches, a quantization-specific failure mode that amplifies the drop is not directly targeted: under shift, activations occupy frozen quantizers' calibrated ranges differently, distorting their code distribution. We propose Quantizer-Aligned Recalibration (QuAR), a single-pass TTA method tailored to quantized ViTs that neither backpropagates nor updates any model parameters. QuAR recalibrates activations at the input to a frozen quantizer, mapping the test stream's running per-channel statistics back toward the source calibration. On ImageNet-C with ViT-B, QuAR achieves the highest mean accuracy among state-of-the-art backprop-free TTA methods at 3-, 4-, 6- and 8-bit weight/activation precision, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.28 points at 8 bits and 4.00 at 3 bits, with 46% lower latency and a memory overhead of only 0.17 MB (0.01% of peak inference memory). Analysis and diagnostics trace the gain to a reduced per-channel mismatch at these quantizers, which restores the code distribution the baselines leave unchanged or distort further. A single fixed configuration remains ahead across continual streams, non-i.i.d. label shift, seven out-of-distribution suites, and three other backbones.

</details>


### [95] [A Stevens's Power Law Check-up of GPT-5.5's Image-Based Visualization Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08365)
*Kaichun Yang,Jian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将Stevens幂律引入AI模型对可视化图形的感知能力测量，通过无图例的相对量级估计任务，评估12种视觉变量，使算法模型的视觉编码读取能力可测量、可与人感知对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型日益参与可视化生成与解读，但其内置的感知机制不透明，缺乏与人感知可比的量化评估手段，因此需要一种能揭示算法模型固有感知机制的测量方法。

Method: 借鉴心理物理学中的Stevens幂律，设计无图例的估计任务：模型先观看参考图形并估计其量级，再对同一视觉表示的后续图像相对参考进行量级估计；覆盖12种视觉变量。

Result: 在12种视觉变量上实现了对算法模型读取视觉编码方式的可测量评估，并使其结果可与人类感知进行对比。

Conclusion: 该方法使AI模型的视觉感知更可测量、可比与可解释，为理解算法模型的感知机制提供了心理物理学层面的评估框架。

Abstract: We adapt Stevens's power law to measure the innate ability of AI models to read visualizations, which can reveal the built-in perceptual mechanisms of algorithmic models. In our pilot study, models see no legend. A model first views a reference visual representation and estimates its magnitude, then estimates the magnitude of each subsequent image of the same representation relative to that reference. Our evaluation of twelve visual variables makes how algorithmic models read visual encodings measurable, comparable with human perception, and more interpretable to humans.

</details>


### [96] [UniCounting: Instance-Aware Proposal Consolidation for Image-Query-Free Multi-Category Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08379)
*Jinshi Liu,Pan Liu,Lei He,Weichao Luo,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniCounting，将固定词表、免图像查询的多类别计数建模为过完备候选集上的实例感知结构推断，仅训练3276参数的关系头，在COCO clean500上取得优于校准OWLv2-All80的向量计数效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉计数多针对单一指定目标（依赖图像样例、文本查询或目标类别），仅返回单一计数，无法在固定词表下、无任何提示地预测图像中出现的各类别完整计数向量。本文希望解决这类更困难的多类别计数设定。

Method: 冻结SAM 2.1生成掩码，冻结DINOv2与OpenCLIP提供关系与类别特征；由3276参数、类别共享的关系头基于实例掩码监督预测同一实例亲和度；再通过稀疏图构建、代表选择、标注与背景边距准入，将每个被接纳的连通分量转换为一个计数并给出可复现的分组证据。仅训练关系头，不使用计数或密度图监督。

Result: 在COCO clean500上，点估计向量ℓ1误差与缺失类虚假质量均低于校准的OWLv2-All80，微平均存在F1相当；在匹配解码器下，所学关系较掩码包含、掩码IoU、CLIP与DINO降低两类误差，并揭示碎片化与合并的权衡；另报告在OmniCount-sub、FSC-147、CARPK上的迁移诊断。

Conclusion: 实例感知的关系推断可作为多类别计数的有效核心，仅需训练极轻量关系头即可在无计数监督下获得稳健向量计数，并具备一定跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Visual counting is commonly formulated as counting a single specified target, with a model receiving an image-specific exemplar, text query, or target category and returning a single count. We instead study fixed-vocabulary image-query-free multi-category counting. A global vocabulary is fixed for each run, and, given only an RGB image, the model predicts a complete category--count vector without being told which categories appear. We present UniCounting, which casts counting as instance-aware structural inference over an over-complete proposal set. Generic segmenters produce duplicate masks, partial views, and proposals from neighboring instances; semantic scores can name them but cannot determine which denote the same object. Frozen SAM~2.1 generates masks, while frozen DINOv2 and OpenCLIP provide relation and category features. A 3,267-parameter category-shared relation head predicts same-instance affinities from instance-mask-derived supervision. Sparse graph construction, representative selection, labeling, and background-margin admission then convert each admitted component into one count with replayable group evidence. Only the relation head is trained, without count or density-map targets. On COCO clean500, UniCounting obtains lower point-estimate vector $\ell_1$ error and absent-class false mass than calibrated OWLv2-All80, with comparable micro presence F1. Under a matched decoder, the learned relation reduces both errors relative to mask containment, mask IoU, CLIP, and DINO, while revealing a fragmentation--merge trade-off. We also report transfer diagnostics on OmniCount-sub, FSC-147, and CARPK.

</details>


### [97] [UP-MOPD: Update Projection in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08398)
*Taojie Zhu,Jing Jin,Yuan Xia,Chenyang Ding,Qunshan He,Wanke Xia,Tao Sun,Yan Chen,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UP-MOPD方法，通过投影优化器候选更新（而非修正梯度）来解决多教师在线策略蒸馏中因AdamW动量、自适应缩放和权重衰减导致的域间冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏希望让学生模型融合多个领域的专长，但不同领域的梯度会产生冲突，阻碍知识整合。已有的梯度修正方法只在普通SGD下有效；在AdamW等优化器中，动量、自适应缩放与权重衰减会把"被修正的梯度"转化成一阶意义上反而增大某个域损失的参数更新，形成理论与实践的缺口。

Method: 提出Update Projection for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation（UP-MOPD）。先让原始混合梯度去更新优化器状态并生成一个候选位移（candidate displacement）；随后只对"违反约束"的候选位移做投影，再提交到参数上。该投影给出在欧氏距离下离候选点最近的、且满足可行性的唯一更新。

Result: 在医学与通用领域结合的实验中，UP-MOPD在训练后期将IFEval-loose准确率相比原始M-OPD提升2.96个百分点；八项指标平均得分60.03，高于梯度投影（59.00）和更新拒绝（59.15）。在覆盖数学、代码与指令遵循的公开基准上，六项任务平均分最佳（32.67），在LiveCodeBench v5上领先，并在IFEval上与最优结果持平。

Conclusion: 结果支持"对优化器更新进行投影"这一思路，能够有效降低多领域之间的相互干扰，验证了在AdamW等现代优化器下应约束更新而非梯度的观点。

Abstract: On-policy distillation from multiple teachers combines expertise from different domains in a single student, but conflicting gradients can hinder this integration. Gradient corrections directly constrain parameter updates under plain SGD. With optimizers such as AdamW, however, momentum, adaptive scaling, and weight decay can turn a corrected gradient into an update that increases a domain loss to first order. To address this gap, we propose Update Projection for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (UP-MOPD). UP-MOPD lets the original mixed gradient update the optimizer state and generate a candidate displacement, then projects only violating candidates before they are committed to the parameters. The projection gives the unique feasible update closest to the candidate in Euclidean distance. In experiments combining medical and general domains, UP-MOPD improves IFEval-loose accuracy late in training by 2.96 points over vanilla M-OPD. It achieves an average score of 60.03 across eight metrics, compared with 59.00 for gradient projection and 59.15 for update rejection. On a public benchmark covering mathematics, code, and instruction following, it achieves the best average across six tasks (32.67), leads on LiveCodeBench v5, and ties for the best IFEval result.These results support projecting optimizer updates to reduce interference between domains.

</details>


### [98] [GeoPID: Decomposing and Steering Visual Information in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08401)
*Seulgi Kim,Zhixiong Zhang,Xinwei Zhang,Jie Ling,Ronn Shaw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架GeoPID，从几何视角分析VLM多模态信息，分解为冗余、模态独有与协同成分，据此在推理时增强视觉表征，提升视觉定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽表现优异，但倾向于少用视觉信息、过度依赖文本上下文，导致视觉定位能力不足。

Method: GeoPID为免训练框架，通过视觉与文本表征子空间之间的几何关系，将信息分解为冗余、模态独有和协同三部分；基于该几何分析，提出针对性干预技术，在推理时沿视觉独有子空间选择性放大视觉表征，无需更新任何模型参数。

Result: 在22个VLM和14个基准上开展分析，发现当问题强依赖视觉定位时，正确预测具有更强的视觉独有成分；干预后视觉定位能力增强，平均相对准确率提升7.63%。

Conclusion: 从几何视角分析多模态信息可有效揭示并缓解VLM对文本的过度依赖，通过免训练的推理期视觉表征放大即可显著提升视觉定位性能。

Abstract: While recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown outstanding performance across diverse applications, they tend to under-use visual information and over-rely on textual context. In this work, we propose \textsc{GeoPID}, a training-free framework that analyzes multimodal information within VLMs from a geometric perspective. \textsc{GeoPID} decomposes information into Redundant, Modality-Unique, and Synergistic components through the geometric relationships between visual and textual representation subspaces. Through an extensive analysis across 22 VLMs and 14 benchmarks, we confirm that correct predictions exhibit stronger vision-unique components when questions strongly require visual grounding. Building on this geometric analysis, we introduce a targeted intervention technique that selectively amplifies visual representations along the vision-unique subspace during inference. As a result, visual grounding capabilities were enhanced without any additional model parameter updates, achieving an average relative accuracy gain of 7.63\%.

</details>


### [99] [Decoy and disclosure radii of invariant shape descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08410)
*Tanush Shaska,Lubjana Beshaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析旋转不变描述子的纤维维度，揭示标准不变量池存在诱饵形状、泄漏原始形状，并给出精确认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转不变描述子只保留表面在描述子纤维上的信息，纤维半径过大易受诱饵欺骗，过小则泄漏原始形状，需要量化这一权衡。

Method: 对截断至球谐次数 L、含 n 个系数的星形曲面，用轨道距离度量纤维半径，计算泛型秩 r 的描述子纤维维度 n-3-r（模旋转），并分析带功率、偶双谱与三阶带三个不变量构成的池。

Result: 该池在 L=4,6,8 时分别容许维度 5、13、20 的诱饵族；秩首次在 L=16 达到 n-3，且每个 L 都存在镜像诱饵。奇双谱在 L≥4 时泛型去除镜像诱饵。L=6 时诱饵以相对精度 2×10^-18 匹配全部 32 个不变量，轨道距离至少为0.87倍范数。对 Bennu 形状模型，该池恢复体积、遗漏手性，keep-out 半径不确定度超 7 m。

Conclusion: 标准不变量池无法同时保证抗诱饵与隐私；固定平均半径时它精确确定体积，却无法判定间隙要求，且存在手性与半径不确定性。

Abstract: A recognizer that compares rotation-invariant descriptors sees a surface only up to the fiber of the descriptor. We measure this fiber by its radius in the orbit distance from the enrolled surface. A large radius admits decoys, that is, distant shapes that pass the matcher. A small radius discloses the enrolled shape to anyone who captures the stored value. For star-shaped surfaces truncated to spherical harmonics of degree at most $L$, with $n$ coefficients, a descriptor of generic rank $r$ has generic fibers of dimension $n-3-r$ modulo rotations. The standard pool of band powers, even bispectra, and three invariants of the degree-three band therefore admits decoy families of dimension $5$, $13$, $20$ at $L=4,6,8$. Its rank first reaches $n-3$ at $L=16$, and a mirror decoy remains at every $L$. The odd bispectra remove the mirror decoy generically for $L \geq 4$. Yet at fixed mean radius the same pool determines the enclosed volume exactly, and it does not determine whether a surface meets a clearance requirement. We certify two cases by exact and interval arithmetic. At $L=6$ a decoy matches all $32$ invariants to relative precision $2 \cdot 10^{-18}$ at orbit distance at least $0.87$ times the norm of the enrolled tuple. For the radar shape model of asteroid (101955) Bennu, the pool recovers the modeled volume, misses the handedness, and leaves the keep-out radius uncertain by more than $7 \, \mathrm{m}$.

</details>


### [100] [Image Bitstream Fine-grained Understanding for Privacy-Friendly AIoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08414)
*Zhen Yu,Wenyang Liu,Kejun Wu,Chengwang Xiao,Renjie Qiao,Chengtao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像比特流细粒度理解（IBFU）任务及基础模型BFG，可直接从编码比特流进行细粒度分类与语义描述生成，无需完整解码到像素域，并构建了大规模损坏比特流数据集CFU-D，实验证明其在比特流损坏下仍保持稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统像素域视觉理解需要完整解码图像，导致像素级视觉内容在处理流程中暴露，不利于隐私保护。AIoT场景中比特流在传输和存储时可能损坏，而现有视觉语言模型在此类损坏下性能严重下降。因此需要一种无需像素重建、可直接理解比特流的隐私友好范式。

Method: 提出基础模型BFG，包含两个组件：比特流语义编码器（BSeE）直接从编码比特流建模语义表示，不进行显式像素重建；细粒度语义生成器（FSeG）通过自回归生成将比特流语义转换为详细自然语言描述。同时构建大规模损坏比特流细粒度理解数据集CFU-D，涵盖完整比特流及多种损坏类型和严重程度的变体。

Result: BFG在比特流损坏下仍能保持稳定的细粒度描述生成。例如在Stanford Dogs Caption数据集上，平均CIDEr分数仅从0.6339轻微变化到0.6077，而Qwen-VL-Chat、BLIP-2、GLM、Gemini、GPT等视觉语言模型性能严重下降。

Conclusion: 本文为AIoT中隐私友好的细粒度理解提供了一种实用范式，验证了直接从比特流进行语义分析在抗损坏性和隐私保护方面的优势。

Abstract: Image Bitstream Fine-grained Understanding (IBFU) aims to directly perform fine-grained classification and semantic description generation from encoded image byte sequences. In contrast to conventional pixel-domain visual understanding, IBFU conducts semantic analysis without fully decoding images into the pixel domain. Since pixel-level visual content is not explicitly reconstructed during inference, this paradigm reduces visual exposure within the processing pipeline and suits privacy-friendly Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) applications. In this paper, we propose Bitstream Fine-grained Generator (BFG), a novel foundation model tailored for IBFU. BFG consists of two main components: a Bitstream Semantic Encoder (BSeE) and a Fine-grained Semantic Generator (FSeG). BSeE directly models semantic representations from encoded image bitstreams without explicit pixel reconstruction, while FSeG transforms the extracted bitstream semantics into detailed natural-language descriptions through autoregressive generation. To train BFG and comprehensively evaluate IBFU in practical AIoT scenarios, where image bitstreams may suffer corruption during transmission and storage, we construct a large-scale Corrupted-bitstream Fine-grained Understanding dataset (CFU-D), containing both intact bitstreams and corrupted variants across multiple corruption types and severity levels. Experiments show that BFG maintains stable fine-grained caption generation under bitstream corruption. For example, the performance only has slight change from 0.6339 to 0.6077 in terms of average CIDEr score on Stanford Dogs Caption dataset, while vision-language models, such as Qwen-VL-Chat, BLIP-2, GLM, Gemini, and GPT suffer severe performance decrease. This paper provides a practical paradigm for privacy-friendly fine-grained understanding in AIoT.

</details>


### [101] [Ariadne's Thread of LipSync: Unraveling Forgeries via Inconsistency between Lip Motions and Head Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08417)
*Tianyi She,Jiawei Liu,Weifeng Liu,Hanqing Zhao,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LipDA框架，利用自然说话视频中唇动与头部姿态之间的生物耦合关系来实现LipSync伪造的联合检测与溯源，在两个数据集及自建大规模多生成器数据集上取得显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LipSync生成技术日益逼真，带来严重社会风险。现有防御方法难以应对，因为先进生成方法不仅唇同步更好，还消除了视觉伪影。其关键原因在于现有方法忽略了自然说话视频中唇动与头部姿态之间固有的生物耦合关系。

Method: 提出LipDA框架，利用头与唇的不一致性进行联合检测与归因。检测方面，通过对比真实与伪造视频的唇部特征和姿态特征来学习量化这一差异；归因方面，捕捉模型独有的时间动态和音视频同步模式作为指纹，实现来源追踪。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的LipSync数据集以及自建的大规模多生成器数据集上进行大量实验，检测AUC超过97%，模型归因准确率达97.5%，显著优于现有方法。代码与LipSync-A数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 通过利用唇动与头部姿态之间的生物耦合不一致性，LipDA有效提升了LipSync伪造检测与溯源的性能，证明了该生物学线索在防御深度伪造中的重要价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in LipSync generation technology have led to the creation of highly realistic videos, posing severe societal risks. However, existing defense strategies struggle against LipSync forgeries, as advanced LipSync generation methods not only achieve better lip synchronization but also eliminate visual artifacts. An important reason is that they overlook an inherent biological coupling between lip movements and head poses in natural speech videos. In this paper, we propose LipDA, a novel framework for joint LipSync Detection and Attribution, which takes advantage of the inconsistency between head and lip. For detection, the framework learns to quantify this discrepancy by contrasting lip and pose features from authentic versus forged videos. For attribution, our method is designed to capture the unique temporal dynamics and audio-visual synchronization patterns that act as the fingerprint of models, enabling source tracing. We conduct extensive experiments on two challenging LipSync datasets as well as our own proposed large-scale and multi-generator dataset. LipDA achieves over 97\% AUC in detection and 97.5\% accuracy in model attribution, significantly outperforming existing methods. Code and the proposed LipSync-A dataset are available at https://github.com/AnsonShe/LipDA.

</details>


### [102] [From the Drosophila Visual Connectome to General-Purpose Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08418)
*Zongyu Li,Akito Yamauchi,Huaizhi Liu,Vishwanatha Rao,Jia Guo,for the Frontotemporal Lobar Degeneration Neuroimaging Initiative,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConnectomeX框架，以受果蝇连接组启发的FlyVision架构为核心，在保持ON/OFF并行处理、循环计算和图交互的同时扩展模型容量，在MNIST、CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K、皮肤病、胸片和BrainAGE等任务上以更少参数量取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物连接组编码了视觉计算的结构化解法，可能为人工视觉提供可复用的归纳偏置，因此需要构建并验证一种连接组启发的可训练架构。
