*Ruoxuan Feng,Yutong Chen,Ruihua Song,Huan Yang,Zhongyuan Wang,Guocai Yao,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Humans inherently understand the physical world through an active process. When sensory evidence is insufficient to infer physical properties, we naturally interact with the environment by deciding what information is missing, how to acquire it, and when sufficient evidence has been obtained. In stark contrast, existing multi-sensory robot systems mainly integrate sensory inputs rather than actively acquiring missing evidence through interactions. In this work, we introduce ROMA, an LLM-based system for Real-World Object-Centric Multi-Sensory Active Perception. ROMA integrates vision, audio, tactile, and force sensing into a reasoning-interaction-feedback loop. The model identifies missing evidence and determines the target objects, interactions, and modalities, while a physical interface executes the selected interactions and collects the multi-sensory feedback. To support this capability, we construct ROMI-2K, a large-scale real-world multi-sensory object interaction dataset covering nearly 2,000 objects and 6 atomic interactions with synchronized sensory feedback. Building on these data, we develop a two-stage training framework that aligns sensory modalities and equips the LLM to assess evidence sufficiency, select informative interactions, and reason over the multi-sensory feedback. We further characterize active perception as perception chains, where acquired evidence guides subsequent interactions and reasoning, and establish ROMA Bench to evaluate single-attribute, long-horizon multi-attribute, and intent-driven active perception. Experiments show that ROMA can actively acquire missing evidence and solve complex, long-chain multi-sensory perception tasks that existing methods struggle to handle, laying a strong perceptual foundation for active multi-sensory embodied agents.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Modular Policy for Multi-Floor Object Navigation:A Factorized Framework for Diagnostic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06958)
*Shichao Zhai,Shuhao Ye,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多楼层场景下的物体目标导航（ObjectNav）问题进行诊断性研究，提出分层因子化策略框架，将全局策略拆分为楼层内探索与楼层间切换两个子策略，并通过VLM蒸馏获得有效初始化，实验表明感知性能与爬楼梯稳定性是多楼层导航的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多楼层ObjectNav因长时域决策导致奖励稀疏，训练与诊断困难，现有研究缺乏对失败因素的系统分析。

Method: 基于模块化框架进行诊断研究，设计分层因子化策略：将单一全局策略解构为楼层内探索策略与楼层间切换策略；利用VLM的探索逻辑蒸馏轻型楼层内策略以提供RL有效初始化；并在理想假设下证明因子化策略在策略表示层面与全局策略理论等价。

Result: 实验结果显示，感知性能与爬楼梯稳定性是多楼层导航的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 分层因子化框架能有效支撑多楼层ObjectNav的失败因素诊断，指出未来改进应聚焦于感知能力与爬楼梯稳定性。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) in multi-floor scenarios presents a challenge due to sparse rewards caused by long-horizon decision-making. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic study based on a modular framework with an effective learnable policy to analyze failure factors in multi-floor scenarios. To achieve an effective policy for diagnosis, we design the hierarchical factorization policy that deconstructs a single global policy into an intra-floor exploration policy and an inter-floor switching policy. To providing an effective initialization for Reinforcement Learning (RL), the lightweight intra-floor policy is learned by distilling the exploration logic of Visual Language Models (VLMs). Under idealized assumptions, we show that the factorized policy is theoretically equivalent to a single global policy at the policy-representation level. Experiment results indicate that perception performance and stair climbing stability are the primary bottlenecks in multi-floor navigation.

</details>


### [139] [ACG-WAM: World-Action Modeling via Action-Conditioned Geometric Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06965)
*Jiangtao Liu,Zishang Xiang,Yage He,Lingguo Cui,Baihai Zhang,Runqi Chai,Senchun Chai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AC G‑WAM及其辅助目标AC G‑JEPA，通过预测多步几何特征提升世界动作模型的策略学习，在仿真与真机上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型虽能联合学习视觉预测与机器人动作，但其视频与动作损失并未显式约束动作序列的几何后果；且经时间注意力后的视觉特征可能包含未来观测信息，不适合作为辅助预测器的唯一当前视觉输入。

Method: 提出AC G‑WAM及辅助目标AC G‑JEPA：以冻结VGGT编码的当前与未来图像对的未来slot为目标，从当前观测和中间动作预测多个时间视野的几何特征；在时间混合前将头部与腕部相机监督施加于共享视觉嵌入，并在推理时移除教师与辅助模块。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务上，干净场景成功率93.46%，随机化成功率92.68%，两设置均值93.07%，均为对比方法最佳；在真机三个任务上成功率85.00%、部分完成得分91.67%，分别超过Motus 10.00与9.17个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过显式几何监督增强世界动作模型的表征学习，且不增加推理开销，能在仿真与真实机器人任务中显著提升性能。

Abstract: World action models jointly learn visual predictionand robot actions, providing a way to use observations ofscene evolution for policy learning. Their video and actionlosses, however, provide no explicit target for the geometricconsequences of a demonstrated action sequence. Moreover,visual features taken after temporal attention can contain futureobservations, making them unsuitable as the sole current visualinput to an auxiliary predictor. We introduce ACG-WAMand its auxiliary objective, the Action-Conditioned GeometricJoint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ACG-JEPA), whichpredicts geometric features at several horizons from the currentobservation and intervening actions, using the future slot of afrozen VGGT encoding of each current and future image pairas the target. We apply this supervision from the head and wristcameras to a shared visual embedding before temporal mixing,and remove the teacher and auxiliary modules at inference.On 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, ACG-WAM achieves 93.46%success in clean scenes, with the best randomized success(92.68%) and mean across both settings (93.07%) among thecompared methods; across three tasks on a real robot, itachieves 85.00% success and 91.67% partial completion score,exceeding Motus by 10.00 and 9.17 percentage points, respec-tively. Code:https://github.com/RoboOpus/ACG-WAM.Website:https://RoboOpus.github.io/ACG-WAM.

</details>


### [140] [ProactiveVLA: Augmenting Embodied Memory through Proactive Environment Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06999)
*Shizuo Tian,Haodong Luo,Yutong Li,Yuebing Song,Yunxin Liu,Yuanchun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProactiveVLA，通过主动环境探索获取可复用知识，提升机器人部署时的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需从有限经验中快速获取关于局部物体、状态和交互的知识。现有将推理智能体与冻结 VLA 结合的系统依赖执行反馈与记忆来适应，因此"选择何种经验"至关重要；而反复练习目标任务可能只优化熟悉解法，忽视了条件变化下相关的其他交互。

Method: ProactiveVLA 采用主动环境探索：完成初始任务后，将剩余交互预算分配给自己提出的目标，涵盖物体可供性、状态变化交互及交互组合；同时验证执行结果，并将任务导向与探索性经验整合进记忆，用以指导后续规划与控制。

Result: 在相同交互轮次预算下，ProactiveVLA 在 LIBERO-Pro 和 RoboCasa365 Composite-Seen 上超越基线。在 LIBERO-Pro Goal-T 上，评估时最多允许一次 VLA 原语调用，ProactiveVLA 完成 48% 的实例，而最先进的 task-refinement 基线仅完成 19%。

Conclusion: 主动探索式经验获取比单纯重复目标任务更有效，能为部署时适应提供可复用知识，显著提升成功率和交互效率。

Abstract: Rapid adaptation to a new environment requires a robot to acquire useful knowledge about local objects, states, and interactions from limited experience. Systems that combine a reasoning agent with a frozen vision-language-action model (VLA) can adapt through execution feedback and memory, making the choice of experience central to their effectiveness. Repeated practice of a target task may refine a familiar solution while leaving other interactions relevant to changed conditions untested. We introduce ProactiveVLA, which uses proactive environment exploration to acquire reusable knowledge for deployment-time adaptation. After completing an initial task, the agent allocates the remaining interaction budget to self-proposed goals covering object affordances, state-changing interactions, and compositions of interactions. It verifies execution outcomes and consolidates both task-directed and exploratory experience into memory that guides subsequent planning and control. ProactiveVLA outperforms the baselines under the same turn budget on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365 Composite-Seen. On LIBERO-Pro Goal-T, with at most one VLA primitive invocation allowed during evaluation, ProactiveVLA completes 48% of instances, compared with 19% for the state-of-the-art task-refinement baseline.

</details>


### [141] [LEAP: Making Privileged Geometry Supervision Effective for Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07015)
*Han Fang,Yunpeng Jiang,Jianshu Hu,Zhiyuan Guan,Ruiguo Sun,Shujia Li,Paul Weng,Xiao Li,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEAP框架，通过潜在编码对齐特权几何信息，改进基于RGB的视觉运动策略学习，在多个基准和真实任务上显著优于Diffusion Policy和ACT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权3D监督在训练时利用额外几何信息指导RGB视觉运动策略学习，部署时无需几何输入。但低重建误差并不能保证视觉表征捕捉到对控制有用的几何信息。作者识别出三个削弱该监督的局限：本体感受捷径、主视角依赖、以及重建目标被任务无关几何主导。

Method: 提出LEAP（Latent Encoding with Aligned Privileged Geometry）。使用辅助解码器仅从视觉特征重建操作工作空间内的点云，同时保留本体感受用于动作预测。除完整重建外，引入手腕视角dropout和与保留手腕匹配的部分重建目标，促使编码器捕捉互补的局部几何。推理时移除辅助解码器，仅保留RGB观测和机器人状态作为策略输入。

Result: 在RoboTwin、ManiSkill和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，相比Diffusion Policy和ACT取得一致且显著的提升，参数量仅小幅增加，推理速度无下降。

Conclusion: LEAP通过将特权几何信息与视觉潜在表征对齐，有效增强了RGB视觉运动策略学习，是一种高效且即插即用的方法。

Abstract: Privileged 3D supervision uses additional geometric information during training to guide RGB-based visuomotor policy learning, without requiring geometric inputs at deployment. However, low reconstruction error does not ensure that visual representations capture geometry useful for control. We identify three limitations that weaken this supervision: proprioceptive shortcuts, dominant-view reliance, and reconstruction objectives dominated by task-irrelevant geometry. To address these limitations, we propose Latent Encoding with Aligned Privileged Geometry (LEAP). Our framework uses an auxiliary decoder to reconstruct point clouds within the manipulation workspace from visual features alone, while retaining proprioception for action prediction. Alongside full reconstruction, we introduce wrist-view dropout and partial reconstruction targets matched to the retained wrist, encouraging the encoder to capture complementary local geometry. The auxiliary decoder is removed at inference, leaving only RGB observations and robot state as policy inputs. Extensive experiments on RoboTwin, ManiSkill, and real-world tasks demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements over Diffusion Policy and ACT, with only a small increase in parameter count and no reduction in inference speed.

</details>


### [142] [BRACE: Adapting Whole-Body References for Force and Terrain Aware Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07052)
*Sudarshan Harithas,Chen Yu,Juan Borbon,Shubhankar Mondal,Winston Zha,Srinath Sridhar,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BRACE 是一种全身跟踪器：将地形自适应与手部力（施加/补偿）折叠进参考轨迹中，使机器人能在斜坡上跟随平地参考并完成力交互，通过 DAgger 将两类教师蒸馏为单一 flow-matching 学生策略，仅用本体感受即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身跟踪器的参考动作录制于平地且无负载，因此跟踪器既不了解机器人需与物体交换的力，也不了解所需站立的地形。现有控制器只解决单侧问题：可施力策略能命令末端力但不规定全身姿态；地形自适应跟踪器则把负载当作扰动来抑制，而非当作可命令的力旋量。

Method: BRACE 不把负载与坡度留给跟踪器被动吸收，而是将两者折叠进所跟随的参考中：(1) 地形顺应将落脚点与根节点抬升到局部表面；(2) 力旋量变换求解产生力所需的手部位移，以及随之诱导的质心与压力中心偏移，并受教师特定的手臂努力上限约束。随后用 DAgger 将"施加力"与"补偿力"两类教师蒸馏为一个 flow-matching 学生策略，仅依赖本体感受运行，无需高度图或实测力旋量。

Result: 在 Unitree-G1 上进行了大量实验，仿真与真机实验均表明 BRACE 能够施加力、补偿力并应对多种地形。

Conclusion: BRACE 让远程操作者只需提供平地轨迹，机器人即可在机上自行解析坡度与负载，从而在斜坡地形上既能施加也能补偿命令的手部力，同时保持对平地参考的跟随。

Abstract: Whole-body tracking has become the interface through which operators drive humanoid robots, yet the references it consumes are recorded on level ground and carrying nothing, so the tracker is aware of neither the forces the robot must exchange with objects nor the terrain it must stand on. Existing controllers address one side of this gap: force-capable policies command an end-effector force but prescribe no whole-body pose, while terrain-adaptive trackers treat loads as disturbances to reject rather than wrenches to command. We present BRACE, a whole-body tracker that exerts and compensates commanded hand forces from diverse poses while following a flat-ground reference on sloped terrain. Rather than leaving the tracker to absorb the load and the slope, BRACE folds both into the reference it follows: terrain conformance lifts footholds and root onto the local surface, and a wrench transformation resolves the hand displacement that produces a force jointly with the center-of-mass and center- of-pressure shifts it induces, bounded by teacher-specific arm-effort limits. Separate exertion and compensation teachers are distilled by DAgger into one flow-matching student that runs on proprioception alone, without a height map or measured wrench, so a teleoperator (even in remote locations) can supply a flat- ground trajectory and the robot resolves slope and load onboard. We extensive experiments on the Unitree-G1 to demonstrate the ability of BRACE to exert, compensate force and handle diverse terrain in simulation and real robot experiments.

</details>


### [143] [Behavioral Cloning Mystery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07056)
*Seohong Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OCBench，一个具有可控脚本策略的机器人操作基准，其策略模仿人类演示的关键特性，能在受控环境中复现多种关于行为克隆（BC）的反直觉现象，从而支持对BC相关现象的科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆虽简单，却存在许多反直觉现象，如模型对数据过拟合越严重性能反而越好，以及完整闭环策略在缺少动作分块时常常完全失败。然而研究这些"行为克隆之谜"面临挑战：真实世界中数据集和实验成本高且不完全可控；合成数据仿真中由于脚本策略与人类演示之间存在差异，这些现象不易被观察到。

Method: 提出OCBench，一个机器人操作基准，采用可控脚本策略，使这些策略具备与人类演示相似的特性。通过模仿人类演示的关键特性，OCBench在受控环境中复现多种与BC相关的传闻现象。借助GPU加速的环境和脚本策略，OCBench支持对已有BC相关现象进行科学研究。

Result: OCBench成功在受控环境中复现了许多与BC相关的传闻现象，并能够通过分析和反驳各种假设，对先前报道的BC相关现象进行科学研究。

Conclusion: OCBench为研究行为克隆的反直觉现象提供了可控、高效且可复现的实验平台，有助于科学地分析和验证关于BC现象的各类假设。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning (BC), despite its simplicity, exhibits many counterintuitive phenomena in the real world. For example, the performance of BC often keeps increasing as the model overfits more to the dataset, and fully closed-loop policies often completely fail without action chunking. Unfortunately, properly studying these anecdotal phenomena ("behavioral cloning mysteries") is challenging: in the real world, datasets and experiments are costly and not fully controllable; in simulation with synthetic data, these phenomena are often not easily observed partly due to the discrepancy between scripted policies and human demonstrations. In this work, we propose OCBench, a robotic manipulation benchmark with controllable scripted policies that have similar properties to human demonstrations. We show that, by mimicking key properties of human demonstrations, OCBench reproduces many anecdotal BC-related phenomena in controlled settings. With its GPU-accelerated environments and scripted policies, we demonstrate how OCBench enables scientific studies of previously reported BC-related phenomena by analyzing and refuting various hypotheses. Project page: https://seohong.me/projects/ocbench

</details>


### [144] [Demo: Vision-Language Model-Guided Online Calibration of an Electromagnetic Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07081)
*Zerui Kang,Yishen Lim,Zhouyou Gu,Seungnyun Kim,Seung-Woo Ko,Tony Q. S. Quek,Jihong Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种视觉语言模型（VLM）引导的电磁数字孪生在线校准框架，用于移动机器人的无线态势感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁数字孪生依赖随环境变化的材料电导率，在线校准面临初始化敏感性和测量移动成本高的问题。

Method: 在Unitree G1机器人和NVIDIA Sionna平台上，使用两次VLM调用：材料分类将可见材料通过ITU-R P.2040映射为电导率先验，供Sionna基于累积RSS测量做梯度下降；路径点规划利用残余RSS校准误差和图像覆盖在线选择下一测量位置。

Result: 在真实室内场景中，框架在20米移动范围内实现归一化平均绝对电导率误差1.74×10⁻⁴；随机初始化无法收敛，随机路径点需要两倍以上移动距离。

Conclusion: VLM引导的先验与在线路径点规划能有效提升电磁数字孪生校准的精度并降低移动成本。

Abstract: An electromagnetic (EM) digital twin gives mobile robots wireless situational awareness but depends on material conductivities that change with the environment. Online calibration faces initialization sensitivity and measurement travel costs. We demonstrate a vision-language model (VLM)-guided framework using a Unitree G1 robot and NVIDIA Sionna, with two VLM calls: material classification maps visible materials through ITU-R P.2040 to conductivity priors for Sionna's gradient descent on accumulated received signal strength (RSS) measurements; waypoint planning selects the next measurement location online using residual RSS calibration error and image coverage. In a real indoor scenario, the framework achieves a normalized mean absolute conductivity error of $1.74\times10^{-4}$ within 20 m of travel; random initialization never converges, while random waypoints require over twice the travel.

</details>


### [145] [TeleHairing: A Teleoperation Baseline for Robotic Haircutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07096)
*Zhendai Huang,Aleksi Vilkki,Jianan Huang,Bolin Liao,Chunbo Luo,Leopoldo Angrisani,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TeleHairing——一套用于假人理发评估的闭环遥操作架构，在本地、中继与远程三种部署条件下量化了延迟、轨迹跟随误差与中断处理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人理发需要在头部附近进行受控工具运动，同时兼顾通信、视觉反馈、工具驱动与中断处理。现有研究尚缺乏"人在环路"的参考基准，难以在人体试验或更高自主性之前分析这些耦合行为。

Method: 构建闭环遥操作架构 TeleHairing，面向假人理发场景，在本地（local）、中继（relay）与远程（remote）三种部署条件下进行实验评估。通过时序日志测量各环节延迟，通过轨迹分析定位误差来源，并通过检测丢失（detection-loss）试验考察中断后的恢复行为（rebase 事件）。

Result: 远程模式下总时序达 190.5 ms，主要延迟增量发生在机器人侧控制端点之前；而机器人侧命令队列、控制处理与控制到机器人的时序在各模式间保持相近。远程命令跟随误差主要由末端撤出段（terminal withdrawal segment）主导，而非全路径均匀累积；剔除该段后远程 RMSE 从 45.1 mm 降至 9.6 mm。检测丢失试验表明，在所测条件下 rebase 事件恢复运动时未出现大的目标跳变。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了部署方式、执行过程与中断处理如何影响机器人理发遥操作回路，为后续自主性、安全性与面向用户的研究提供了定量参考基准。

Abstract: Robotic haircutting requires controlled tool motion near the head while simultaneously accounting for communication, visual feedback, tool actuation, and interruption handling. Existing studies still lack an operator-in-the-loop reference for analyzing these coupled behaviors before human trials or stronger autonomy. This paper presents TeleHairing, a closed-loop teleoperation architecture for mannequin-based robotic haircutting evaluation under local, relay, and remote deployment conditions. Logged timing shows that the main remote latency increase occurs before the robot-side control endpoint: overall timing reached 190.5~ms in remote mode, while robot-side command queue, control processing, and control-to-robot timing remained similar across modes. Trajectory analysis shows that the larger remote command-following error was dominated by the terminal withdrawal segment rather than accumulated uniformly over the path; excluding this segment reduced remote root-mean-square error (RMSE) from 45.1 mm to 9.6 mm. Detection-loss trials further show that rebase events resumed motion without a large target jump under the tested condition. These results clarify how deployment, execution, and interruption affect the robotic haircutting teleoperation loop, providing a quantitative reference for future autonomy, safety, and user-facing studies.

</details>


### [146] [AIM: Adaptive Interaction Modeling Networks for Real-to-Sim Soft-Body Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07116)
*Tiancheng Yang,Dingshuo Chen,Tianle Chen,Zhaocheng Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AIM框架，将真实到仿真的软体仿真建模为局部-全局交互问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作可变形物体（如叠衣、处理食物）需控制形状变化与物体运动。预测性软体仿真可支持这类任务，但现有方法存在两个问题：一是空间邻域可能错误表达形变依赖关系，引入的局部误差会在连续预测中累积；二是针对单个场景拟合的模型难以适应物体几何与操作条件的变化。

Method: AIM利用运动历史与几何信息，在当前空间邻居和保留连接上自适应调整粒子关系，并通过几何条件化的全局通信协调物体整体响应。采用统一的运动学控制点接口表示不同操作配置，并以多步自回归监督在自身预测轨迹上训练模型。

Result: 在PhysTwin与PGND数据集上实验表明，运动精度与视觉保真度均有提升：相对PhysTwin，未来预测跟踪误差降低20.0%；相对PGND，在六类物体上平均长时程粒子误差降低22.8%。该框架还支持跨动作、跨物体实例和跨场景迁移，包括从机器人交互到人类操作的零样本迁移，且无需目标域动力学拟合。

Conclusion: AIM通过局部-全局交互建模与自适应粒子关系，有效缓解误差累积并提升泛化能力，实现了真实到仿真的软体仿真，并具备跨动作、实例与场景的迁移能力。

Abstract: Deformable-object manipulation is essential for robotic tasks such as folding laundry and handling food, where robots must control shape changes as well as object motion. Predictive soft-body simulation supports these tasks by anticipating deformation under external interactions. However, spatial neighborhoods can misrepresent deformation dependencies, introducing local errors that accumulate over successive predictions. Models fitted to individual scenes must also accommodate changes in object geometry and manipulation conditions. In this work, we propose AIM, an Adaptive Interaction Modeling framework that treats real-to-sim soft-body simulation as a local-global interaction modeling problem. AIM uses motion history and geometry to adapt particle relations over current spatial neighbors and retained connections, while geometry-conditioned global communication coordinates object-wide responses. A unified kinematic control-point interface represents different manipulation configurations, and multi-step autoregressive supervision trains the model on its own predicted trajectories. Experiments on PhysTwin and PGND demonstrate improved motion accuracy and visual fidelity, with a 20.0% reduction in future-prediction tracking error relative to PhysTwin and a 22.8% reduction in mean long-horizon particle error across six object categories relative to PGND. The framework further supports transfer across actions, object instances, and scenes, including zero-shot transfer from robot interactions to human manipulation without target-domain dynamics fitting.

</details>


### [147] [RoboCap: A New Platform for Egocentric Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07217)
*Grounded Superintelligence,BitRobot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboCap惯捕采集硬件（250克六摄像头双IMU帽子）及配套Grounded API三维算法套件，用于野外第一视角操作数据采集，并在SLAM、深度估计、手部跟踪等公开基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角操作数据对机器人学习规模化至关重要，但目前数据仍然稀缺。大规模采集需要将符合人体工学的硬件与厘米级精度的三维算法垂直整合，而现有公开工作尚未展示出该精度水平的整合能力。

Method: 设计250克六摄像头双IMU帽子RoboCap用于野外第一视角数据采集，并开发设备无关的Grounded API三维算法套件，针对RoboCap进行调优；通过硬件、标定与三维算法的协同交互实现性能优化。

Result: 在公开基准上取得最先进性能：在不同场景与设备配置下的SLAM、第一视角场景的深度估计，以及适配到第三方设备时的手部跟踪。

Conclusion: 硬件、标定与三维算法的紧密协同是实现高精度第一视角数据采集的关键，该方案为可规模化的机器人学习数据采集提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Despite its promise for scaling robot learning, egocentric manipulation data is still scarce today. Collection at scale requires vertically integrating ergonomic hardware with centimeter-precise 3D algorithms, at a precision that has not been publicly demonstrated. To address this gap, we introduce RoboCap, a 250\,g six-camera dual-IMU hat designed for in-the-wild egocentric data capture, and the Grounded API, a suite of device-agnostic 3D algorithms tuned for RoboCap. In this report, we demonstrate how hardware, calibration, and 3D algorithms interact to achieve state-of-the-art performance on the public benchmarks: our SLAM across diverse settings and rigs, our depth estimation on egocentric settings, and our hand tracking when adapted to third-party devices.

</details>


### [148] [Monocular Navigation Relative to Unknown Spacecraft Using a Transformer-Aided Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07231)
*Pol Francesch Huc,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用单目图像的未知航天器位姿估计学习管线，结合Transformer网络与MSCKF，在SPE3R数据集上实现对未见目标的泛化，姿态中位误差3.7°、ROE距离误差2.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的位姿估计方法存在局限：需要目标形状或惯性的先验知识，依赖深度、激光雷达或立体视觉等额外传感器，或只能恢复至尺度的平移。本文旨在仅用单个服务航天器的单目相机，对未知（未见过）航天器在交会与近距离操作中实现完整位姿估计。

Method: 管线由两部分组成：1）Transformer神经网络，基于SuperPoint特征并由LightGlue匹配，从图像估计里程计（图像间至尺度位姿变化）；2）多状态约束卡尔曼滤波（MSCKF），将伪测量与轨道及姿态运动学模型结合，直接估计相对轨道要素、目标相对相机姿态及角速度。通过服务航天器的姿态机动恢复单目情况下目标距离的完全可观测性。在重渲染的SPE3R高分辨率数据集上训练与评估，含103个航天器图像，其中11个在训练时留出以测试泛化。

Result: 在留出航天器的渲染轨迹上进行蒙特卡洛仿真评估，学习视觉管线作为卡尔曼滤波前端，对未知目标导航时姿态中位误差为3.7°，相对轨道要素（ROE）距离误差为2.2%。

Conclusion: 学习型视觉管线可作为卡尔曼滤波前端，在仅用单目相机、无目标先验且无需额外传感器的条件下，对未知航天器实现泛化性良好的交会与近距离位姿估计。

Abstract: This work presents a novel learning-based pipeline for pose estimation of unknown spacecraft using only monocular images from a single servicer. The approach combines a transformer-based neural network with a Multi-State Constraint Kalman Filter (MSCKF) to estimate the pose (i.e., position and orientation of the target spacecraft relative to the camera) throughout rendezvous and proximity operations. Unlike existing vision-based methods that require prior knowledge of the target shape or inertia properties, rely on additional sensing modalities such as depth, lidar, or stereo, or only recover translation up to scale, the proposed pipeline generalizes to previously unseen spacecraft using a single monocular camera. The transformer network estimates the odometry, the change in pose between images up to scale, from SuperPoint features matched by LightGlue. The MSCKF uses these pseudo-measurements along with an orbit and attitude kinematics model to estimate the pose of the target. In particular, the relative orbit elements, the target's attitude with respect to the servicer's camera, and the associated angular velocity are estimated directly by the filter. Given the monocular approach and short distance to the target, the full observability of the range to the target is recovered via attitude maneuvers by the servicer. The method is trained and evaluated on a re-rendered high-resolution version of the SPE3R dataset, which includes synthetic images of 103 spacecraft. Eleven of these spacecraft are held out during training to evaluate the generalization to unseen targets. Monte Carlo simulations are then used to evaluate the navigation pipeline on rendered trajectories of the held out spacecraft. The results demonstrate that learned vision pipelines as a front-end for Kalman filters provide median errors of 3.7° in attitude and 2.2% of range in ROE when navigating about unknown targets.

</details>


### [149] [Robust Nonprehensile Object Transport with Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07245)
*Ainoor Teimoorzadeh,Riccardo Pretto,Mario Selvaggio,Gokhan Alcan,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四足机器人的鲁棒非抓取物体运输框架，通过不确定性感知轨迹优化与耦合凸模型预测控制，在物体惯性参数变化下实现更稳定的运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人非抓取运输中，物体惯性参数存在不确定性，传统固定姿态或直线轨迹对参数变化敏感，易导致物体滑动。

Method: 采用不确定性感知轨迹优化生成对不确定参数闭环敏感度最小的物体运动；用耦合凸模型预测控制器联合预测四足与负载的质心动力学，再通过全身QP施加地面反作用力约束进行跟踪。

Result: 在大量仿真与真实实验、物体惯性参数变化条件下评估，与固定姿态和直线轨迹基线对比，优化轨迹使滑动分别减少约50%和30%，同时机器人质心跟踪误差更低。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效提升四足机器人非抓取运输的鲁棒性与跟踪精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a robust nonprehensile object transportation framework for quadruped robots. An uncertainty-aware trajectory optimization method generates object motions with minimal closed-loop sensitivity to uncertain parameters. The resulting reference trajectory is tracked using a coupled convex model predictive controller that jointly predicts the CoM dynamics of the quadruped and the payload followed by a whole-body QP that enforces ground reaction constraints. The approach is evaluated through extensive simulations and real-world experiments under variations in the object's inertial parameters. Its performance is compared with fixed-orientation and straight-line trajectories as baseline. The results show that the optimized object motion reduces the sliding by approximately 50% compared with the fixed-orientation baseline and 30% compared with the straight-line baseline, while also achieving lower robot CoM tracking errors.

</details>


### [150] [Fluorescence-enhanced Whisker Array with Vision-based Deformation Analysis for Underwater Source Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07275)
*Xiaochi Xie,Hao Li,Shixuan Zhao,Siyue Yao,Shuran Song,Mark R. Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出荧光增强须阵列传感系统，通过局部光学读出定位水下流体动力源，实现低光照动态场景的源定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深海生物观测中，传统光学和声学方法会引入刺激，改变动物行为并导致观测偏差，因此需要一种非侵入式的流体动力源定位手段。

Method: 设计五根空间取向的须（镍钛合金芯+荧光聚氨酯外壳），结合紫外激发与单目相机；通过图像增强与分割跟踪须的变形；用轻量级卷积神经网络从2秒序列中提取时间与跨须特征来估计源定位。

Result: 水池实验中平均空间定位误差88 mm（半径73.5 mm、角度2.5°），测试区域600 mm、±30°；并实现对移动推进器的实时定位。

Conclusion: 该方法在动态场景下具备流体动力源检测、定位与跟踪能力，有望集成到水下机器人中，用于低光照环境下的流体动力源探测。

Abstract: Deep-water biological observation is essential for understanding marine organisms and their interactions with the environment. However, conventional optical and acoustic approaches can introduce stimuli that alter animal behavior and bias biological observations. This paper proposes a fluorescence-enhanced whisker array sensing system that pinpoints underwater hydrodynamic sources through local optical readout rather than direct source imaging. Five spatially oriented whiskers, fabricated with nitinol cores and fluorescent urethane shells, are integrated with ultraviolet excitation and a monocular camera. Image enhancement and segmentation are applied to track the whisker deformation. A lightweight convolutional neural network captures temporal and cross-whisker features from 2 s sequences to estimate source localization. Pool experiments achieve a mean spatial localization error of 88 mm, with 73.5 mm in radius and $2.5^\circ$ in angle, across a test region of 600 mm with $\pm30^\circ$. Real-time localization of a moving thruster demonstrates the capability of the proposed method in dynamic scenarios, highlighting its potential for integration into underwater robots for hydrodynamic source detection, localization, and tracking in low-light environments.

</details>


### [151] [Distribution-Transfer Safe-Horizon MPC under Mode Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07277)
*Stephen Crawford,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对场景法MPC在分布不匹配下的安全性问题，提出置信集与乘法支配界，将安全-视界碰撞风险证书从采样分布迁移到所有可能分布，并用Wasserstein几何正则化模式概率重分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景采样的MPC虽然适合机会约束运动规划，但对分布不匹配非常敏感；当障碍物具有切换动态模式、且模式律未知时，采样分布与真实分布之间的偏差会破坏原有的安全证书。

Method: 从有限模式观测中构造未知类别模式律的置信集；推导乘法支配界，将Safe-Horizon碰撞风险证书从选定的场景采样分布传递到置信集内每个分布；用Wasserstein几何按模式诱导轨迹预测的相似性正则化模式间概率重分配；同时用碰撞风险代理将采样偏向危险模式。

Result: 得到的证书显式量化了分布不匹配下所需的额外收紧量，并揭示出多个障碍物各自的迁移因子组合时产生的乘法保守性。

Conclusion: 该工作为带有未知切换模式障碍物的场景法SH-MPC提供了分布不匹配下的安全性保证，明确了保守性来源，并为后续降低组合保守性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Scenario-based MPC is an attractive strategy for chance-constrained motion planning that approximates uncertainty via a finite set of sampled scenarios. As a sampling-based method, scenario-based MPC is sensitive to distribution mismatch. We address this problem in the context of Safe-Horizon Model Predictive Control (SH-MPC) with obstacles governed by switching dynamic modes. From finite mode observations, we construct a confidence set for the unknown categorical mode law and derive a multiplicative domination bound that transfers a Safe-Horizon collision-risk certificate from a selected scenario-sampling distribution to every law in the confidence set. Wasserstein geometry is used to regularize probability reallocation among modes according to the similarity of their induced trajectory predictions, while a collision-risk surrogate biases sampling toward dangerous modes. The resulting certificate explicitly quantifies the additional tightening required under distribution mismatch and exposes the multiplicative conservatism that arises when several obstacle-wise transfer factors are combined

</details>


### [152] [Embedded Bare-Metal Radar-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07278)
*Nicolai Adil Øyen Aatif,Morten Nissov,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向低算力平台的嵌入式雷达-惯性状态估计器，全部传感器驱动、数据处理与辅助惯性导航在单核微控制器上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型行星探测车和微型无人机需要在严格算力约束和恶劣环境下运行，而视觉或LiDAR方案计算开销大且易受感知退化影响，因此需要兼具鲁棒性与计算效率的替代方案。

Method: 利用调频连续波（FMCW）雷达直接提供速度测量并具抗感知退化能力，设计嵌入式雷达-惯性估计器，将传感器驱动、数据处理和辅助惯性导航全部部署于单核微控制器上。

Result: 飞行实验显示，相对动作捕捉的平移APE为0.51–0.82 m，RPE小于3%，并实现了未经修改的PX4栈闭环飞行；固件与PCB设计已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在资源受限平台上实现鲁棒雷达-惯性状态估计的可行性，减少了对人工监督的依赖，并提供了开放硬件与固件。

Abstract: Compact extraplanetary rovers and micro aerial vehicles require robust state estimation frameworks designed to operate under strict computational constraints in unforgiving environments. Typical solutions involving vision- or LiDAR-based sensing are computationally expensive and vulnerable to environments with perceptual degradation, making them poorly suited for resource-constrained platforms and austere conditions. Alternatively, Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar offers both robustness and computational efficiency by directly providing velocity measurements coupled with inherent resilience to perceptual degradation. These factors enable robust estimation, thereby reducing reliance on human operators for supervision to ensure platform safety in challenging environments. In this manuscript, we propose an embedded radar-inertial estimator tailored for low-compute platforms. All sensor drivers, data processing, and aided inertial navigation are performed on a single-core microcontroller, demonstrating its computational efficiency. Flight experiments show translational APE of $0.51-0.82\,\mathrm{m}$ and RPE of $<$3\,\% against motion capture, alongside closed-loop flight through an unmodified PX4 stack. The firmware and printed circuit board design are openly available on GitHub at \href{https://github.com/ntnu-arl/embedded_rio}{ntnu-arl/embedded\_rio} and \href{https://github.com/ntnu-arl/embedded_rio-pcb}{ntnu-arl/embedded\_rio-pcb} respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Lego-Like Stiffness Configuration of Planar Compliant Modules for Task-Specific Flexible Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07319)
*Siyue Yao,Xiaochi Xie,Shixuan Zhao,Yutong Li,Hao Li,Mark R. Cutkosky,Genliang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种类乐高刚度配置方法，利用可堆叠平面柔顺模块构建任务定制刚度特性，并通过两级优化实现目标刚度，实验偏差低于6.5%，并开发了柔性腕部应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同任务需要不同的刚度特性，但现有柔顺机构受限于几何设计空间不足以及多刚度分量之间的内在耦合，往往需要针对具体任务重新优化和重新设计，缺乏通用性和系统性。

Method: 引入三种互补的模块几何结构，通过梁宽、板厚和模块朝向调控刚度；建立统一刚度模型对单个与组合模块进行定量分析；提出两级优化方法，结合遗传算法进行构型搜索、序列二次规划进行参数细化。

Result: 实验验证显示仿真刚度偏差低于6.5%；开发了柔性腕部作为代表性实现，在不同刚度特性下表现出不同的柔顺与动态响应；优化的模块化组合实现预设刚度值，在1 m/s高速运动中保持柔顺避障交互，最大测试角柔顺约15°。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建具有任务特定刚度特性的柔性接口提供了系统化方法，兼具可配置性、可优化性与实际应用可行性。

Abstract: Compliant mechanisms provide compact and intrinsic structural compliance for regulating physical interactions between mechanisms and environments. However, different tasks demand distinct stiffness characteristics, often requiring task-specific optimization and redesign due to limited geometric design space and inherent coupling among multiple stiffness components. This paper presents a Lego-like stiffness configuration approach using stackable planar compliant modules. Three complementary module geometries are introduced, with their stiffness characteristics further regulated through beam width, plate thickness, and module orientation. A unified stiffness model is established for quantitative analysis of individual and composed modules. Further, a two-stage optimization method is presented to achieve desired stiffness profiles, combining a genetic algorithm for configuration and sequential quadratic programming for parameter refinement. Experimental verification shows deviations below 6.5% for simulated stiffness. A flexible wrist is further developed as a representative implementation, exhibiting distinct compliant and dynamic responses under different stiffness characteristics. An optimized modular composition realizes prescribed stiffness values and maintains compliant obstacle interaction during high-speed motion at 1 m/s, with a maximum tested angular compliance of approximately $15^\circ$. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach for constructing flexible interfaces with task-specific stiffness characteristics.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Decentralized Formation of Minimum-Length Communication Networks Using Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07320)
*Genki Miyauchi,Mohamed S. Talamali,Julian Rau,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全去中心化的多机器人通信网络构建与动态维护框架，机器人仅依赖局部交互即可重配置拓扑与调整位置，在保证连通性的同时最小化网络长度，逼近集中式欧氏斯坦纳树性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础设施缺失或通信受阻的环境（如地下探索、行星任务）中，空间分离位置之间需要可靠通信链路，但现有方法依赖集中式拓扑规划或全局定位基础设施，限制自主性。

Method: 设计完全去中心化的局部交互规则：机器人通过局部重配置操作（如分支转移）调整局部网络拓扑和物理位置，不依赖全局定位或集中规划，用于构建并动态维护满足通信约束的通信网络。

Result: 形式化分析表明局部重配置保证网络持续连通、每次分支转移严格缩短网络长度，并将最坏性能限制在最短星形树；实体仿真与物理多机器人实验证实网络长度接近集中式计算得到的欧氏斯坦纳树，且能适应移动目标、仅用必要连接器并保留多余机器人用于辅助任务。

Conclusion: 该工作使自主集群能在无通信环境中自组织自适应自组网通信基础设施，可支持从地下探索到行星任务等多样化应用。

Abstract: Multi-robot missions in infrastructure-denied environments frequently rely on reliable communication links between spatially separated locations. We propose a fully decentralized framework for constructing and dynamically maintaining communication networks without centralized topology planning or global positioning infrastructure. Driven strictly by local interactions, robots reconfigure local network topologies and adjust their physical positions to minimize overall network length while adhering to communication constraints. Formal analysis shows that our local reconfiguration operations guarantee continuous network connectivity, strictly decrease network length with every branch transfer, and bound worst-case performance to the shortest starlike tree. Embodied simulations and physical multi-robot experiments confirm that our approach forms networks near the length of centrally computed Euclidean Steiner trees. Additionally, the system dynamically adapts to moving targets and optimizes deployment by utilizing only necessary connectors, preserving excess robots for auxiliary tasks. This work enables autonomous swarms to self-organize adaptive ad hoc communication infrastructure in communication-denied environments, which could support applications varying from subterranean exploration to planetary missions.

</details>


### [155] [SharedKV-BT: Node-Local Typed Decisions for Behavior-Tree Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07327)
*Naoki Wake,Justin Wagle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SharedKV-BT，用行为树节点暴露阶段局部字段与候选，Shared-KV 并行打分并将选定决策交给独立执行系统，在机器人操作、移动导航与计算机使用任务上实现更快的类型化决策与更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体任务需要序列化的相互依赖决策。自回归模型决策接口灵活，但逐 token 生成带来延迟；现有共享前缀方法虽能复用编码上下文并行打分多个决策，却不建模决策依赖，也不验证执行过程。

Method: SharedKV-BT 将行为树（BT）的每个活跃节点作为决策单元，暴露阶段局部字段与候选；Shared-KV 对这些候选并行打分，再把选中的决策传递给独立的执行系统，从而在保持并行效率的同时引入结构化的阶段依赖与执行验证。

Result: 在机器人操作、移动导航和计算机使用三类任务中，SharedKV-BT 做出类型化决策的速度是提示匹配的自回归解码的 2.36–4.15 倍；在操作任务上，节点局部 Shared-KV 将联合决策准确率从 75% 提升到 94%，闭环成功率从 0% 提升到 60%。固定分数策略回放显示阶段门控防止了乱序动作，外部后置条件防止了过早完成。

Conclusion: 将并行候选打分与行为树的结构化阶段控制、执行验证结合，可同时提升决策速度、准确率与闭环可靠性。

Abstract: Agent tasks require sequences of interdependent decisions. Autoregressive models support more flexible decision interfaces than conventional classifiers but incur the latency of token-by-token generation. Recent shared-prefix methods reduce this cost by reusing encoded context and scoring multiple decisions in parallel, but do not model decision dependencies or verify execution. We propose SharedKV-BT, where each active node of a behavior tree (BT) exposes stage-local fields and candidates, and Shared-KV scores the candidates in parallel and passes the selected decision to a separate execution system. We tested SharedKV-BT on robot manipulation, mobile navigation, and computer-use tasks. Across three tasks, SharedKV-BT made typed decisions 2.36-4.15 times faster than prompt-matched autoregressive decoding. On the manipulation task, node-local Shared-KV improved joint decision accuracy from 75% to 94% and closed-loop success from 0% to 60%. Fixed-score policy replay showed that stage gating prevented out-of-order actions and external postconditions prevented premature completion.

</details>


### [156] [ScanSTL: Parallel Robustness Evaluation for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07346)
*Gokhan Alcan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScanSTL：用并行扫描与有序块归约实现STL鲁棒性的高效求值与可微计算，较STLCG++大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL鲁棒性的重复求值与微分是机器人规划控制的算力瓶颈：时序递归限制并行，稠密掩码增加内存开销。需要兼顾并行性、内存效率与精确性的方法。

Method: 将结合性时序聚合与并行扫描、有序块归约结合；Eventually与Always用区间极值，强Until组合紧凑分段表示；统一区间引擎处理有界与移位区间（含延迟Until见证所需守卫）。每个时序算子以线性工作量与存储、对数并行深度计算完整鲁棒性轨迹，并提供支持自动微分、批处理与编译的JAX开源实现。

Result: 在CPU与GPU算子基准及九个复合规范上对比STLCG与STLCG++。512采样下，相对JAX版STLCG++，前向求值几何平均加速243倍，梯度计算104倍。RTX 5090上对超两百万样本的无界Until，前向中位时间<0.1 ms，梯度<0.25 ms。机器狗护送与巡逻仿真显示更快修复违规规划，并提升MPC求解容量。

Conclusion: ScanSTL以线性/对数复杂度与并行扫描实现精确STL鲁棒性求值与微分，显著提升速度与内存效率，实测加速机器人规划控制中的修复与MPC性能。

Abstract: Repeated evaluation and differentiation of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) robustness can become a computational bottleneck in robot planning and control. Sequential temporal recurrences limit parallelism, while dense masking increases memory requirements. We propose ScanSTL, which combines associative temporal aggregation with parallel scans and ordered block reductions. Eventually and Always use range extrema, while inclusive strong Until composes compact segment representations. A common range engine handles bounded and shifted intervals, including the guards required before delayed Until witnesses. Each exact temporal operator computes complete robustness traces with linear work and storage and logarithmic parallel depth on uniformly sampled finite signals. An open source JAX implementation supports automatic differentiation, batching, and compilation. We compare ScanSTL with STLCG and STLCG++ using CPU and GPU operator benchmarks and nine composed specifications. Across these nine specifications at 512 samples, ScanSTL achieves geometric mean speedups of 243 times for forward evaluation and 104 times for gradient computation over STLCG++ in JAX on the CPU. On an RTX~5090 GPU, ScanSTL evaluates unbounded Until over more than two million samples with median times below 0.1 ms for forward evaluation and 0.25 ms for gradient computation. Simulated escort and patrol experiments with a robot dog further demonstrate faster repair of violating plans and greater solver capacity in model predictive control.

</details>


### [157] [Expressiveness, Equivalence, and Uncertainty in Velocity Obstacles and Closest Point of Approach Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07371)
*Elizabeth Dietrich,Liam M. Imagawa,Hanna Krasowski,Aurora Haraldsen,Murat Arcak,Kristin Y. Pettersen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文建立了TCPA、DCPA与速度障碍(VO)之间的关系，并分析其在确定性及有界不确定性下对碰撞风险评估的等价性与行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCPA、DCPA和VO广泛用于自主导航碰撞风险评估，但三者之间的关系以及在不确定性下的行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 在完美状态信息下，于有限与无限预测时域内建立这些表示之间的关系，推导其等价刻画碰撞风险的条件；在有界不确定性下，将CPA指标与VO推广至凸相对状态集。

Result: 在不确定性设置下，独立计算的TCPA与DCPA边界丢失了VO隶属所需的联合关系，而不确定性感知的VO通过碰撞诱导速度的集合值表示保留了该关系。

Conclusion: VO在不确定性下能更好保持与CPA指标的一致性关系，为碰撞风险评估提供更稳健的表征。

Abstract: Time to Closest Point of Approach (TCPA), Distance to Closest Point of Approach (DCPA), and Velocity Obstacles (VOs), are widely used to assess and mitigate collision risk in autonomous navigation, yet their relationship and behavior under uncertainty remain largely unexplored. Assuming perfect state information, we establish a relationship between these representations over finite and infinite prediction horizons and derive conditions under which they provide equivalent characterizations of collision risk. Under bounded uncertainty, we extend the Closest Point of Approach (CPA) metrics and VO to convex relative-state sets. We show that in this setting, independently computed TCPA and DCPA bounds lose the joint relationship required for VO membership, while uncertainty-aware VOs preserve this relationship through a set-valued representation of collision-inducing velocities.

</details>


### [158] [Toward Trustworthy Physical AI for Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07382)
*Niccolò Pagliarani,Maximilian Stölzle,Cosima du Pasquier,Jeff Lui,Andrew Sabelhaus,Cecilia Laschi,Gentiane Venture,Daniela Rus,Matteo Cianchetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向可信物理AI的框架，将安全性、行为可理解性与感知一致性整合到具身、控制、认知与设计四个层面，认为可信度源于物理能力、可观察行为与人类交互期望三者的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人进入人类生活空间的速度快于我们建立其可信度判定标准的速度，因此亟需系统性的可信物理AI框架。

Method: 提出一个概念性框架，围绕安全（Safety）、行为可理解性（Behavioral Intelligibility）与感知一致性（Perceptual Alignment）三个维度，覆盖具身、控制、认知与设计四个层面进行整合。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果或量化指标，属于框架性/立场性论述。

Conclusion: 可信度并非单一技术属性，而是物理能力、可观察行为与人在交互中形成的期望之间实现对齐的产物。

Abstract: Robots are entering human spaces faster than we can establish when they deserve trust. We propose a framework for trustworthy physical AI that integrates Safety, Behavioral Intelligibility, and Perceptual Alignment across embodiment, control, cognition, and design. Trustworthiness emerges from aligning physical capabilities, observable behavior, and expectations people form during interaction.

</details>


### [159] [AeroBuoy: A Drone Deployable, 3D Printed, Autonomous Robotic Buoy for Environmental Inspection in Remote and Hazardous River Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07390)
*Reuben O'Brien,Angus Lynch,Minas Liarokapis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可搭载无人机投放与回收的自主数据采集浮标环境检查系统，浮标借助河流自然水流、GPS与磁力计实现自主导航并采集水质数据，并在新西兰奥克兰Ōrewa河完成了完整系统演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远及危险的河流、溪流水道监测对评估施工径流或气候变化等外部因素影响十分重要。自主机器人船虽能提供良好的环境巡检方案，但在机动性方面存在不足，难以适应难以进入的河系。

Method: 设计一种可由无人机部署到不可达河系的自主数据采集浮标，可依据具体地点与监测任务需求进行投放和回收。浮标利用河流自然水流自主向下游航行，通过GPS与磁力计维持期望轨迹。浮标可测水温，也可搭载水氧仪、河床探测声呐、浊度计等多种传感器。文中还给出了系统设计并对浮标自扶正能力进行了分析。

Result: 给出了系统设计描述、浮标自扶正能力分析，并在新西兰奥克兰Ōrewa河进行了完整系统演示。

Conclusion: 该无人机部署的自主浮标系统可为偏远、危险或受保护水域提供可行的环境检查与监测方案，补充了现有机器人船在机动性上的不足。

Abstract: Monitoring of waterways such as remote and hazardous rivers and streams is important so as to assess the impact of external factors including construction runoff or climate change. Versatile, autonomous robotic boats can offer excellent environmental inspection and monitoring solutions for remote, dangerous, or access protected water bodies but they have several shortcomings in terms of maneuverability. This paper proposes an environmental inspection system consisting of an autonomous data collection buoy which is designed to be deployed to inaccessible river systems using a drone. The system can perform a drop off and pickup of the buoy depending on the requirements of a particular location and monitoring task. Utilising the natural flow of the river the buoy autonomously steers down, using GPS and magnetometers so as to maintain the desired trajectory. The buoy is capable of measuring water temperature but it can also be equipped with a range of sensors such as water oxygen meter, sonar for river bed inspection, or turbidity for water clarity. This paper describes the system design, presents an analysis of the self-righting capabilities of the buoy, and shows a full system demonstration at the Ōrewa River in Auckland, New Zealand.

</details>


### [160] [What the Elevation Map Cannot See: Semantic-Aware Locomotion and Execution-Aware Navigation for Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07396)
*Shunyu Yao,Songyang Liu,Dinghao Chen,Yuanyuan Lei,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对人形机器人视觉语言导航（VLN）的新基准，同时建模"高程图难以区分的隐性危险"与"运动执行偏差"，并提出闭环 VLN+运动控制框架，在仿真中评估并在 Unitree G1 实体机器人上验证运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人 VLN 基准虽实现了物理可执行的导航，但存在两个不切实际的假设：(1) 所有危险都能从高程图中观测到；(2) 实际运动与期望运动高度一致。现实中，倒地瓶子在高程图中语义模糊，手机与水渍难以区分；而运动策略为规避危险会使实际轨迹偏离 VLN 策略预期的路径，这种"指令-执行"不匹配会累积，把机器人引向非预期位置。因此需要一个能暴露这些失效模式的基准。

Method: 构建了一个同时包含"高程细微危险"与"执行偏差"的仿真基准；并设计了一个闭环的 VLN + 运动控制框架，持续将高层导航与机器人实际状态重新对齐。

Result: 在仿真中进行了导航评估，并在实体 Unitree G1 人形机器人上验证了运动策略。结果显示：语义输入可减少与高程图中表征不佳的危险物发生接触；抗偏差机制可提升导航成功率。

Conclusion: 人形机器人导航的评估需同时考虑路径完成度与危险规避两个维度，二者应联合评价而非单独衡量。

Abstract: Navigation for humanoid robots is critical, yet large-scale evaluation on physical hardware is often impractical due to cost and safety concerns, making simulation benchmarks essential. Existing VLN benchmarks achieve physically executable navigation, but still assume (1) all hazards are observable from elevation maps; (2) realized motions closely match desired motions. In real environments, however, fallen bottles may be ambiguous in elevation maps, while phones and water spills may be difficult to differentiate; hazard avoidance by the locomotion policy can cause the robot's actual trajectory to deviate from the path intended by the VLN policy. Such command-execution mismatch can accumulate and lead the robot toward unintended locations. To expose these failure modes, we introduce a benchmark that models both elevation-subtle hazards and execution deviations, together with a closed-loop VLN + locomotion control framework that continuously realigns high-level navigation with the robot's actual state. We evaluate navigation in simulation and further validate the locomotion policy on a physical Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Results show that semantic input reduces contact with hazards poorly represented in elevation maps, while anti-deviation improves navigation success. These findings highlight the need to evaluate humanoid navigation jointly in terms of route completion and hazard avoidance.

</details>


### [161] [An Autonomous, 3D Printed, Waterjet-Powered, Open-Source Robotic Trimaran for Environmental Inspection and Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07398)
*Reuben O'Brien,Martin Lambrechtse-Reid,Minas Liarokapis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本、自主水翼三体船平台，用于偏远或受保护水域的环境监测与水深测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏远、危险、难以到达或受保护水域缺乏高效的水下监测手段，传统科考船资源消耗大。研究旨在为这类环境（如新西兰多样水域）提供高效、低成本、易复制的自主监测方案。

Method: 设计并开发自主、低成本、喷水推进的三体船平台，采用现成商用组件与3D打印技术制造，配备水深测量和水质检测传感系统，具备避障能力，最高速度可达2 m/s。

Result: 平台开发成本约600-1500美元（取决于传感配置），重量小于5 kg，在水深测量与水质测试方面表现优异，可躲避岩石等自然障碍物，并能在浅水中通过喷水推进稳定运行。

Conclusion: 该三体船平台具备良好的可复制性与鲁棒性，为浅水环境监测提供了经济高效的替代方案，并具有未来数据采集与研究的应用潜力。

Abstract: Versatile, autonomous robotic boats can offer excellent environmental inspection and monitoring solutions for remote, dangerous, hard to reach, or access protected water bodies. This paper introduces such a platform in the form of an autonomous, cost-effective, waterjet-powered robotic trimaran. Motivated by the need for an efficient aquatic monitoring, particularly in Aotearoa - New Zealand's diverse environments, the trimaran provides an efficient, low-cost, and easy to replicate alternative to resource-intensive research vessels. The proposed platform, costs $600-1,500 USD to develop (depending on the sensing system configuration), weighs under 5 kg, and excels in bathymetry and water quality testing. The trimaran can reach speeds of up to 2 m/s offering obstacle avoidance of natural features, such as rocks. Utilizing off-the-shelf components and 3D printing technology, the proposed platform offers excellent reproducibility and robustness while operating in shallow waters with its jet propulsion system. The paper presents in detail the design characteristics, the sensing system employed, testing results focusing on bathymetry, and highlights the ability of vessel and the potential for future research and data collection.

</details>


### [162] [RACER: Residual-Adaptive Closed-Loop Estimation for Sampling-Based Planning in Wheeled-Quadruped Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07409)
*Yuxiang Liu,Marla Eisman,Lizhi Yang,Aaron Ames,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER分层控制框架，用MPPI规划器结合学习到的残差动力学模型与低层RL速度跟踪器，实现轮式四足机器人竞速，并提出LoRRA两阶段残差自适应方法以在有限真实数据下训练残差模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式四足竞速需要高层规划与低层速度跟踪协同，但RL速度跟踪器存在跟踪误差，尤其在高速转弯时，导致标称单轮车运动学模型不准确；同时真实世界数据有限，难以直接训练残差动力学模型。

Method: 框架将MPPI规划器与神经残差项结合，残差项用于捕捉RL策略的闭环跟踪行为；提出LoRRA两阶段方法：先在大规模仿真数据上预训练获得广泛覆盖，再用低秩约束在少量真实数据上微调。

Result: 仿真实验表明：(A) 残差动力学通过捕捉高速转弯时RL速度跟踪器的跟踪误差提升了整体性能；(B) 同时使用源域和目标域数据训练的残差动力学显著优于仅用目标域数据训练；(C) 当地面系数或关节增益的域差距增大时，低秩约束比全量微调和从零训练具有更高成功率和性能。

Conclusion: RACER通过残差动力学建模与LoRRA低秩自适应，有效结合仿真先验与有限真实数据，提升了轮式四足机器人竞速的控制性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present RACER, a hierarchical control framework for wheel-based quadruped racing that combines an MPPI planner with a learned residual dynamics model and a low-level RL velocity tracker. The planner augments a nominal unicycle kinematic model with a neural residual term to capture the closed-loop tracking behavior of the RL policy. To train this residual model under limited real-world data, we propose Low-Rank Residual Adaptation (LoRRA), a two-stage approach that pre-trains on large-scale simulation data for broad coverage and then fine-tunes on a small real-world dataset with a low-rank constraint. In simulation, we empirically validate our engineering choices by showing (A) Residual dynamics improve the overall performance of our pipeline by capturing the tracking error of RL velocity tracker at high-speed cornering. (B) Residual dynamics trained with both source-domain and target-domain data gives racing performance significantly better than the residual dynamics trained with only target-domain data. (C) Low-rank constraint at target-domain adaptation gives higher success rates and higher performance than full-tune and from-scratch when domain gap in ground coefficient or joint gain increases.

</details>


### [163] [Entropy-Gated Belief Coordination for Decentralized Multi-Agent Search Under Intermittent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07432)
*Mohamed Abdelnaby,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究泊松通信下的去中心化多智能体目标搜索，提出熵门控信念协调机制，在高熵探索阶段跳过信念融合、仅在利用阶段合并，并推导出无通信的传感器信息量度量，实验表明该方法显著优于多种融合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无条件信念融合会在高熵探索阶段浪费通信机会，使智能体过早达成共识，破坏了多样性独立信念带来的覆盖优势，因此需要一种能区分探索与利用阶段的协调机制。

Method: 提出熵门控信念协调：当集体熵比超过阈值θ时跳过融合，仅在利用阶段合并，该设计与非线性意见动力学的分岔结构和每步信息增益目标的子模结构一致；同时推导Istep(α,β)，即两智能体二值传感器每观测步的期望互信息，作为可解释且无需通信的传感器信息量度量；并据此构建加入检测概率规划器切换的EG-TDP。

Result: 在103,680次试验（九种网格至100×100、泊松通信时序、四种目标运动模式）中，EG-TDP取得平均信念质量Q̄=0.300，较算术平均提升58.9%，较访问加权融合提升37.4%；在代表性配置下，其表现还超过每步使用全部智能体观测的联合贝叶斯参考。

Conclusion: 熵门控的信念协调能够在随机间歇通信条件下有效利用通信机会，EG-TDP在信念质量上大幅优于常见融合策略，甚至超过拥有完整观测的联合贝叶斯基准，说明按探索/利用阶段差异化融合是提升多智能体目标搜索性能的关键。

Abstract: We study decentralized multi-agent target search where homogeneous agents communicate intermittently at Poisson-distributed times. Standard unconditional belief fusion wastes communication opportunities by synchronizing agents during high-entropy exploration, when diverse independent beliefs provide better coverage than a premature consensus. We introduce \emph{entropy-gated belief coordination}, in which agents skip fusion while their collective entropy ratio exceeds a threshold~\(θ\) and merge only during exploitation, consistent with the bifurcation structure of nonlinear opinion dynamics and the submodular structure of the per-step information gain objective. We further derive \(\Istep(α,β)\), the expected mutual information per observation step between two agents' binary sensors, as an interpretable, communication-free measure of sensor informativeness that motivates the gating design and guides system-level analysis. Experiments across 103{,}680 trials (nine grid sizes up to \(100{\times}100\), Poisson communication timing, four target movement patterns) show that the Entropy-Gated Trust-Decay Planner (\textsc{EG-TDP}), which adds a detection-probability planner switch in exploitation mode, achieves mean belief quality \(\bar{Q}=0.300\) (mean belief mass at the true target cell, averaged across all trials and steps), a \(58.9\%\) gain over arithmetic mean and a \(37.4\%\) gain over visit-weighted fusion. On representative configurations, EG-TDP also outperforms a joint-Bayesian reference that uses all agents'~observations at every step, despite operating under random intermittent contact only.

</details>


### [164] [Dynamics Modeling of a Multi-UAV Slung Load System Using a Discrete-Link Cable Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07456)
*Harvey Merton,Ian W. Hunter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种用多段刚性连杆建模柔性缆索的多无人机吊挂负载系统（MUSLS）动力学模型，采用Euler-Newton方法推导并适配Featherstone铰接体算法以高效仿真，通过真实飞行实验验证模型精度，并将飞行数据公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为简化多无人机吊挂负载系统控制问题，常用假设是将柔性缆索视为无质量刚性杆，但这种简化忽略了缆索的柔性与张力变化特性，可能影响模型保真度。因此需要更贴近真实物理特性的缆索建模方式，同时兼顾仿真效率。

Method: 提出一种基于Euler-Newton推导的动力学模型，将柔性缆索表示为一系列刚性连杆，并专门设计以适配Featherstone铰接体算法进行高效仿真。在温和、激进以及张力介入（tension-engagement）等机动动作下开展真实世界验证，并进行参数扫描以确定连杆数量、关节阻尼和关节摩擦的最优取值。

Result: 模型与真实飞行数据高度吻合：负载平移误差均值低于132 mm（约为缆索长度的5.5%），姿态误差低于11.4度。同时论文将真实飞行数据公开，以支持未来缆索模型的开发。

Conclusion: 用多段刚性连杆模拟柔性缆索的建模方法能够有效提升MUSLS模型的真实性与保真度，并可通过Featherstone算法保持高效仿真；公开的实验数据为后续缆索建模研究提供了基准与资源。

Abstract: A common assumption to simplify the problem of controlling a multi-UAV slung load system (MUSLS) is that the flexible cables can be modeled as massless rigid rods. In this work, we propose an alternative Euler-Newton derived dynamical model which uses a series of rigid links to model the flexible cables. The model is specifically designed to allow efficient simulation using Featherstone's articulated body algorithm. We perform real-world validation of this model on gentle, aggressive, and tension-engagement maneuvers and run a parameter sweep to determine the number of links, joint damping, and joint friction to achieve the greatest model fidelity. The model closely matches real-world flight data with mean load translation errors below 132 mm (5.5% of the cable length) and orientation errors below 11.4 degrees. We make the real-world flight data publicly available for the development of future cable models.

</details>


### [165] [SURGE: Sonar-fUsed Reconstruction and localization via image-gated Graph Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07472)
*Mohammed Ibrahim M,Vallabh Deogaonkar,Trung Dong,Jane Shin,Abhilash Somayajula,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SURGE通过因子图联合融合视觉与声呐观测，估计ROV轨迹和目标位置，并用恢复的度量位姿进行声呐高斯泼溅重建，提升了定位一致性和度量重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下ROV任务需要准确的三维环境理解，依赖可靠的载体定位与度量场景重建。但水下常无法获得外部定位，只能依赖机载感知。光学视觉信息丰富却存在尺度模糊与轨迹漂移，2D成像声呐提供度量距离但三维几何不完整。已有方法通常单独处理这些局限或假设已知传感器位姿，导致定位与重建相互割裂。

Method: 提出SURGE相机-声呐框架，在因子图内集成视觉与声学观测，联合估计ROV轨迹和目标位置，随后利用恢复的度量位姿进行声呐高斯泼溅重建。

Result: 在真实水下RGB-声呐观测上的实验表明，SURGE相比传统基于视觉的位姿估计显著提升了定位一致性，并比RGB高斯泼溅基线生成更紧凑、原生态度量的重建结果。

Conclusion: 联合视觉与声呐的因子图优化能够有效弥合水下定位与重建之间的脱节，实现度量一致的轨迹估计与场景重建。

Abstract: Remotely operated vehicles (ROVs) are widely used to explore and inspect underwater environments such as caves, shipwrecks, and submerged infrastructure. These missions require accurate 3D understanding of the surrounding environment, which depends on both reliable vehicle localization and metric scene reconstruction. However, external positioning is often unavailable underwater, requiring small ROVs to rely primarily on onboard perception. Optic vision provides rich visual and geometric information but suffers from scale ambi- guity and trajectory drift, whereas 2D imaging sonar provides metric range but incomplete 3D geometry. Existing underwater reconstruction approaches typically address these limitations separately or assume known sensor poses, leaving localization and reconstruction disconnected. We present SURGE, a camera sonar framework that jointly estimates the ROV trajectory and target location by integrating visual and acoustic observations within a factor graph, then uses the recovered metric poses for sonar Gaussian splatting. Experiments on real underwater RGB sonar observations show that SURGE substantially improves localization consistency over conventional vision based pose estimation and produces a more compact, natively metric reconstruction than RGB Gaussian splatting baselines.

</details>


### [166] [Risk-Sensitive Crowd Navigation with Adaptive Ellipsoidal Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07474)
*Ruihan A. Li,Ziyao Guo,Yingying Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种风险感知人群导航框架，利用各向异性共形椭球将结构化预测不确定性转化为episode级CVaR信号来调节拉格朗日安全惩罚，在分布外场景下显著提升成功率并降低碰撞率，并在真实机器人上零微调部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移下安全人群导航需要能捕捉结构化人类运动预测误差的不确定性表示，以及能应对罕见但后果严重失败的安全目标。现有不确定性感知方法通常用各向同性区域表示预测误差，要么过于保守，要么与方向性运动不确定性对齐不佳。

Method: 风险感知导航框架：采用自适应椭球共形预测（AECP）捕捉方向性预测误差并在分布偏移下自适应校准不确定性区域；将结构化预测不确定性转化为episode级条件风险价值（CVaR）信号，调节拉格朗日安全惩罚；导航策略用拉格朗日近端策略优化（Lagrangian PPO）进行优化。

Result: 在分布内与分布外（涉及行人运动模式偏移）设置下评估。相比最先进基线，分布内性能保持竞争性，分布外设置成功率提升5.68–7.44个百分点、碰撞率降低5.44–6.64个百分点。并在搭载板载感知、仅CPU推理的真实机器人上无需微调部署成功。

Conclusion: 各向异性共形椭球结合CVaR调节的拉格朗日安全优化，可在分布偏移下实现更安全高效的人群导航，且完整流程在真实机器人上具备可行性。

Abstract: Safe crowd navigation under distribution shift requires uncertainty representations that capture structured human-motion prediction errors and safety objectives that account for rare but consequential failures. Existing uncertainty-aware methods typically represent prediction errors using isotropic regions, which can be either overly conservative or poorly aligned with directional motion uncertainty. We introduce a risk-aware navigation framework that uses anisotropic conformal ellipsoids to translate structured prediction uncertainty into an episode-level conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) signal that regulates the Lagrangian safety penalty. In particular, adaptive ellipsoidal conformal prediction (AECP) captures directional prediction errors and adaptively calibrates uncertainty regions under distribution shift, while the resulting CVaR-regulated navigation policy is optimized using Lagrangian proximal policy optimization. We evaluate our proposed method under both in-distribution settings and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings involving shifts in pedestrian motion patterns. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, our method maintains competitive in-distribution performance while improving success rates by 5.68-7.44 percentage points and reducing collision rates by 5.44-6.64 percentage points across OOD settings. We further deploy the trained policy without fine-tuning on a physical robot with onboard perception and CPU-only inference, showing that the full pipeline is feasible in physical crowd navigation.

</details>


### [167] [MobileVISTA: Generative Data Augmentation for Pose Generalization in Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07511)
*Suzannah Wistreich,Stephen Tian,Isabella Huang,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Sergey Zakharov,Katherine Liu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MobileVISTA是一个数据生成框架，通过联合增强自我中心视觉观测与重定向动作，将单一机器人位姿采集的演示数据转化为多样化的位姿扰动训练数据，从而提升移动操作策略在部署时的位姿鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人（如人形机器人）在动态非结构化环境中执行灵巧操作任务时，基于单一机器人位姿采集的演示数据所训练的端到端模仿策略非常脆弱：部署时即使厘米级的位姿偏差，也会使自我中心观测和末端执行器轨迹偏离训练分布，导致性能急剧下降。现有方法假设相机刚性安装在驱动链之外，或假设机器人结构化几何大部分不在画面中，因此不适用于相机既受运动链影响又需观测运动链的自我中心平台。

Method: MobileVISTA通过两个联合手段将标准位姿采集的演示转化为多样且带位姿扰动的训练数据：(1) 增强自我中心视觉观测；(2) 重定向动作以补偿基座位姿变化。该方法无需额外采集演示数据，也无需训练生成模型。

Result: 在涵盖人形和双臂本体的模拟任务以及真实Galaxea R1 Pro平台上进行研究。结果显示，使用MobileVISTA增强数据训练的策略对测试时遇到的、此前处于分布外的位姿具有更强的鲁棒性，且无需额外演示采集或训练生成模型。此外，MobileVISTA的收益在所测试的人形机器人上最大，因为此时相机随驱动链运动且机器人在画面中占据较大比例。

Conclusion: MobileVISTA是一种有效且兼容自我中心平台（如人形机器人）的数据生成框架，能够在不增加演示采集或生成模型成本的前提下，显著提升移动操作策略对位姿扰动的鲁棒性，尤其适用于相机随运动链运动、机器人在画面中占比大的场景。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators such as humanoid robots are increasingly deployed in dynamic, unstructured environments to perform dexterous manipulation tasks. However, end-to-end manipulation policies trained to imitate demonstration data collected from a single robot pose are brittle: even centimeter-scale deviations in robot pose at deployment can drive ego-centric observations and end-effector trajectories out of the training distribution, leading to sharp drops in performance. We introduce MobileVISTA, a data generation framework that transforms demonstrations captured at canonical poses into diverse, pose-perturbed training data by jointly (1) augmenting egocentric visual observations and (2) retargeting actions to compensate for base pose changes. Unlike prior methods, which assume a camera rigidly mounted off the actuated chain or non-trivial articulated robot geometry largely out of frame, MobileVISTA targets compatibility with egocentric platforms (e.g., humanoids) where the camera is both influenced by and must observe the robot's kinematic chain as it moves. We study MobileVISTA in simulated tasks spanning humanoid and bimanual embodiments, and on a real Galaxea R1 Pro. We find policies trained on MobileVISTA-augmented data demonstrate improved robustness to previously out-of-distribution poses encountered at test time, without additional demonstration collection or a trained generative model. Additionally, we find MobileVISTA's benefit is largest on tested humanoids, where the camera rides the actuated chain and the robot fills much of the frame. Additional videos and appendix can be found on our website: https://mobilevista.github.io

</details>


### [168] [ProCut: Probabilistic Cutting Topology for Autonomous Electrosurgical Tissue Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07515)
*Xiao Liang,Fei Liu,Florian Richter,Genie Rubaiyat,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个完全可微的框架，通过连续化切割事件与概率推断，实现软组织变形跟踪中的拓扑变化建模，并用于自主切割控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软组织的变形建模与跟踪对介入和外科手术至关重要，但现有方法在涉及切割、剥离等拓扑变化时表现不佳，原因在于连接性显式改变带来的非线性与不连续性。

Method: 引入基于sigmoid的连续化公式，将离散的切割事件平滑化，使其可在可微的位置基础动力学（PBD）仿真中进行梯度优化；同时采用Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）进行粒子级概率推断，生成多个拓扑状态假设以应对噪声视觉数据的不确定性；并据此为薄壳组织开发了自主剥离算法，利用拓扑更新引导闭环切割轨迹控制。

Result: 在仿真和真实电外科环境中评估，拓扑估计精度与剥离精度相比现有方法均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架可在复杂结构变化下实现可靠感知与控制，有望推动软组织手术流程的自动化。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and tracking the deformation of soft tissue is critical for a wide range of interventional and surgical procedures. However, current methods struggle in scenarios involving topological changes, such as cutting and dissection, due to the inherent non-linearity and discontinuity introduced by explicit changes in connectivity. In this work, we present a novel, fully differentiable framework that enables robust estimation and modeling of topological changes during deformable tracking. Our method introduces a continuous, sigmoid-based formulation to smooth the otherwise discrete event of tissue cutting, making it amenable to gradient-based optimization within a differentiable Position-Based Dynamics (PBD) simulation. To account for uncertainty and improve robustness in the presence of noisy visual data, we incorporate Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD) for particle-based probabilistic inference, generating multiple hypotheses for topological state estimation. Building on this foundation, we develop an autonomous dissection algorithm for thin-shell tissues that leverages topological updates to guide closed-loop cutting trajectory control. We evaluate our approach in both simulated and real-world electrosurgical environments, demonstrating significant improvements in topological estimation accuracy and dissection precision over existing methods. Our results highlight the potential of this framework to advance automation in soft-tissue surgical procedures by enabling reliable perception and control in the presence of complex structural changes.

</details>


### [169] [ReDex: Repairing Sim-to-Real Dexterous Policies by Finger-Level Compliant Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07525)
*Jinzhou Li,Hadi Tabatabaee,Kelin Yu,Yuyin Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Roberto Martín-Martín,Nima Fazeli,X. Alice Wu,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDex：通过人类局部纠正接触失败并重建力反馈目标，将仿真训练的本体感觉策略适配到真实世界，显著提升接触丰富的灵巧操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的灵巧操作策略在真实世界中常因接触时序与力调节误差而迁移失败，但这些策略仍保留了可用的多指协调能力。因此需要一个既能纠正局部接触失败、又无需触觉仿真或复杂全手遥操作的适配框架。

Method: 以仅本体感觉的仿真基策略为起点，在真实部署中由人类操作者在柔顺控制下对选定手指的接触失败进行物理纠正，冻结的基策略继续控制其余手指；这些rollout包含基策略执行、人类纠正的手指运动与指尖力观测，据此重建含力信息的目标，并通过行为克隆训练独立的力条件策略。

Result: 在两个接触丰富的真实硬件灵巧操作任务上评估。相比仿真到真实迁移的基策略，ReDex将Object Flipping成功率从14%提升至86%（两个物体），Screwdriver Rotation平均进度从26.0%提升至95.3%（三个物体）。

Conclusion: ReDex降低了人类纠正成本，能在真实交互中学习接触调节，并在不依赖触觉仿真与全手遥操作的情况下为仅本体感觉策略引入力反馈，有效提升灵巧操作的真实世界表现。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer to the real world because of errors in contact timing and force regulation. Yet these policies can retain useful multi-finger coordination for task progression. We propose ReDex, a framework for adapting a simulation-trained base policy to the real world by correcting local contact failures and incorporating tactile feedback. Starting from a proprioception-only base policy, ReDex allows a human operator to physically correct contact failures at selected fingers under compliant control during real-world rollouts, while the frozen base policy continues to control the remaining fingers. These rollouts combine base policy execution, human-corrected finger motion, and fingertip force observations. We reconstruct force-informed targets from these rollouts to train a standalone force-conditioned policy via behavior cloning. This design reduces human correction effort, enables learning of contact regulation from real-world interaction, and introduces force feedback into a proprioception-only policy without tactile simulation or complex full-hand teleoperation. We evaluate ReDex on two challenging, contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks on real hardware. Compared with sim-to-real transferred base policies, ReDex increases Object Flipping success rate from 14\% to 86\% across two objects and average Screwdriver Rotation progress from 26.0% to 95.3% across three objects.

</details>


### [170] [Task-Space Imitation Guidance for Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07527)
*Salar Asayesh,Hossein Darani,Todd Cao,Evgeny Andriash,Mani Ranjbar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TIGER 将动作分块模仿策略用作任务空间进度估计器，通过稠密进度奖励和模仿引导预训练提升稀疏奖励桌面机器人操作强化学习的效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励桌面机器人操作中，在线强化学习样本效率低、早期探索易偏离安全流形。已有 IL-RL 方法多把模仿策略当作可执行控制器或动作先验，未充分利用其进度信息。

Method: TIGER 是一种奖励构建与预训练框架。将动作分块模仿策略视为局部任务空间进度估计器，通过控制器感知的动作到运动映射，把预测动作块转换为短时域末端执行器参考；RL 智能体据此获得稠密进度奖励，同时稀疏环境奖励仍为主导目标。预训练阶段使用模仿引导的前瞻信号，对预测能带来任务空间进展的动作放松保守价值惩罚，减少早期在线 RL 的流形外探索。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中，TIGER 提升了早期样本效率，减少了实测安全违规，同时在所评估任务上匹配或优于既有 RL 与 IL-RL 基线的最终成功率。

Conclusion: 将模仿策略用作任务空间进度估计器而非控制器或动作先验，可为稀疏奖励操作任务提供有效的稠密引导与安全预训练，兼顾效率、安全与成功率。

Abstract: We introduce Task-Space Imitation Guidance for Efficient Reinforcement Learning (TIGER), a reward-construction and pretraining framework for sparse-reward tabletop robotic manipulation. TIGER treats an action-chunked imitation policy not as an executable controller or action prior, but as a local task-space progress estimator: predicted action chunks are converted, using controller-aware action-to-motion mapping, into short-horizon end-effector references, and the RL agent receives dense progress rewards toward these references while the sparse environment reward remains the dominant objective. During pretraining, TIGER uses imitation-guided look-ahead signals to relax conservative value penalties for actions predicted to make task-space progress, reducing off-manifold exploration during early online RL. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, TIGER improves early sample efficiency and reduces measured safety violations while matching or improving final success rates relative to prior RL and IL-RL baselines on the evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [171] [Seeing the Invisible: Physics-Guided Visual Prompting for Temperature- and Radiation-Aware VLA Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07558)
*Hojoon Son,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Physics-Guided Visual Prompting（PG-VP），一种即插即用的多模态感知模块，通过物理引导的风险评估生成动态虚拟障碍物，使冻结的 VLA 导航模型能够规避辐射、高温等 RGB 相机无法检测的隐形风险，且无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键设施中，辐射或温度骤升等隐形风险无法被 RGB 相机检测；而每处理一种新风险都需要新编码器、新数据和模型重训练，成本高昂。因此作者希望复用冻结 VLA 模型已具备的"规避可见障碍"能力，以低成本方式实现隐形风险规避。

Method: PG-VP 在检测到近端辐射或热源时，进行物理引导的风险评估以确定规避方向，并在连续帧上叠加一个移动的虚拟障碍物（Dynamic Visual Prompting），使导航策略自然地绕开该隐形风险。无论风险类型如何，均使用相同的虚拟障碍物，因此视觉提示模式在增加传感器时保持不变；无风险时不渲染任何内容，策略行为与无 PG-VP 时完全一致。

Result: 在 OmniNav 上使用 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 的 val-unseen 划分评估，PG-VP 在 84.9% 和 83.2% 的情况下引导策略采取预期的低风险动作，导航成功率代价为 6.8 和 7.9 个百分点。在真实机器人上针对实际热源和辐射源测试（无需任何重训练），PG-VP 有效规避了隐形风险，worst-10% 平均轨迹安全性分别提升 63.45%（热源）和 32.59%（辐射源）。

Conclusion: PG-VP 是一种无需重训练、即插即用的方案，通过动态视觉提示将隐形风险的规避问题转化为模型已擅长的可见障碍规避问题，在仿真与真实机器人场景中均验证了其有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a major paradigm for Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN). However, in safety-critical facilities, invisible risks such as radiation or temperature spikes cannot be detected by an RGB camera, and handling each risk is expensive, requiring a new encoder, new data, and model retraining. We propose Physics-Guided Visual Prompting (PG-VP), a plug-and-play multimodal perception module that instead reuses what a frozen VLA model already does well: avoiding visible obstacles. Given a proximal radiation or thermal source, PG-VP performs a physics-guided risk assessment to determine the avoidance direction and overlays a corresponding virtual obstacle that moves across consecutive frames (Dynamic Visual Prompting). The navigation policy then naturally detours around this invisible hazard. The identical virtual obstacle is used regardless of hazard type, so the visual prompting pattern remains fixed as sensors are added. When no hazard is detected, nothing is rendered, and the policy behaves exactly as it would without PG-VP. We evaluate PG-VP on OmniNav using the val-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, where it guides the policy toward intended low-risk actions in 84.9% and 83.2% of cases, at a cost of 6.8 and 7.9 percentage points in navigation success rate. We further test it with distinct scenarios on a real robot in the presence of actual thermal and radiation sources, all without any retraining. The real test shows that PG-VP effectively avoids these invisible hazards, improving worst-10% average trajectory safety by 63.45% and 32.59% against thermal and radiation sources, respectively.

</details>


### [172] [OpenSplatGraph: From Dense Semantic Maps to Structured Scene Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07569)
*Binh Long Nguyen,Kien Nguyen,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSplatGraph：从在线高斯开放词汇语义地图构建持久3D场景图，兼顾稠密几何保真与结构化关系推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密3D语义建图对复杂环境中的机器人感知至关重要。基于3D高斯泼溅的建图方法虽能实现高保真几何与高效开放词汇感知，但语义以非结构化特征场表示，限制了以对象为中心的推理；而3D场景图虽显式建模对象及关系，却常由稀疏几何表示构建，未能充分利用稠密语义地图。

Method: 提出统一框架OpenSplatGraph，直接从在线高斯开放词汇语义地图构建持久3D场景图。为稠密语义地图引入可靠性感知语义场，维护轻量观测统计，实现置信度感知、查询条件的对象提取；将提取的对象实例与持久图节点关联，使对象属性和关系能随观测与查询增量更新；将稠密语义建图与持久对象中心表示紧密耦合。

Result: 在标准3D场景理解基准和真实机器人实验上的综合评估表明，OpenSplatGraph在在线开放词汇感知与下游机器人任务上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架支持语言引导的对象定位与结构化关系推理，同时保持高斯建图的几何保真度，为机器人结构化感知提供统一方案。

Abstract: Dense 3D mapping with semantic understanding is essential for robotic perception in complex environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting-based mapping approaches enable high-fidelity geometry and efficient open-vocabulary perception, but typically represent semantics as unstructured feature fields that limit object-centric reasoning. In contrast, 3D scene graphs explicitly model objects and their relationships for structured reasoning, but are commonly constructed from sparse geometric representations that do not fully exploit dense semantic maps. In this work, we present OpenSplatGraph, a unified framework that constructs persistent 3D scene graphs directly from an online Gaussian-based open-vocabulary semantic map. The proposed framework augments the dense semantic map with a reliability-aware semantic field that maintains lightweight observation statistics for confidence-aware, query-conditioned object extraction. Extracted object instances are associated with persistent graph nodes, allowing object attributes and relationships to be incrementally updated across observations and queries. By tightly coupling dense semantic mapping with persistent object-centric representations, our framework supports both language-guided object grounding and structured relational reasoning while preserving the geometric fidelity of Gaussian-based mapping. Comprehensive evaluations on standard 3D scene understanding benchmarks and real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that OpenSplatGraph achieves competitive performance for online open-vocabulary perception and downstream robotic tasks. Project page: https://csiro-robotics.github.io/OpenSplatGraph.

</details>


### [173] [BiGym 2.0: Benchmarking Learned and Agent-Developed Policies for Humanoid Household Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07594)
*Zexi Zhang,Zecheng Zhu,Zidong Chen,Zulkhuu Tuya,Stephen James*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiGym 2.0 将 BiGym 适配到 Unitree G1 人形机器人，涵盖 20 项家务任务，并采用统一全身控制器进行演示与评估，开源了环境、人类 VR 演示与评估轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形家务操作需要手臂动作与身体平衡、迈步、姿态变化协同进行，因此需要一个统一全身控制、配套人类演示与多方法基准的评测平台。

Method: 基于 Unitree G1 适配 BiGym，使用统一全身控制器；每项任务提供 60 条原生人类 VR 演示，含同步多相机视角与全身执行记录；基准测试视觉-语言-动作微调、模仿学习、演示驱动强化学习与冷启动编码智能体。

Result: 在相同机载视角、本体感觉与全身控制器下，VLA 微调取得最高的九任务均值；智能体开发程序在该均值上优于所有演示驱动强化学习基线，并在双臂抓取上领先。跨工作区堆叠仍待解决，π0.5 在抓取盒任务上表现偏低，多物体搬运对模仿学习、演示驱动强化学习与编码智能体均困难。

Conclusion: BiGym 2.0 为人形全身家务操作提供了统一基准与人类演示数据，揭示了 VLA 微调与智能体程序的优势，同时指出跨工作区堆叠、π0.5 抓取盒、多物体搬运等开放难题。

Abstract: Humanoid household manipulation requires the arms to act while the body balances, steps and changes posture. We present BiGym 2.0, an adaptation of BiGym for the Unitree G1 across 20 household tasks using a unified whole-body controller for demonstration and evaluation. The suite provides 60 native human virtual-reality demonstrations per task with synchronised multi-camera views and full-body execution records. We benchmark vision-language-action fine-tuning, imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning, and cold-start coding agents given the interaction budget of online reinforcement learning. With the same onboard views, proprioception and whole-body controller for every method, vision-language-action fine-tuning has the highest nine-task mean, and agent-developed programs outperform every demo-driven reinforcement learning baseline on this mean and lead on bimanual reaching. Cross-workspace stacking remains open, $π_{0.5}$ stays low on pick-box, and multi-object transport is hard for imitation learning, demo-driven reinforcement learning and coding agents. All environments, human demonstrations, and evaluation traces are open-sourced at https://github.com/swirl-uk/BiGym2.

</details>


### [174] [The Robot Is Not Its Description: GaugeBench for Representation Robustness in Morphology-Aware Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07597)
*Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,Rajesh K. Gupta,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出GaugeBench基准，研究当机器人机制不变、仅等价地改写其描述约定（如关节轴方向、零位、顺序与命名）时，形态感知策略的行为是否稳健。结果显示描述变化可比重换机器人更严重地摧毁性能，但可通过跨描述传输与训练缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人描述不仅定义物理机制，还编码了关节轴方向、关节角零位、连杆与关节的顺序及命名等任意约定。形态感知策略依赖这些描述构建的接口，但现有跨形态评估通常在改变机器人的同时保持这些约定不变，从而忽略了"机制固定但描述改变"这一情形，导致表示层面的稳健性未被检验。

Method: GaugeBench在同一物理机制下，用物理等价的约定改写机器人描述，并验证物理与策略接口保持不变，再评估同一策略权重。实验对比三类设置：80个熟悉机器人、这些机器人被等价重描述、98个真正留出的机器人。进一步进行消融：单独轴反转、零位变化、重排序，以及仅改变关节状态/力矩坐标与仅改变描述派生特征。并在ModuMorph和无关的PyBullet框架上验证。最后测试精确的两描述传输与跨等价轴约定训练。

Result: 三个MetaMorph策略在80个熟悉机器人上得分4030.6，但等价重描述后仅51.6，而98个真正留出的机器人得分为1489.6——新描述比新机器人更具破坏性。单独轴反转即可复现崩塌，零位变化几乎无害，重排序介于二者之间；仅改变关节状态与力矩坐标就足以导致失败，而仅改变描述派生特征不会。该现象在ModuMorph和PyBullet中同样出现。精确两描述传输可恢复原控制器；跨等价轴约定训练使轴反转下的保留回报从3.6%提升至80.6%。

Conclusion: 研究区分了机制稳健性与表示稳健性：跨形态评估必须同时测试两者。描述层面的等价变换是策略失效的重要来源，但可通过描述传输与跨约定训练加以缓解。

Abstract: A robot description does more than specify a physical mechanism: it also encodes arbitrary conventions, such as joint-axis direction, joint-angle zero, and the order and names of links and joints. Morphology-aware policies consume interfaces built from these descriptions, yet cross-embodiment evaluation typically changes the robot while keeping those conventions fixed. This leaves a simple question unanswered: does behavior survive when the robot stays fixed but its description changes? GaugeBench isolates this case by rewriting a fixed mechanism under physically equivalent conventions, verifying that its physics and policy interface are preserved, and then evaluating the same policy weights. The result is stark: three MetaMorph policies score 4030.6 on 80 familiar robots, but only 51.6 when those same robots are equivalently re-described, while 98 genuinely held-out robots score 1489.6. A new description can therefore be more damaging than a new robot. Tracing the failure reveals that axis reversal alone reproduces the collapse, joint-angle zero changes are nearly harmless, and reordering lies between them; moreover, changing joint-state and torque coordinates alone is sufficient to cause the failure, while changing description-derived features alone is not. The same phenomenon appears in ModuMorph and an unrelated PyBullet framework.
