The models are identified using one subset of experiments and validated using the other on a motor-drive-propeller unit. Independent estimates of rotational inertia and aerodynamic drag complement predictive validation by assessing whether the models correctly attribute the measured power to reversible kinetic-energy exchange and irreversible dissipation and, within the latter, to aerodynamic and electromechanical losses. The results show that the reversible kinetic-energy rate is necessary but insufficient for accurate dynamic power prediction. Dissipation proportional to the squared motor torque provides the main additional improvement, while speed-proportional dissipation further prevents irreversible losses from being attributed to reversible kinetic-energy exchange. The resulting methodology provides a reusable and experimentally verifiable basis for developing and selecting dynamic propulsion-power models for multirotor systems.

</details>


### [210] [Compact Robot Policies Need Fine-Grained Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08183)
*Nanhe Chen,Runqiu Yang,Jiawei Tang,Sichao Liu,Yuquan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出紧凑策略 CoRP（4890万参数，无VLM、无视频生成先验），在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上匹配大40–164倍的模型，并论证多任务操作策略的性能主要来自视觉表征而非参数规模或生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务操作策略在架构、规模和预训练先验上同时存在差异，导致已发表对比无法将性能归因于任何单一组件。作者主张性能主要来自视觉表征，而参数规模与生成先验基本是次要因素，需要受控实验来验证。

Method: 构建紧凑策略 CoRP，分解为表征提取器与流匹配动作生成器；固定动作生成器，逐一改变提取器属性进行消融：预训练初始化（随机ViT-S/14、ImageNet ResNet-34）、是否冻结编码器、压缩方式（每视图重采样至48个token vs 全部patch token）、变分信息瓶颈 vs 硬token预算、以及语言条件的贡献。

Result: CoRP在LIBERO达97.0%，RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized达75.78%/73.36%，匹配大40.9–163.6倍的系统。预训练初始化决定性：随机ViT-S/14降至78.1%，ImageNet ResNet-34降至74.5%；冻结编码器损失19.8分。压缩同样关键：48 token（97.0%）优于全部patch token（83.2%）；变分信息瓶颈使LIBERO-Goal从95.8%降至33.0%。语言条件仅在目标模糊时有贡献（LIBERO-Goal从9.2%升至95.8%），在观测无歧义的RoboTwin 2.0上移除反而略优。

Conclusion: 紧凑策略有效的关键在于其表征同时满足预训练、任务适配与压缩三个条件；性能主要归因于视觉表征，而非参数规模或生成先验。

Abstract: Multi-task manipulation policies differ in architecture, scale, and pretrained priors all at once, so published comparisons cannot attribute performance to any single component. We argue that most of it comes from the visual representation, and that parameter scale and generative priors are largely incidental. To test this, we build CoRP (Compressed Representation Policy), a deliberately compact policy (48.9M parameters, no vision-language model and no video-generative prior) that factorizes into a representation extractor and a flow-matching action generator. It reaches 97.0% on LIBERO and 75.78%/73.36% on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Randomized, matching systems 40.9-163.6x larger. Holding the action generator fixed, we then vary one extractor property at a time. Pretrained initialization is decisive: a random ViT-S/14 drops to 78.1% and an ImageNet ResNet-34 to 74.5% on LIBERO. Pretraining alone is not enough, as freezing the encoder costs 19.8 points. Compression matters as much: resampling each view to 48 tokens beats passing all patch tokens (97.0% vs 83.2%), and a variational information bottleneck over those tokens is worse than a hard token budget, cutting LIBERO-Goal from 95.8% to 33.0% by suppressing the instruction-dependent token selection the policy relies on. Language conditioning contributes only where the observation leaves the goal ambiguous (LIBERO-Goal: 9.2% to 95.8%), while on RoboTwin 2.0, where observations are unambiguous, removing it slightly improves success. Therefore, we argue that a compact policy works when its representation is pretrained, task-adapted, and compressed. Project page: https://corp-policy.github.io/

</details>


### [211] [VOMMI: Collecting and Leveraging Portable Demonstrations for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08220)
*Yutian Zhang,Xingrui Xiong,Siyuan Ma,Yang Li,Jiawen Wen,Jiaqi Zhai,Liwen Yang,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Yangkun Zhu,Yifan Zhu,Xiaowen Chu,Dong Wei,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOMMI 提出了一种便携式 RGB 演示采集与学习框架，通过离线轨迹重建和在线视觉-运动条件化，将便携演示连接到 VLA 后训练，无需额外传感硬件或遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作演示数据稀缺，而现有方法依赖遥操作或专用设备，直接用估计的视觉里程计轨迹又会因累积漂移和运动监督不完善而引入不一致。

Method: VOMMI 同步身体与手部视角以捕捉导航上下文与局部物体交互，无需人机运动学校准；R2-VO 利用稀疏几何锚点精修离线演示轨迹，并生成多预测时域上的因果局部运动 token；动作组残差适配器仅将这些 token 注入基础分支。

Result: 仅用便携演示后训练的策略相比用机器人采集演示训练的策略，基座速度误差降低 18.2%，末端平移精度相当；离线重建使身体与手部流的绝对轨迹误差平均降低 24.6%；在三个真实机器人任务上成功率较 OpenPI 0.5 平均提升 8.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: VOMMI 证明了便携、无机器人、低成本的 RGB 演示结合轨迹重建与视觉-运动条件化，可有效支持 VLA 后训练并提升真实机器人任务表现。

Abstract: Portable mobile-manipulation demonstrations can help alleviate data scarcity for embodied intelligence, but obtaining reliable, low-cost, and robot-free motion supervision from RGB observations remains challenging. Existing approaches often rely on teleoperation or specialized devices equipped with additional sensing hardware, while directly using estimated visual odometry (VO) trajectories can introduce inconsistencies due to accumulated drift and imperfect motion supervision. We present the Visual-Odometry-Conditioned Mobile Manipulation Interface (VOMMI), a portable demonstration collection and learning framework that connects portable RGB demonstrations to vision-language-action (VLA) post-training through offline trajectory reconstruction and online visual-motion conditioning. VOMMI synchronizes body and hand views to capture navigation context and local object interactions without requiring human-robot kinematic correspondence calibration. R2-VO refines offline demonstration trajectories using sparse geometric anchors and produces causal local-motion tokens over multiple prediction horizons for online policy conditioning. An action-group residual adapter incorporates these tokens only into the base branch. Experiments use a 500-trajectory portable for each task, with 75 trajectories held out for RGB-VO evaluation, and 200 robot demonstrations as references. Our policy, post-trained only on portable demonstrations, achieves 18.2% lower base-velocity error than a policy trained with robot-collected demonstrations, while maintaining comparable end-effector translation accuracy. Offline reconstruction reduces absolute trajectory errors for the body and hand streams by 24.6% on average relative to the best evaluated baseline for each stream. The complete system improves the mean success rate by 8.3 percentage points over OpenPI 0.5 across three real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [212] [UWB Meets Crazyflow: Simulating Degraded Feedback at Scale for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08225)
*Martin Schuck,Marcel P. Rath,Yufei Hua,Abhishek Goudar,SiQi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Crazyflow 是一个基于 JAX 的高精度可微分仿真器，通过统一物理与控制计算图实现大规模并行加速，可将可部署强化学习智能体的训练时间缩短至数秒，并支持模块化扩展（如 UWB/IMU 与 EKF 仿真）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器在速度与建模精度之间难以兼顾，且缺乏高效的可微分与并行能力，限制了空中机器人研究中强化学习训练与真实状态反馈下的控制器评估效率。

Method: 基于 JAX 构建可微分仿真器 Crazyflow，利用 XLA 的 jit 编译将物理与控制统一为单一可微分计算图，在加速硬件上实现大规模并行化；并采用高度模块化设计，集成完整的 UWB 与 IMU 高保真仿真管线及全状态扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）。

Result: 相比现有基线实现数量级的速度提升，可在数秒内训练出可部署的强化学习智能体；集成 UWB/IMU 与 EKF 后，可在真实退化状态反馈下进行大规模并行控制器评估，且对 GPU 吞吐量影响极小。

Conclusion: Crazyflow 兼具速度、精度与可扩展性，可作为下一代空中机器人研究的基础工具。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Crazyflow, an accurate, differentiable simulator built on JAX. By leveraging jit compilation via XLA, Crazyflow unifies physics and control into a single differentiable computation graph, enabling massive parallelization on accelerated hardware without sacrificing modeling accuracy. This architecture achieves order-of-magnitude speedups over existing baselines, capable of training deployable reinforcement learning agents in seconds. To highlight its highly modular design, we demonstrate how easily Crazyflow can be extended by integrating a complete, high-fidelity Ultra-Wideband (UWB) and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) simulation pipeline coupled with a full-state Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). This capability allows for massive parallel controller evaluation under realistic, degraded state feedback with minimal impact on GPU throughput. By combining speed, accuracy, and extensibility, Crazyflow serves as a foundational tool for the next generation of aerial robotics research.

</details>


### [213] [HexaGripper: A Single-Actuator, Winch-Deployed Gripper for Autonomous Aerial Parcel Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08259)
*Nimantha Adikaram,Mahen Abeyratne,Lakmina Chandrajith,A. H. T. E. De Silva,Isira Naotunna,Asanka Perera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HexaGripper——一种单驱动、绞盘下放的六板夹爪，用于无人机自主采集长方体包裹，无需着陆或地面设施。实验表明静态抓取成功率100%（15/15）、工作空间内28个测试位置全部成功（径向偏移达150mm）、户外端到端采集成功率80%（4/5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中包裹采集受限于需要人工装载、着陆或专用地面基础设施的包裹转运操作，现有方式难以实现无需着陆的全自主采集。

Method: 设计单执行器、绞盘下放的六板夹爪机构，实现同步抓取并具备较大捕获区域与位置偏差容忍能力；系统集成视觉对齐、测距感知、绞盘下放与基于状态的控制，在保持无人机与拾取表面分离的前提下完成自主采集。通过静态抓取、捕获工作空间评估与户外端到端采集实验进行验证。

Result: 静态实验在三种包裹几何形状下取得100%拾取成功率（15/15次）；工作空间实验在28个测试位置全部成功拾取，径向偏移最大达150 mm；户外端到端空中采集成功率为80%（4/5次）。

Conclusion: 结果验证了机械同步、绞盘下放式夹爪用于免着陆自主空中包裹采集的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial parcel collection remains constrained by package-transfer operations that require manual loading, landing, or dedicated ground infrastructure. This letter presents HexaGripper, a single-actuator, winch-deployed six-plate gripper for autonomous collection of cuboid parcels. The proposed mechanism provides synchronized grasping with a wide capture region and tolerance to positional misalignment during pickup. The system integrates vision-based alignment, range sensing, winch deployment, and state-based control to enable autonomous collection while maintaining UAV separation from the pickup surface. Experimental evaluation covered static grasping, capture-workspace assessment, and end-to-end outdoor aerial collection. Static experiments achieved 100% pickup success (15/15 trials) across three parcel geometries, while workspace experiments achieved successful pickup at all 28 tested positions up to a 150 mm radial offset. End-to-end outdoor aerial collection achieved an 80% success rate (4/5 trials). These results demonstrate the feasibility of mechanically synchronized, winch-deployed grasping for landing-free autonomous aerial parcel collection.

</details>


### [214] [Mitigating Concept Drift in QoS Prediction for Teleoperation of Autonomous Vehicles Using Historic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08297)
*Xiyan Su,Jianning Gao,Mahmoud Ashri,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于数据测量活动，提出一个预测框架来预测远程驾驶的两个关键网络KPI：上行数据速率和往返时延；并通过将历史数据纳入预测流程来缓解概念漂移导致的模型性能下降，同时引入关键场景检测指标来评估预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶是自动驾驶的后备方案，但其可靠运行需要一定量的移动网络资源，而这些资源无法在所有时刻都得到保证。因此引入预测性服务质量（pQoS）概念以提升远程驾驶的韧性，论文旨在为其提供网络KPI预测能力。

Method: 基于一次数据测量活动，提出预测框架，用于预测远程驾驶的两个关键网络KPI——上行数据速率和往返时延；并引入一种方法，通过将历史数据纳入预测流程，来缓解基于机器学习的预测模型在面对未见过的数据时因概念漂移而产生的性能退化。

Result: 实现了对上行数据速率与往返时延两个关键KPI的预测，并通过融入历史数据缓解了概念漂移带来的性能下降；同时提出关键场景检测指标，可针对远程驾驶场景专门评估预测性能。

Conclusion: 该预测框架与关键场景检测指标有助于提升远程驾驶在移动网络资源不稳定条件下的可靠性与韧性，为pQoS在远程驾驶中的应用提供了支撑。

Abstract: Teleoperation serves as the fallback solution to autonomous driving but reliable functions of the teleoperation require a certain amount of mobile network resources, which cannot be guaranteed at all times. Therefore, predictive quality of service (pQoS) is introduced as a concept to increase the resilience of the teleoperation. In this paper, based on a data measurement campaign, we propose a prediction framework to prediction two important network KPIs of teleoperation: uplink data-rate and round-trip latency. Furthermore, we introduce a method to alleviate the performance degradation of machine-learning-based prediction models on previously unseen data due to concept drift by incorporating historic data into the prediction pipeline. Additionally, we introduce the metric of critical scenario detection to evaluate the prediction performance specifically for teleoperation.

</details>


### [215] [Sensor-Layout-Agnostic Navigation via Geometric Observation Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08306)
*Welf Rehberg,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具身感知导航策略，通过将任意深度传感器布局显式投影到机器人中心球面距离图像与有效性掩码，实现跨相机配置的零样本泛化，成功率随覆盖度从78%升至95%，并在真实四旋翼上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航策略与固定相机配置绑定，难以在不同机器人传感器布局间零样本部署，这是一个根本性障碍。作者希望让导航策略能泛化到同一空中平台上多样化的深度传感器配置。

Method: 不隐式学习空间对齐，而是显式地将任意深度传感器载荷（传感器数量、安装外参、内参各异）的深度测量反投影到共享的机器人中心坐标系，拼接成统一的球面距离图像和二元有效性掩码，使下游策略能区分已覆盖空间与未观测盲区。通过强化学习并配合激进的相机随机化进行训练。

Result: 策略可零样本泛化到最多七相机的未见布局，成功率随总空间感知覆盖度提升从78%增至95%。真实四旋翼在障碍走廊与户外森林的飞行试验验证了跨相机配置的零样本迁移能力及对在线传感器突然丢失的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式的机器人中心球面表示加有效性掩码，是解耦导航策略与具体相机配置的有效途径，可实现跨传感器布局的零样本泛化并适应传感器故障。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation policies are inherently bound to fixed camera configurations, creating a fundamental barrier to zero-shot deployment across heterogeneous robot sensor layouts. To overcome this limitation, we present an embodiment-informed navigation policy capable of generalizing across diverse depth sensor configurations on a specific aerial platform. Instead of implicitly learning spatial alignments, our approach explicitly unprojects depth measurements from arbitrary depth sensor payloads, varying in sensor count, mounting extrinsics, and intrinsics, into a shared robot-centric frame, stitching them into a unified spherical range image and a binary validity mask. This mask allows the downstream policy to explicitly distinguish covered space from unobserved blind spots. Trained via reinforcement learning with aggressive camera randomization, our policy generalizes zero-shot to unseen layouts featuring up to seven cameras, scaling success rates from 78% to 95% as total spatial sensing coverage increases. Finally, real-world flight trials on a physical quadrotor, conducted in an obstacle-filled corridor and an outdoor forest, validate the policy's zero-shot transfer across camera configurations and its resilience to sudden online sensor dropouts.

</details>


### [216] [Humanoid Horizon: Extending Task Horizon in Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation via Parallel Training, Dynamic Starting, and Reward Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08320)
*Haozhuo Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Jian Tang,Mingzhe Ni,Michele Caprio,Angelo Cangelosi,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Humanoid Horizon 统一策略框架，通过并行训练、动态起始与奖励门控三项机制，解决长时程全身移动操作中的易奖励偏差与灾难性遗忘问题，在两物体基准上各阶段成功率超 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内杂乱环境中，人形机器人需在单一不间断回合内完成导航、抓取、搬运与精确放置的长时程全身移动操作任务。现有方法存在两大问题：一是易奖励偏差，训练过度侧重早期搬运阶段而忽视后期阶段；二是灾难性遗忘，专注后期阶段会导致早期阶段性能下降。

Method: 框架包含三个相互关联的机制：1) 并行训练策略，将 N 个场景组织为 S 个并发的阶段流，由共享策略统一调控，确保所有搬运阶段都能获得持续梯度更新，消除顺序优化的瓶颈；2) 动态起始机制，用上游 rollout 的终止状态更新各环境的初始状态，逐步扩大状态转移覆盖范围，增强阶段边界的鲁棒性；3) 奖励门控，当紧邻的前一个物体被移动超过设定阈值时，将后期阶段流中该回合剩余奖励置零，使共享策略学会不干扰刚放置好的物体，从而在整段回合中保留早期放置成果。

Result: 在两物体 LHM-Humanoid 基准（350 个训练场景、66 个留出场景）上，各阶段成功率均超过 80%。当顺序搬运的物体数量超过两个时，成功率随任务时程增加而下降，但相较于所有基线方法的急剧下滑，其性能退化较为平缓。

Conclusion: Humanoid Horizon 通过并行训练、动态起始与奖励门控的协同设计，有效缓解了长时程移动操作中的易奖励偏差与灾难性遗忘，在多物体长时程任务中展现出更优的可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cluttered indoor environments, where large and heavy objects are scattered across diverse surfaces, require humanoid robots to sequentially navigate, grasp, transport, and accurately place each item at its target location within a single uninterrupted episode. This long-horizon, whole-body loco-manipulation task remains a significant challenge for current methods. Previous approaches often suffer from two main issues: easy-reward bias, where training overemphasizes early transport stages at the expense of later ones, and catastrophic forgetting, where focusing on later stages leads to a decline in earlier-stage performance. In this work, we introduce Humanoid Horizon, a unified policy framework designed to overcome these limitations through three interrelated mechanisms. The Parallel Training Strategy organizes $N$ scenes into $S$ concurrent stage streams governed by a shared policy, ensuring all transport stages receive continuous gradient updates and removing the bottleneck of sequential optimization. The Dynamic Starting Mechanism updates each environment's initial state with terminal states from upstream rollouts, gradually broadening transition coverage and enhancing robustness at stage boundaries. Reward Gating sets the reward to zero for the rest of the episode in later-stage streams when the immediately preceding object is displaced beyond a set threshold, so the shared policy learns not to disturb a just-placed object and earlier placements are preserved throughout the episode. Collectively, these strategies achieve per-stage success rates exceeding 80\% on the two-object LHM-Humanoid benchmark (350 training scenes, 66 held-out scenes). As the number of sequentially transported objects grows beyond two, success declines with the horizon, but the degradation is graceful relative to the sharp drop seen in all baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Communication-Free Obstacle Localization from Aggregate Wrench Measurements in Leader--Follower Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08324)
*Amin Kashiri,Aditya Mohan,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无机器人间显式通信条件下，多机器人协同搬运刚性负载时的障碍物定位问题：领导者通过测量跟随者的合力/合力矩，借助分段线性关系与自适应探测，恢复障碍物的方位与距离并识别响应的跟随者，仿真验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同搬运刚性负载时，跟随者会局部感知并反应障碍物，但领导者无法直接获知其个体反应，且系统不依赖机器人间显式通信，因此需要一种仅凭领导者可测的聚合力/力矩信息来定位障碍物的方法。

Method: 设计跟随者控制律：每个跟随者抵抗朝附近障碍物的运动，使负载的平移与角速度相对于聚合力/力矩呈现分段线性关系；相邻线性区域的变化揭示障碍物方位、距离并识别响应的跟随者。给出固定负载构型下精确恢复的充分条件，并设计自适应探测流程，由领导者施加平移与旋转输入以获取所需测量。

Result: 在固定负载构型下给出了精确恢复障碍物的充分条件；自适应探测流程能够获取所需测量；仿真展示了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 无需机器人间显式通信，领导者可仅凭聚合力/力矩测量实现障碍物定位，并通过自适应探测完成恢复，仿真结果验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: We consider obstacle localization for a team of robots cooperatively transporting a rigid payload without explicit inter-robot communication. A leader robot directs the payload's motion, while follower robots assist and react to locally detected obstacles. The leader measures the followers' aggregate wrench, i.e., the combined force and torque they exert on the payload, but cannot directly distinguish their individual reactions. We design a follower control law that allows the leader to recover obstacle locations from these measurements. Each follower resists motion toward nearby obstacles, resulting in a piecewise-linear relationship between the payload's translational and angular velocity and the aggregate wrench. Changes between adjacent linear regions reveal an obstacle's bearing and distance and identify the responding follower. We give sufficient conditions for exact recovery at a fixed payload configuration and develop an adaptive probing procedure in which the leader applies translational and rotational inputs to the payload to obtain the required measurements. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed method in simulations.

</details>


### [218] [SC3BF: Shifted Collision Cone Control Barrier Function for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08332)
*Amin Kashiri,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移位碰撞锥CBF（SC3BF），通过在锥条件中加入随状态变化的“允许量”，使机器人可按距离和自身速度增长的速率接近障碍物，从而在保证安全的同时减少保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速度空间控制障碍函数所用的碰撞锥较为保守：任何指向障碍物的相对速度都会被拒绝，无论其多慢，这限制了机器人的可行运动并造成不必要的控制修正。

Method: 在碰撞锥条件中加入状态相关的允许量，使接近速率可随距离和机器人自身速度增长；通过普通二次规划（QP）施加SC3BF约束；证明存在保持安全的非零允许量并给出闭式表达；在运动学自行车模型上，与三种速度空间基线方法对比，测试最多100个移动障碍物场景。

Result: SC3BF的安全集在有界输入下前向不变，无需最小前进速度或安全间隙裕度；可证明始终存在保持安全的非零允许量并给出闭式解；实验中到达目标更频繁，且对名义输入的修改量不到基线的一半。

Conclusion: SC3BF通过引入状态依赖允许量缓解碰撞锥的过度保守性，在保持安全性的同时显著提升任务成功率并减少控制修正，且可由标准QP高效实现。

Abstract: The collision cone used by velocity-space control barrier functions is conservative: it rejects every relative velocity aimed into an obstacle, however slow. We propose the \emph{shifted collision-cone CBF} (SC3BF), which adds a state-dependent \emph{allowance} to the cone condition, so the robot may approach the obstacle at a rate that grows with distance and with its own speed. SC3BF is enforced by an ordinary quadratic program, and its safe set is forward invariant under bounded inputs without a minimum forward speed or a clearance margin. We prove that a nonzero allowance preserving safety always exists, and derive one in closed form. Against three velocity-space baselines on a kinematic bicycle among up to $100$ moving obstacles, SC3BF reaches the goal more often and modifies the nominal input less than half as much.

</details>


### [219] [Event-Driven Proactive Robot Assistance through Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08344)
*Fengkai Liu,Hao Su,Haozhuang Chi,Rui Geng,Congzhi Ren,Xuqing Liu,Chenfei Xu,Yuichi Ohsita,Liyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"事件驱动"的主动协助框架：不依赖用户指令，而是通过监测人—物交互的结果来触发协助推理，利用冻结的预训练VLM推断任务上下文、判断是否需要协助并生成动作序列，在三个真实桌面协作任务上达到与给定用户指令版本相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作中的协助通常由用户指令触发，属于"请求驱动"的高层推理。但流畅的人类团队协作中，伙伴往往是从观察到的动作结果推断下一步有帮助的行为，而非等待指令。受此启发，作者希望研究一种事件驱动的主动协助范式，让交互结果本身触发协助推理，且推理时不依赖用户给出的任务说明。

Method: 提出事件驱动框架：用事件监视器监测工作空间状态变化，在事件完成时提取稳定的"前/后"快照以刻画状态转换；使用冻结的预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）借助其语义先验推断任务上下文、判断协助是否合适，并在需要时基于观测到的转换生成一串协助动作。为使输出可执行、可验证，动作被限制为一组动作原语，并用整数ID引用物体。

Result: 在三个不同的真实世界桌面协作任务上评估同一框架，无需任务特定的训练或微调；事件驱动框架取得了与给定用户指令的变体相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于人—物交互结果的主动协助可以在推理阶段不依赖用户任务说明的情况下实现，且借助冻结VLM的语义先验、动作原语与ID引用，无需任务特定训练即可跨多个真实协作任务达到与指令驱动方案相当的效果。

Abstract: Assistance in collaborative manipulation is often initiated by user instructions, making high-level reasoning request-driven. In fluent human teamwork, however, partners often infer the next helpful step from the observed outcome of an action rather than waiting for instructions. Motivated by this, we investigate an event-driven formulation of proactive assistance, where human--object interaction outcomes initiate assistive reasoning without user-provided task specifications at inference time. To this end, we propose an event-driven framework that monitors workspace state changes with an event monitor and, upon event completion, extracts stabilized pre/post snapshots that characterize the resulting state transition. A frozen pretrained Vision-Language Model (VLM) then uses its semantic priors to infer the task context, decide whether assistance is appropriate, and, when needed, generate a sequence of assistive actions from the observed transition. To make outputs executable and verifiable, we restrict actions to a set of action primitives and reference objects via integer IDs.We evaluate the same framework across three distinct real world tabletop collaboration tasks without task-specific training or fine-tuning. The event-driven framework achieves performance comparable to variants given user instructions.

</details>


### [220] [How Much Planning Is Enough? Reducing Search and Computation in World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08350)
*Changbai Li,Sirui Li,Yichen Yang,Tongfei Chen,Zichao Feng,Shuwei Shao,Huobin Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SufficientPlan部署框架，通过配对式序贯预算认证（PSBC）和静态上下文复用（SCR）降低视觉世界模型在决策时动作搜索中的规划预算与延迟，同时保持有竞争力的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型通过决策时动作搜索实现目标导向控制，但其部署效率常受限于过于保守的大规划预算。论文发现：无需与全预算动作一致也能达到有竞争力的任务表现；充分预算因模型-任务组合而异；迭代式规划器会反复编码与求解无关的上下文。这些低效促使作者寻找更高效的部署方案。

Method: SufficientPlan框架无需修改预训练世界模型或规划器更新。其PSBC组件利用配对闭环证据，在预设的全性能容差范围内搜索并认证一个缩减的、针对模型-任务组合的预算；其SCR组件在搜索迭代间缓存观测与目标表征，同时保留候选相关的规划过程与所选动作。

Result: 在多种世界模型骨干与视觉控制任务上的实验表明，SufficientPlan显著降低了搜索预算与规划延迟，同时维持了有竞争力的控制性能。

Conclusion: 通过预算认证与上下文复用，可在不修改模型的前提下有效提升世界模型规划部署效率，为视觉世界模型的高效控制提供了一种简单可用的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual world models enable goal-directed control through decision-time action search, but their deployment efficiency is often limited by conservatively large planning budgets. We show that competitive task performance can be achieved without agreement with the Full-budget action, that sufficient budgets vary across model--task pairs, and that iterative planners repeatedly encode solve-invariant context. To address these inefficiencies, we propose {SufficientPlan}, a simple deployment framework that requires no modification to pretrained world models or planner updates. Its {Paired Sequential Budget Certification (PSBC)} component uses paired closed-loop evidence to search for and certify a reduced model--task-specific budget within a predefined Full-performance tolerance. Its {Static-Context Reuse (SCR)} component caches observation and goal representations across search iterations while preserving candidate-dependent planning and selected actions. Experiments across multiple world-model backbones and visual-control tasks show that SufficientPlan substantially reduces search budgets and planning latency while maintaining competitive control performance.

</details>


### [221] [From Legs to Wheels: Embodiment-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Mobile-Base Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08381)
*Jiyeon Koo,Eunseom Pyo,Jeonghee Seo,Taehwa Kim,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种任务条件化的框架，将重建的人体动作分配给轮式移动人形机器人的底座、升降和双臂，实现从人到机器人的动作重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是机器人演示的可扩展来源，但现有人到人形机器人的重定向方法大多假设机器人具有类人的腿部运动学。这一假设对配有轮式底座、垂直升降和双臂的移动底座人形机器人不成立：人的行走需要表达为底座运动，躯干弯曲可能需要升降与手臂的协同运动。因此需要解决这一不匹配问题。

Method: 提出任务条件化的分配框架，将重建的人体动作分配到底座、升降和手臂的职责上，再进行机器人特定实现。分配器保留人体路径、稳定朝向、必要时分离转向与平移、对指令重定时以满足底座限制，并修复升降和手臂轨迹。随后由部署适配器通过静止底座检测、死区与摆率滤波、时间一致的播放缩放，以及分离的线性与角增益，将参考转换为50 Hz指令。

Result: 通过基于人体派生的任务空间对比、无策略的仿真回放，以及在物理机器人上的定性执行来评估生成的参考。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够处理轮式移动人形机器人与人体的运动学不匹配问题，生成可部署的参考动作，具备实际机器人执行的可行性。

Abstract: Human video offers a scalable source of robot demonstrations, yet most human-to-humanoid retargeting methods assume a legged robot with human-like kinematics. This assumption does not hold for mobile-base humanoids equipped with a wheeled base, vertical lift, and two arms. Human walking must be expressed through base motion, while torso bending may require coordinated lift and arm motion. We address this mismatch with a task-conditioned framework that assigns reconstructed human motion to base, lift, and arm responsibilities before robot-specific realization. The allocator preserves the human-derived path, stabilizes heading, separates turn and translation when needed, retimes commands to satisfy base limits, and repairs lift and arm trajectories. A deployment adapter then converts the reference to 50 Hz commands using stationary-base detection, deadband and slew-rate filtering, time-consistent playback scaling, and separate linear and angular gains. We evaluate the resulting references with human-derived task-space comparisons, policy-free simulation replay, and a qualitative execution on a physical robot.

</details>


### [222] [Post-Grasp Kinematic Repair for Robotic Insertion via Object-in-Gripper Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08421)
*Haegu Lee,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究抓取后通过物体在夹爪中的再定向来修复运动学可行性，提出基于分支感知IK图和任务条件学习先验的方法，并用触觉外在操作执行，在仿真中显著降低了再定向幅度和规划时间，并通过真机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定抓取不能保证机器人插入操作在运动学上可行，因为物体在夹爪中的位姿可能使机器人在预设插入路径上接近奇异点或关节极限。顺序IK可能因沿不利的关节空间路径搜索而错过可行解，同时可行性景观的非光滑性使搜索计算代价高。

Method: 给定已完成的抓取和固定插入路径，寻找一个小的物体在夹爪中的再定向以恢复运动学可行性。使用分支感知的IK图评估候选解，最大化离散插入路径上的最小可行性裕度，并引入任务条件的学习先验来改进查询顺序；所选再定向通过基于触觉的外在操作执行。

Result: 在UR5e仿真中，不使用学习排序的规划器相比基于网格的顺序IK，将成功试验中的平均再定向幅度降低51.5%；加入学习排序后，该规划器的平均规划时间进一步降低51.1%。真机实验验证了完整流程。

Conclusion: 通过物体在夹爪中的再定向可以有效修复抓取后的运动学可行性，分支感知IK图与学习排序能够提升规划质量与效率，触觉外在操作为该修复策略提供了可行的执行方式。

Abstract: A stable grasp does not guarantee kinematically feasible robotic insertion because the object-in-gripper transform may force the robot towards singularities or joint limits along the prescribed insertion path. We study post-grasp kinematic feasibility repair through object-in-gripper reorientation. Given an achieved grasp and a fixed insertion path, we seek a small reorientation that restores kinematic feasibility. Sequential IK can miss such candidates by following an unfavorable joint-space path, while the nonsmooth feasibility landscape makes the search computationally expensive. We evaluate candidates using a branch-aware IK graph that maximizes the minimum feasibility margin over the discretized insertion path and use a learned task-conditioned prior to improve query ordering. The selected reorientation is executed through tactile-based extrinsic manipulation. In UR5e simulations, the planner without learned ranking reduces mean reorientation over successful trials by 51.5% compared with grid-based sequential IK. Adding learned ranking reduces this planner's mean planning time by an additional 51.1%. Real-robot experiments validate the complete pipeline.

</details>


### [223] [MIM-VLA: Learning Physical Interaction Representations from Gripper Motor Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08425)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Jiyeon Koo,Taehwa Kim,Yerin Cha,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MIM-VLA，用电机反馈（电流、位置、速度等）编码交互token，只调节夹爪动作通路，实现物体阻力比较、真假物体区分与脆弱物体轻柔抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略主要依赖视觉观测与机器人状态推断抓取动作，未显式表征接触后产生的物理响应，导致难以判断物体阻力、材质差异及脆弱性。

Method: 将最近的夹爪电流、位置、速度与信号有效性编码为128维交互token；用人工标注的接触与交互阶段标签预训练仅基于电机的Motor Interaction Module (MIM)，然后仅条件化SmolVLA的夹爪动作通路（手臂动作与位置控制接口不变）；同一token还支持MEM选择器VLM，用于比较候选交互并给出基于证据的选择与解释。

Result: 在13组物体配对中，MIM-VLA以75.0%的比例选择阻力更大的物体，而SmolVLA基线为48.8%；在视觉相似的真假物体主动探测及脆弱物体（含留出实例）轻柔抓取任务上也进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该方法利用夹爪已有的电机反馈即可实现接触后物理响应感知，无需额外触觉阵列、力/力矩传感器、标定力估计或直接电流控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies infer grasp actions primarily from visual observations and robot state, but do not explicitly represent the physical response observed after contact. We present MIM-VLA, a motor-feedback-based architecture that encodes recent gripper current, position, velocity, and signal validity as a 128-dimensional interaction token. A motor-only Motor Interaction Module (MIM) is pretrained with human-reviewed contact and interaction-phase labels and then conditions only the gripper-action pathway of SmolVLA; arm actions and the position-control interface remain unchanged. The same token supports the MEM selector VLM that compares candidate interactions and produces evidence-conditioned selections and explanations. We evaluate MIM-VLA in three real-world settings: comparing the interaction resistance of visually different objects, disambiguating visually similar real and replica objects through active probing, and gently grasping fragile objects, including held-out instances. Across 13 object pairs, MIM-VLA selects the higher-resistance object in 75.0% of trials, compared with 48.8% for the SmolVLA baseline. For the evaluated tasks, the approach uses motor feedback already available from the gripper and does not require an additional tactile array, force-torque sensor, calibrated force estimate, or direct current control.

</details>


### [224] [Safe Multi-Robot Collaborative Transport Using Density Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08441)
*Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Sriram S. K. S. Narayanan,Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层基于密度的MPC框架，实现多四足机器人安全协同推物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多四足机器人协同操作需要安全且无需预计算参考轨迹的方案，仅凭初始与目标位姿即完成推物任务。

Method: 集中式box-level MPC优化接触力并施加control-density约束以保证收敛与避障；每台机器人再运行分布式robot-level全身MPC，在共享信息假设下跟踪移动接触点，并考虑静态障碍与邻居时变位置。

Result: 在MuJoCo中采用全身接触动力学，用2台和3台Unitree Go2穿越狭窄通道协同推动刚体；与匹配的CBF和RRT*跟踪基线对比，验证了密度式表述在仅推、力与力矩耦合任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于密度的分层MPC可有效支持多足机器人安全协同推物操作。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical density-based model predictive control framework for safe collaborative manipulation by multiple quadrupedal robots. The framework enables a team of robots to push a shared object to a desired pose using only the initial and goal poses, without requiring a precomputed reference trajectory. A centralized box-level MPC optimizes contact forces while enforcing a control-density constraint for goal convergence and obstacle avoidance. Each robot then solves its own distributed robot-level whole-body MPC, under a stated shared-information assumption, to track its moving contact location while accounting for static obstacles and the time-varying positions of neighboring robots. The approach is evaluated in MuJoCo using whole-body contact dynamics for two and three Unitree Go2 quadrupeds collaboratively pushing rigid objects through narrow passages. Comparisons with matched Control Barrier Function and RRT* based tracking baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed density-based formulation for push-only, force- and torque-coupled manipulation tasks. Implementation videos are available at https://jaggu2606.github.io/go2-density-mpc-pushing/

</details>


### [225] [ActTune: Action-Aware Precision and GPU Operating-Point Adaptation for Energy-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08444)
*Zou Qingyun,Bin Gao,Wenju Zhao,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ActTune，通过动作感知的层级精度分配与GPU工作点选择，在保持任务成功率和延迟约束下大幅降低每成功任务的GPU能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略反复推理控制机器人，GPU能耗是任务执行的重复成本；但单纯降低单次推理能耗未必降低每成功任务能耗，因为数值误差可能增加失败、推理变慢可能延长执行时间。

Method: 基于两点观察：量化敏感性随动作类别、模型层、权重与激活而异；数值精度改变工作负载从而影响有利GPU工作点。提出ActTune：轻量决策树从配置动作误差学习分裂与叶子精度配置，在每次策略调用前选择精度；控制器预测下一工作负载，用延迟预算下校准的查找表异步应用GPU工作点；共享常驻量化权重库支持配置切换，无需权重重建或额外策略评估。

Result: 在LIBERO终身机器人学习基准上，平均任务成功率相对SOTA提升最多2.3%；相对原始BF16实现，推理最快提升2.02倍，结合GPU工作点自适应，每成功任务能耗降低最多76.8%。

Conclusion: ActTune以动作感知的精度分配和GPU工作点协同优化，在保证成功率与延迟约束（延迟增幅≤10%）的前提下显著降低每成功任务的GPU能耗。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies repeatedly invoke inference to control robots, making graphics processing unit (GPU) energy a recurring cost of task execution. Reducing energy per inference call, however, may not reduce energy per successful task if numerical errors increase failures or slower inference prolongs execution. We therefore target GPU energy per successful task while preserving task success and keeping the inference-latency increase within 10\%. Our approach builds on two observations: quantization sensitivity varies across action classes, model layers, and weights versus activations; and numerical precision changes the workload, shifting favorable GPU operating points. We introduce ActTune, an action-aware framework that connects layer-wise precision allocation with workload-dependent GPU operating-point selection over requested frequency--power-cap pairs. A lightweight decision tree learns its splits and leaf precision configurations directly from configuration action errors, then selects precision before each policy call. The controller forecasts the next workload and applies the selected GPU operating point asynchronously using a lookup table calibrated under a latency budget. A shared resident quantized weight bank enables configuration switching without weight reconstruction or additional policy evaluations. On LIBERO, a benchmark for lifelong robot learning, ActTune improves mean task success by up to 2.3\% relative to state of the art. Relative to the original BF16 implementations, it delivers up to $2.02\times$ faster inference and, with GPU operating-point adaptation, reduces energy per successful task by up to 76.8\%.

</details>


### [226] [Behavioral Safety Assessment towards Large-scale Deployment of Autonomous Vehicles, Part I: Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08450)
*Henry X. Liu,Tinghan Wang,Xintao Yan,Haowei Sun,Zhijie Qiao,Kenneth Boyd,Shuo Feng,Greg Stevens,Greg McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出从传统功能安全转向"行为安全"的范式转变，并构建包含行为能力测试与驾驶智能测试的第三方AV安全评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车实际部署进展显著，但安全仍是广泛落地的关键障碍。传统功能安全方法以车辆为中心，主要验证AV软硬件的可靠性、鲁棒性与充分性，无法充分应对AV与周边交通环境的交互及其行为影响，因此需要新的评估范式。

Method: 提出面向行为安全的评估范式，并设计第三方AV安全评估框架，包含两个互补的评估组件：行为能力测试（Behavioral Competency Test）与驾驶智能测试（Driving Intelligence Test）。前者在受控场景下评估AV的反应性行为，确保基本行为能力；后者在自然交通条件下评估AV的交互行为，量化安全关键事件频率。

Result: 通过驾驶智能测试可量化安全关键事件频率，从而在大规模部署前提供具有统计意义的安全指标。研究第二部分对一个开源L4级自动驾驶系统（ADS）进行测试，以验证所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 行为安全是对传统功能安全的重要补充与范式升级，所提出的双组件第三方评估框架能够更全面地衡量AV在交通环境中的表现，为AV大规模部署前的安全评估提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) have significantly advanced in real-world deployment in recent years, yet safety continues to be a critical barrier to widespread adoption. Traditional functional safety approaches, which primarily verify the reliability, robustness, and adequacy of AV hardware and software systems from a vehicle-centric perspective, do not sufficiently address the AV's broader interactions and behavioral impact on the surrounding traffic environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose a paradigm shift toward behavioral safety, a comprehensive approach focused on evaluating AV responses and interactions within the traffic environment. To systematically assess behavioral safety, we introduce a third-party AV safety assessment framework comprising two complementary evaluation components: the Behavioral Competency Test and the Driving Intelligence Test. The Behavioral Competency Test evaluates the AV's reactive behaviors under controlled scenarios, ensuring basic behavioral competency. In contrast, the Driving Intelligence Test assesses the AV's interactive behaviors within naturalistic traffic conditions, quantifying the frequency of safety-critical events to deliver statistically meaningful safety metrics before large-scale deployment. In Part II of this study, an open-source Level 4 Automated Driving System (ADS) is tested to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [227] [Behavioral Safety Assessment towards Large-scale Deployment of Autonomous Vehicles, Part II: Assessment Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08458)
*Henry X. Liu,Tinghan Wang,Xintao Yan,Haowei Sun,Zhijie Qiao,Kenneth Boyd,Shuo Feng,Greg Stevens,Greg McGuire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真与实车测试验证了第三方自动驾驶行为安全评估框架，发现Autoware.Universe仅具备14项行为胜任力中的6项，碰撞率约为人类驾驶员的1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方评估对提升公众接受度、建立消费者信心和制定有效安全标准至关重要，需要在自动驾驶大规模部署前系统评估其行为安全性。

Method: 沿用第一部分提出的第三方测试方案，采用开源L4级自动驾驶系统Autoware.Universe，在仿真环境和密歇根大学Mcity测试场实体跑道上进行验证测试。

Result: Autoware.Universe具备14项行为胜任力中的6项；碰撞率为每英里3.01×10^-3次，约为人类驾驶员平均碰撞率的1000倍；测试中还发现若干此前未知的不安全场景。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了行为安全评估的必要性，在自动驾驶广泛部署前需通过此类评估改进其安全性能。

Abstract: Third-party evaluations of autonomous vehicle (AV) safety can play a vital role in improving public acceptance, building consumer confidence, and establishing effective safety standards. In Part I of this study, we propose a dedicated third-party testing initiative for systematically evaluating AV behavioral safety. In this paper, we validate our proposed framework using Autoware.Universe, an open-source Level 4 Automated Driving System (ADS), tested both in simulated environments and on the physical test track at the University of Michigan's Mcity Testing Facility. The results indicate that Autoware.Universe possesses 6 out of 14 behavioral competencies and exhibited a crash rate of 3.01x10^-3 crashes per mile, approximately 1,000 times higher than the average human driver crash rate. During the tests, we also uncovered a number of unknown unsafe scenarios for Autoware.Universe. These findings underscore the necessity of behavioral safety evaluations for improving AV safety performance prior to widespread public deployment.

</details>


### [228] [A Belief-State World Model for Catheter Navigation under Sparse Fluoroscopy: A Planar Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08469)
*Damini Rijhwani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文把稀疏透视下的血管内导管导航建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，用Cosserat杆仿真器提供动力学、稀疏投影提供观测、粒子滤波维护信念状态，在简化仿真中验证了信念模型可在低频观测下跟踪导管尖端并给出可靠性与接触风险信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内导管导航依赖连续透视，使患者和医护人员在整个手术过程中持续暴露于电离辐射。论文希望探索基于物理的世界模型能否在刻意稀疏的X射线采集中维持导航，并在内部状态估计不可靠时给出信号。

Method: 将稀疏透视导航形式化为POMDP：Cosserat杆仿真器提供转移动力学，稀疏带噪投影作为观测，粒子滤波维护对器械状态的信念（本实现简化为尖端状态与几何接触代理）。在刻意简化的合成平面血管体模中评估，采用准静态杆模型与模拟投影，不使用临床或动物数据。

Result: 在该设置下，信念状态模型以1.30 mm的均方根误差跟踪仿真尖端，观测频率仅为连续频率的十五分之一（每帧0.97 mm，三十分之一频率时1.51 mm）；报告的90%可信区间达到0.92的经验覆盖率；由信念导出的接触风险估计区分不安全接触事件的AUROC为0.68。

Conclusion: 结果属于简化仿真上的概念验证，而非临床就绪性的证明；其意义在于表明，校准良好的信念而不仅是点估计精度，才是稀疏成像世界模型必须提供的核心属性。

Abstract: Endovascular catheter navigation relies on continuous fluoroscopy, exposing patients and clinical staff to ionizing radiation throughout the procedure. We investigate whether a physics-based world model can sustain the navigation task between deliberately sparse X-ray acquisitions, and whether the model can signal when its internal state estimate is no longer reliable. We formulate sparse-fluoroscopy navigation as a partially observable Markov decision process where a Cosserat rod simulator supplies transition dynamics, sparse noisy projections provide observations, and a particle filter maintains a belief over the device state, reduced in this implementation to a tip state with a geometric contact proxy. We evaluate this formulation in a deliberately simplified setting: a synthetic planar vessel phantom with a quasi-static rod model and simulated projections, without clinical or animal data. In this setting, the belief-state model tracks the simulated tip with a root mean square error of 1.30 mm while acquiring observations at one fifteenth of the continuous rate (0.97 mm at every frame, 1.51 mm at one thirtieth), reported 90 percent credible intervals achieve 0.92 empirical coverage, and the belief-derived contact risk estimate discriminates unsafe contact events with an AUROC of 0.68. These results constitute a proof of concept on a simplified simulation rather than a demonstration of clinical readiness; their purpose is to establish that calibrated belief, rather than point-estimate accuracy alone, is the essential property a sparse-imaging world model must deliver.

</details>


### [229] [No Need to Stop the Fleet: Localized Anomaly Isolation via Dead Zones in AGV Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08524)
*Natalia Ogorelysheva,Falk Howar,Jian-Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"死区"——一种形式化定义的局部隔离方法,作为全域急停之外的失效可运行机制,使异常区域被隔离时不受影响的机器人可继续运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGV车队依赖全域急停处理局部异常(故障、危险或受污染区域),虽安全但会导致整个车队停机,即使仅小区域受影响,造成不必要的中断。

Method: 定义死区机制并引入两种运行策略:基本死区处理(受影响机器人进入死区并停止)与带逃逸的死区处理(距离足够远的机器人绕行)。针对逆时针轨迹上的逃逸,证明在保持机器人间最小间距的前提下无碰撞且有界到达延迟。

Result: 实验评估验证了理论保证,并刻画了全域急停、基本死区处理、带逃逸死区处理三种方法的吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: 死区机制作为附加的失效可运行手段,可在局部异常时避免全域停机,在安全性与吞吐量之间提供更优权衡。

Abstract: Automated Guided Vehicle (AGV) fleets in production and logistics environments rely on a system-wide emergency stop to handle local anomalies such as malfunctions, hazards, or contaminated areas. While safe, this approach halts the entire fleet even when only a small area is affected, causing unnecessary downtime. This paper proposes dead zones --- a formally defined localized isolation approach that serves as an additional fail-operational mechanism for situations where a full system-wide halt is not strictly needed. Rather than shutting down the entire fleet, a dead zone isolates the anomalous area, allowing unaffected robots to continue operating normally. We introduce two operational strategies: basic dead zone handling, in which affected robots enter a dead zone and stop, and dead zone handling with escaping, in which robots that are sufficiently far from a dead zone reroute around it. For escaping on counterclockwise trajectories we prove collision-freedom with a bounded arrival delay for all escaping robots, provided a minimum pairwise separation is kept between robots. Experimental evaluation confirms the theoretical guarantees and characterizes the throughput tradeoffs between all three approaches: full emergency stop, basic dead zone handling, and dead zone handling with escaping.

</details>


### [230] [WareFly-VLA: A Vision-Language-Action Framework for UAV Navigation and Human Tracking in Smart Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08526)
*Thinh D. Le,Son T. Nguyen,Duong Q. Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Ngo Anh Vien,H. Nguyen-Xuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WareFly-VLA，一个面向智能仓库中语言引导无人机人体搜索、定位与跟踪的逼真 UAV VLA 框架与数据集，并对四种开源 VLA 架构进行统一基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在机器人操作与地面移动导航中表现优异，但智能仓库中语言条件下的无人机控制仍鲜有探索，主要受限于缺少同时提供连续低层飞行动作、细粒度自然语言目标描述和真实工业环境的基准。

Method: 构建 WareFly-VLA：在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中采集 507 条人工遥操作飞行回合、8,504 个高分辨率 RGB 帧转移，每条配有目标工人外观描述与同步四自由度控制指令；覆盖目标接近与人员跟随两类任务，以及遮挡、远距搜索、高度变化和杂乱场景；在无泄漏的回合级协议下，对 SmolVLA、GR00T N1.7、pi_0 和 OpenVLA 四种开源架构在两个控制频率下建立统一基准。

Result: 仓库中语言条件空中控制远未解决：严格泛化设置下性能大幅下降；连续动作建模持续优于离散动作分词；单帧仅前向通道可靠可学；当前基础模型接口从地面与人形具身向空中平台迁移效果差。

Conclusion: 同步的视频、语言、动作、位姿与难度标注可支持世界模型研究；数据集、基线与评测协议已发布，以促进智能仓库中语言驱动的空中自主能力研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation and ground-mobile navigation, yet language-conditioned control of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in smart warehouses remains largely unexplored, hindered by the lack of benchmarks that jointly provide continuous low-level flight actions, fine-grained natural-language target descriptions, and realistic industrial environments. This paper introduces WareFly-VLA, a photorealistic UAV VLA framework and dataset for language-guided human search, localization, and tracking in warehouse environments. It contains 507 human-teleoperated flight episodes and 8,504 high-resolution RGB transitions collected in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, each paired with a human-written appearance description of the target worker and a synchronized four-degree-of-freedom control command. Two aerial tasks are covered: target approach and person following, under occlusion, long-range search, altitude variation, and clutter. A unified benchmark of four open-source VLA architectures (SmolVLA, GR00T N1.7, pi_0 and OpenVLA) is established under a leakage-free episode-level protocol at two control rates. The results show that language-conditioned aerial control in warehouses is far from solved: performance drops substantially under strict generalization settings, continuous action modeling consistently outperforms discrete action tokenization, only the forward channel is reliably learnable from a single frame, and current foundation-model interfaces transfer poorly from ground and humanoid embodiments to aerial platforms. The synchronized video, language, action, pose, and difficulty annotations further support world-model research. The dataset, baselines, and evaluation protocol are released to support language-grounded aerial autonomy in smart warehouses.

</details>


### [231] [Pareto-Optimal Entropy-Regularized Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08532)
*Dimitrios S. Georgiou,Augustinos D. Saravanos,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出PER-DDP，一种基于熵正则化种群框架的轨迹优化方法，通过先验引导采样、扩展轨迹评估和帕累托筛选，在保持DDP二阶结构的同时拓宽探索范围，成功率优于现有采样增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化在非线性动力学、驱动限制和避碰约束下高度非凸。DDP作为高效二阶打靶法，因局部结构易陷入次优盆地。现有采样增强变体虽通过随机探索缓解，但通常仅围绕少数保留轨迹采样，且仅基于代价筛选，限制了探索广度。

Method: 提出PER-DDP（帕累托最优熵正则化DDP），一种源自自由能/相对熵不等式的熵正则化种群框架。方法包括：先验引导采样，围绕每条保留轨迹塑造探索；扩展轨迹评估，超越少数保留候选探测采样策略；帕累托筛选，跨迭代保留任务约束的替代方案。该设计将采样努力与优化种群规模解耦。

Result: 在多个系统和数百个环境中，PER-DDP成功率高于最先进的采样增强轨迹优化方法，并在所有基线无法企及的环境中找到了可靠解。

Conclusion: PER-DDP通过解耦采样与种群规模，在不牺牲DDP二阶结构有效性的前提下拓宽探索，显著提升轨迹优化的成功率与环境适应性。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization (TO) under nonlinear dynamics, actuation limits and collision avoidance constraints is a fundamental problem in robotics, albeit especially challenging due to its highly non-convex nature. For this setting, Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) is an efficient second-order shooting method, yet its local structure renders it vulnerable to suboptimal basins. Sampling-augmented variants mitigate this susceptibility through stochastic exploration, but often sample only around the few trajectories they retain for reoptimization, based solely on their cost which restricts exploration breadth. We introduce Pareto-Optimal Entropy-Regularized DDP (PER-DDP), an entropy-regularized population framework derived from the free-energy/relative-entropy inequality. Our method combines prior-guided sampling that shapes exploration around each retained trajectory, with expanded rollout evaluations that probe these sampling policies beyond the few retained candidates, and Pareto filtering for preserving task-constraint alternatives across iterations. This decouples sampling effort from the optimization population size and broadens exploration without sacrificing the second-order structure that makes DDP effective. Across multiple systems and hundreds of environments, PER-DDP achieves higher success rates than state-of-the-art sampling-augmented TO methods and finds reliable solutions in environments beyond the reach of all baselines.

</details>


### [232] [Micro Neural Policies for Safe Real-Time Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08541)
*Hongpeng Cao,Riccardo Curcio,Daniele Ottaviano,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出微型神经策略（MNP），结合进化策略与统计模型检验进行策略搜索，将神经网络规模大幅压缩，在Cartpole和四旋翼任务上实现安全、鲁棒的零样本仿真到现实迁移，并可在微控制器上以极低时延与抖动实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算力受限的嵌入式设备上实现安全且鲁棒的实时机器人控制具有挑战性，常规神经网络策略规模大、难以部署。

Method: 将进化策略（ES）与基于统计模型检验（SMC）的验证相结合用于策略搜索，合成微型神经策略；在Cartpole与四旋翼控制任务上进行大规模训练与评估，改变控制频率与网络架构，并在仿真验证后通过零样本迁移到物理系统进行部署测试。

Result: MNP能够在不牺牲控制性能的情况下成功实现安全的仿真到现实迁移；策略内存占用为0.5至7.5 kB，可在微控制器上部署，实时推理抖动低于25 ns，芯片超过97%的时间处于空闲状态以支持其他工作负载。

Conclusion: MNP是面向资源极度受限机器人系统的高实用性解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the synthesis of Micro Neural Policies (MNP) to enable safe and robust real-time robotic control on computationally constrained embedded devices. We demonstrate that integrating Evolution Strategy (ES) and Statistical Model Checking (SMC)-based verification for policy search can drastically reduce neural network size without compromising safety and robustness. We conduct a large-scale training and evaluation of MNP on Cartpole and Quadrotor control tasks, varying control frequencies and network architectures. After validating these policies in simulation, we evaluate their deployability through zero-shot transfer to physical systems. Our experiments show that MNP can successfully achieve safe sim-to-real transfer without sacrificing control performance. We then show that the policies' memory footprint, ranging from 0.5 to 7.5 kB, allows deployment on microcontrollers, where they achieve real-time inference latency with under 25 ns of jitter while leaving the chip idle for over 97% of the time for additional workloads. This makes them a highly practical solution for severely resource-constrained robotic systems.

</details>


### [233] [Towards Efficient Robotic Manipulation Models with Self-Recursive Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08555)
*Zijia Chen,Yuenan Hou,Yu Li,Weijie Li,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LCAM（Loss-Conditioned Activation-Moment）剪枝方法，一种无需训练的机器人操作策略非结构化剪枝方法，通过损失条件化的激活矩排序连接重要性，在LIBERO与OpenVLA上取得competitive性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用剪枝准则主要面向图像识别任务，设计目标是保留权重幅值、局部重建或语言模型似然，而非闭环动作行为，直接应用于机器人任务效果不佳。同时机器人策略剪枝常需昂贵的恢复训练与仿真器rollout。

Method: 提出LCAM：训练无关的非结构化剪枝方法。首先结合行归一化权重贡献、在标定演示上测得的激活矩、以及输出方向对动作预测损失的敏感度来对连接排序；随后设计自递归由粗到细流程：每个嵌套粗剪枝阶段后重新标定重要性，在稀疏度拐点之后用留出的离线动作失真指导细粒度预算分配。

Result: 在三个LIBERO套件及OpenVLA上评估，LCAM在广泛剪枝比例下取得competitive性能。在LIBERO-Object结合OpenVLA时，50%非结构化剪枝下成功率84.0%，保留超过90%稠密策略成功率。真实世界机器人乒乓球实验也显示有效。

Conclusion: LCAM是一种免训练、无需仿真rollout的机器人策略剪枝方法，以损失条件化的重要性度量与由粗到细的预算分配实现高效剪枝，在仿真与真实任务上均展现竞争力。

Abstract: Network pruning can reduce parameter redundancy in robotic policies. However, generic pruning criteria are tailored for image recognition tasks and commonly designed to preserve weight magnitude, local reconstruction, or language-model likelihood rather than closed-loop action behavior. Directly applying these pruning algorithms to robotic tasks yields unsatisfactory performance. In this paper, we propose Loss-Conditioned Activation-Moment (LCAM) pruning, a training-free method for unstructured pruning of pre-trained robotic manipulation policies. Specifically, we first rank connections using row-normalized weight contribution, activation moments measured on calibration demonstrations, and the sensitivity of output directions to the action-prediction loss. We further design a self-recursive coarse-to-fine procedure: importance is recalibrated after each nested coarse pruning stage, while held-out offline action distortion guides fine-grained budget allocation after a sparsity knee. Our algorithm is free from costly recovery training and simulator rollouts after pruning. Experiments on three LIBERO suites with competitive robotic policies, together with evaluations on OpenVLA, show that LCAM attains competitive performance across a broad range of pruning ratios. Notably, on LIBERO-Object with OpenVLA, our LCAM achieves 84.0% success at 50% unstructured pruning, retaining over 90% of the dense policy's success rate. Promising results on real-world robotic ping pong further demonstrate the effectiveness of our pruning algorithm.

</details>


### [234] [One for All, All for One: Coordinated Multi-Agent Diffusion Steering via Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08595)
*Riccardo Barbano,Vincent Pauline,Runchang Li,George Webber,Alexander Denker,Željko Kereta,Stefan Bauer,Francisco Vargas,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMDS框架，将冻结的预训练扩散模型作为可复用生成基元，通过学习控制协调其逆向过程，实现模块化结构化输出生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型常需生成由交互组件构成的结构化输出，而用单一模型建模需同时学习组件分布及其交互关系，难度大且难以复用。作者希望采用模块化替代方案：复用独立训练的组件生成器，仅学习如何协调它们以产生连贯的结构化输出。

Method: 将协调问题形式化为随机最优控制问题，在"装配级奖励"（描述组合输出所需属性）与"偏离预训练动力学的程度"之间进行权衡。通过学习到的控制对优化过程进行摊销，使其可跨新任务实例复用，从而通过控制协调冻结扩散模型的逆向过程。

Result: CMDS能够恢复已知的目标分布、用同一个训练好的控制满足不同空间约束，并从退化混合中恢复各个源；在多智能体迷宫导航、铰接机器人规划和文本条件人体运动任务中，成功将冻结模型转变为协同的多智能体生成器。

Conclusion: 通过将协调建模为随机最优控制并摊销优化，CMDS提供了一种模块化框架，无需重训组件生成器即可复用并协调冻结扩散模型，实现连贯的多智能体结构化输出生成。

Abstract: Deep generative models often produce structured outputs composed of interacting components. Modelling these outputs with a single model requires learning both the component distributions and their interactions. We pursue a modular alternative: reuse independently trained component generators and learn only how to coordinate them to produce coherent structured outputs. Our framework, Coordinated Multi-Agent Diffusion Steering (CMDS), treats frozen pretrained diffusion models as reusable generative primitives and coordinates their reverse processes through a learned control. We formulate coordination as a stochastic optimal control problem, balancing an assembly-level reward that specifies the desired properties of the combined output against deviations from the pretrained dynamics. The learned control amortises this optimisation, allowing reuse across new task instances. Experiments show that CMDS can recover a known target distribution, satisfy different spatial constraints with the same trained control, and recover individual sources from degraded mixtures. Across multi-agent maze navigation, articulated robot planning, and text-conditioned human motion, CMDS turns frozen models into coordinated multi-agent generators.

</details>


### [235] [A Swarm-Coordinated Multi-Robot System for Early Stress Detection in Agricultural Rows Using Multimodal Leaf Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08603)
*Rishi Gupta,Astha Goyal,Vinay Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CropSentry，一种低成本地面多机器人系统，通过多模态叶片感知持续监测作物健康并检测早期胁迫，实验分类准确率达 84.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代农业中早期胁迫检测对提高效率、减少时间金钱与人力浪费十分必要，但高光谱成像、AI 系统等现有技术成本高、复杂度大，中小农户难以实施。

Method: CropSentry 由两个自主机器人组成，逐行检测叶片颜色与环境数据，并将观测结果进行空间映射后发送至主机器人；主机器人以颜色编码的行段生成实时网页仪表盘，展示作物健康状况。

Result: 在 63 次观测实验中，整体作物健康分类准确率为 84.12%，其中健康植株 82.60%、营养缺乏植株 88%、患病植株 80%；10 次从属机器人观测实现了 100% 无线通信成功率。

Conclusion: 多机器人近距离叶片检测能够在可负担、可获取、可扩展的前提下发现作物早期胁迫，为农户提供及时信息，改善资源利用与作物管理。

Abstract: Early stress detection in crops is a necessity today to improve efficiency and reduce waste of time, money, and effort. However, most modern techniques, such as hyperspectral imaging and AI-based systems, are too costly and complex for medium and small-scale farmers to implement. This paper showcases CropSentry, a low-cost, ground-based multi-robot system that uses multimodal leaf sensing to continuously monitor crop health by tracking stress levels. The system comprises two autonomous bots that continuously detect leaf color and environmental data row by row. The observations are spatially mapped and sent over to the master bot, which uses color-coded row segments to generate a real-time web-based dashboard displaying crop health. After 63 observations were collected during the experiments, the results showed an overall crop health classification accuracy of 84.12%, with 82.60% for healthy plants, 88% for nutrient-deficient plants, and 80% for diseased plants. Also, 100% wireless communication success rate across 10 slave observations was achieved. Close-range leaf inspection across multiple bots can detect early stress in crops while remaining affordable, accessible, and scalable. It provides farmers with timely information to improve resource utilization and crop management.

</details>


### [236] [Feeling Through the Load: Compliant Quadruped Locomotion under Payload Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08637)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Mayank Mishra,Prajit KrisshnaKumar,Sebastian Scherer,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种力感知运动框架，使四足机器人能在负载未被固定的情况下，通过机载测量推断人的外部交互力，并柔顺响应，同时保持稳定行走与负载平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人越来越多被期望在人类环境中搬运物体。当人直接与负载交互（而非与机器人本身交互）时，若负载未被约束，机器人必须区分有意的人为外力与负载的正常运动，同时维持负载平衡与运动稳定。而仅依靠机载测量实现这种推断与柔顺响应是一个难点，现有方法尚不充分。

Method: 提出力感知运动框架，将负载交互视为塑造"机器人—负载"联合系统运动的指令。方法将力感知运动学习与力估计分离：1）训练一个柔顺的载物策略；2）训练一个因果力估计器；通过"估计器在环"（estimator-in-the-loop）的数据聚合与微调进行联合训练，从机器人机载测量中预测交互力。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该控制器能够维持稳定的载物行走，对外部交互柔顺让步（yield compliantly），并利用推断出的力支持人在环中引导机器人轨迹的改变。

Conclusion: 仅用机载测量即可实现力感知的载物行走与柔顺的人机交互，验证了将"柔顺载物策略+因果力估计器"分离并通过估计器在环训练的有效性，为四足机器人在人类环境中的人为负载交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to carry objects while moving through human environments. But what happens when a person interacts directly with the payload rather than with the robot? If the payload is unrestrained, the robot must distinguish intentional external interactions from ordinary payload motion, while still keeping the load balanced and maintaining stable locomotion. How can a quadruped infer and compliantly respond to such interactions using only onboard measurements? In this work, we develop a force-aware locomotion framework that treats payload interactions as commands that shape the motion of the combined robot-payload system. Our approach separates the learning of force-aware locomotion and force estimation on an unrestrained payload. We combine a compliant load-carrying policy with a causal force estimator, trained through estimator-in-the-loop data aggregation and finetuning, to predict interactions from onboard robot measurements. Our simulations and real-world experiments show that the resulting controller can maintain stable payload-carrying locomotion, yield compliantly to external interactions, and use the inferred force to support human-guided changes in the robot's trajectory.

</details>


### [237] [RIWANav: Recursive World-Action Models with Self-Improvement for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08640)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Haitao Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIWANAV 提出一种后训练框架，将世界模型与动作模型（策略）的协同自适应建模为任务特定的递归自我改进循环，用于长时程城市导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程城市导航中顺序局部决策的误差会随时间累积；模仿学习很少从失败中学习，而物理试错强化学习代价高昂。动作条件世界模型虽能通过预测候选动作的视觉后果来提供想象反馈，但冻结的世界模型会随策略演化而变得不可靠。

Method: 每个循环交替进行两步更新：世界模型通过想象结果评估策略动作，为组相对策略优化（GRPO）提供比较式反馈；改进后的策略据此构建基于真实数据的自我课程，按行为新颖性和预测误差选择与专家一致的动作-视频对；精炼后的世界模型再为下一轮策略更新提供反馈，从而闭合递归自我改进回路。

Result: 实验表明 RIWANAV 优于训练基线和先前方法，验证了策略与世界模型之间的递归自我改进循环；真实世界试验进一步证明了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: RIWANAV 通过世界模型与策略的耦合递归自我改进，有效提升了长时程城市导航性能，并具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon urban navigation requires sequential local decisions whose errors can compound over time. Imitation learning (IL) rarely learns from failures, while physical trial-and-error reinforcement learning (RL) is costly. Action-conditioned world models can provide imagined feedback by predicting visual consequences for candidate actions. However, a frozen world model may become less reliable as the policy evolves. In this paper, we introduce RIWANAV, a post-training framework that casts the coupled adaptation of a world model and an action model (policy) as task-specific recursive self-improvement (RSI). Each cycle alternates two updates. The world model evaluates policy actions through imagined outcomes, providing comparative feedback for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). The improved policy then constructs a grounded self-curriculum, selecting expert-consistent action-video pairs by behavioral novelty and prediction error. The refined world model supplies feedback for the next policy update, closing the recursive self-improvement loop. Experiments show that RIWANAV outperforms training baselines and prior methods, validating the proposed recursive self-improvement loop between the policy and world model. Real-world trials further demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [238] [HygieneRoboBench: Benchmarking Hygiene-Aware Planning for Household Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08642)
*Yurun Chen,Josh Qixuan Sun,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Tianyi Wang,Mark Crowley,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HygieneRoboBench 基准（624 实例、134 任务族）评估家用机器人在接触污染历史下安全续规划能力，并提出 Hygiene-NSP 方法，安全与最优安全解决率分别达 94.4% 和 90.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家用机器人的夹爪、工具和共享表面会通过接触传播污染物，新的接触事件可能使已有计划变得不安全。现有基准无法同时评估两件事：规划器如何从接触历史中识别卫生风险，以及在新接触事件发生后如何规划安全的后续动作。同时规划器还须在时间和资源限制内、并尊重用户优先级的前提下完成这些工作。

Method: 引入 HygieneRoboBench，包含 624 个实例、134 个任务族，用于从给定执行历史出发评估家庭任务的安全解决。任务涵盖两个夹爪与共享物体造成的污染、处理成本与用户优先级。通过受控的历史、画像与事件对比，结合独立的计划评估，考察安全解决、用户优先级下的成本效率以及对接触事件的响应。进一步提出 Hygiene-NSP，将基于 LLM 的语义接地、接触历史重建与 CP-SAT 相结合，在用户优先级下联合规划卫生处理与任务执行。

Result: 对基于 LLM 与符号规划器的评测表明，安全完成任务并不保证在用户优先级下具有最低执行成本。Hygiene-NSP 达到 94.4% 的安全解决率和 90.4% 的最优安全解决率，二者均高于所评测的基线规划器在全数据集上的表现。

Conclusion: 安全性与成本最优性在卫生感知规划中并不一致，需显式建模接触历史与用户优先级。Hygiene-NSP 通过 LLM 接地、接触历史重建与 CP-SAT 联合优化，在统一基准上同时提升安全解决率与最优安全解决率，优于现有基线。

Abstract: Contact with contaminated objects can spread hazards through a household robot's grippers, tools, and shared surfaces, while new contacts can make an existing plan unsafe. Existing benchmarks do not jointly assess how planners identify hygiene risks from contact history and plan safe continuations after new contact events. Planners must do so within time and resource limits while respecting user priorities. We introduce HygieneRoboBench, with 624 instances across 134 task families, to evaluate safe resolution of household tasks from a given execution history. Tasks capture contamination through two grippers and shared objects, treatment costs, and user priorities. We combine controlled history, profile, and event comparisons with independent plan evaluation. These assess safe resolution, cost efficiency under user priorities, and responses to contact events. Evaluation of LLM-based and symbolic planners shows that safely completing a task does not guarantee the lowest execution costs under the user's priorities. To address this problem, we introduce Hygiene-NSP. It combines LLM-based grounding, contact-history reconstruction, and CP-SAT to jointly plan hygiene treatment and task execution under user priorities. Hygiene-NSP achieves safe resolution and optimal safe resolution rates of 94.4% and 90.4%, respectively. Both rates are higher than those of the evaluated baseline planners on the full dataset. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/HygieneRoboBench/.

</details>


### [239] [Fast Non-Parametric Heteroscedastic Imitation Learning With Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08650)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Arne Sachtler,Markus Knauer,Cem Küçükgenç,Yanlong Huang,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何先验的非参数方法，可在数据稀缺和异方差不确定性下建模概率策略，支持流形输入/输出，更新快且易于自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示学习概率策略时，数据高效利用和快速适应新场景是关键需求。现有的非参数（多为核方法）方案要么忽略机器人中常见的流形几何结构而降低数据效率，要么虽考虑几何但不确定性估计不可靠或需要重新训练才能适应。

Method: 提出一种融合几何先验的非参数方法，用于数据稀缺且存在异方差不确定性的概率建模。用该方法构建基于时间或机器人状态的策略，采用不可分离的对角核来捕捉相同尺寸输入输出间各自由度之间的不确定性关系；并通过优化公式实现快速更新。

Result: 包含位置和姿态的轨迹更新耗时低于3 ms；方法支持流形输入和输出，并能处理大幅姿态变化；借助任务参数化可轻松适应不同物体位姿。在多个玩具示例和真实机器人操作任务（自主执行与共享控制）上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该几何感知的非参数方法兼具数据高效、不确定性可靠、适应快速与支持流形输入输出等优点，适用于真实机器人概率策略学习与自适应任务。

Abstract: When learning probabilistic policies from human demonstrations, data-efficient learning and fast adaptations to new scenarios are key requirements. One popular way to achieve intuitive and reliable adaptations is through non-parametric, typically kernel-based, methods. However, existing solutions either fail to account for the geometry of manifolds common in robotics, limiting data efficiency, or, when geometry-aware, provide unreliable uncertainty estimates or require retraining to adapt. We propose a non-parametric approach leveraging geometric priors in scenarios of data scarcity and heteroscedastic uncertainties for probabilistic modeling. We utilize the method to formulate policies based on time or robot state, where non-separable diagonal kernels allow capturing uncertainty relations between degrees of freedom for same-sized in- and outputs. Fast updates, requiring less than 3 ms for a trajectory involving both position and orientation are possible through an optimized formulation. Our approach supports both manifold-valued input and manifold-valued output with large orientation changes. Using task parameterization, adaptation to different object poses is easily possible. We evaluate the approach on a set of toy examples and on real robot manipulation tasks both in autonomous execution and in shared control scenarios.

</details>


### [240] [Magnet-Aware Control of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08653)
*J. Playan Garai,S. B. Djuve,C. McGreavy,M. Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向强磁场环境的足式机器人控制框架，通过磁场力建模、磁场估计与磁感知MPC/WBC，实现对空间变化磁场扰动的动态补偿，将可操作区域扩大约29.34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大科学装置中自主机器人可提升运行时间并减少人员暴露，但强磁场会干扰传感器并产生随位姿变化的机械扰动，使机器人失稳，传统反应式控制器难以应对。

Method: 1) 为MuJoCo开发自定义物理插件，建模刚体单元所受磁力；2) 提出逆磁场估计框架，从四足机器人动态响应及任意数量传感器读数中推断潜在磁场；3) 构建磁感知模型预测控制（MPC）与全身控制（WBC）架构，在运动过程中预测并抵消磁扰动。

Result: 仿真与实机实验表明，相比无补偿系统，力空间中最大可抑制磁扰动提高2.5倍，力矩空间提高1.6–2倍；在给定磁场内，机器人可操作区域增加29.34%，其中10.84%的区域在无补偿控制器下会导致立即崩塌。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效建模、估计并补偿空间变化的磁力扰动，显著扩展足式机器人在强磁场环境中的稳定运行范围。

Abstract: Autonomous robots can increase uptime and reduce human exposure in Big Science facilities, but strong magnetic fields needed for their operation corrupt sensors and induce pose-dependent mechanical wrenches that destabilize robots and challenge conventional reactive controllers. This paper presents a control framework for modeling, estimating, and dynamically compensating for spatially varying magnetic wrenches acting on legged robots to improve robustness in these fields. We introduce a custom physics plugin for the MuJoCo simulator to model magnetic forces on rigid-body elements, alongside an inverse field-estimation framework to infer the latent magnetic field directly from quadruped dynamic responses and any number of sensor readings. Furthermore, we develop a Magnet-Aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Whole-Body Control (WBC) architecture that predicts and counteracts magnetic perturbations during locomotion to increase the range of magnetic fields in which the robot can operate. The effectiveness of the framework is validated through both simulation and physical hardware experiments. We show our method increases the the maximum rejectable disturbances from magnetic field in the force space by a factor of 2.5 and and between 1.6-2 times in torque space compared to a non-compensated system. Within the proposed magnetic field, this constitutes an increase in the area in which the robot can operate by 29,34% of which 10,84% would have previously caused an immediate collapse to non-compensated controllers.

</details>


### [241] [NMPP: Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning for Agile UAV Flight in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08695)
*Ondřej Procházka,Juraj Pauko,Robert Pěnička,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出非线性模型预测规划(NMPP)，将障碍作为硬几何约束，为无避障能力的SE(3)控制器提供全状态参考轨迹，提升四旋翼在杂乱环境中的敏捷飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼穿越杂乱环境需要既无碰撞、又动力学可行且满足驱动限制的参考轨迹。现有方法多在凸走廊内优化光滑多项式（限制敏捷性），或将障碍作为软代价与跟踪性能权衡，难以兼顾敏捷性与安全性。

Method: NMPP将感知到的障碍作为硬几何约束，规划器输出全状态参考轨迹，交给一个不考虑障碍的SE(3)控制器进行精确跟踪。

Result: 位置RMSE比线性MPC轨迹规划器低58-67%，比多项式轨迹规划器低41-70%；在最高9.5 m/s的森林飞行中全部无碰撞完成；在更激进的速�度剖面下取得86%飞行成功率，而某最先进规划器仅26%；真实世界部署可在未知杂乱环境中以最高5.5 m/s可靠飞行。

Conclusion: 将障碍作为硬约束并配合无避障控制器，可显著提升跟踪精度、飞行速度与成功率，并在真实环境中得到验证。

Abstract: Flying a quadrotor through a cluttered environment requires not only planning a collision-free reference trajectory based on perceived obstacles, but the reference also needs to be dynamically feasible and within the actuation limits of the vehicle, so that the controller can track it precisely. Existing methods either optimize a smooth polynomial inside a convex corridor, which limits agility, or treat obstacles as soft costs traded against tracking performance. We propose a Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning (NMPP) that imposes perceived obstacles as hard geometric constraints and hands a full-state reference to an obstacle-blind SE(3) controller. Our planner achieves a 58-67 % lower position RMSE than a linear Model Predictive Control trajectory planner and a 41-70 % lower RMSE than a polynomial trajectory planner. It also completes all forest flights with up to 9.5 m/s speed without collisions, and achieves 86 % flight success rate under a more aggressive speed profile where a state-of-the-art planner has only 26 % success rate. The real-world deployment showed reliable execution flying up to 5.5 m/s in an unknown cluttered environment.

</details>


### [242] [EgoLAP: Learning from Egocentric Human Data through Language-Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08726)
*Lihan Zha,Shresth Grover,Tenny Yin,Samuel M. Bateman,Hengkai Pan,Mengchao Zhang,Aykut Onol,Allen Z. Ren,Dhruv Shah,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLAP 通过共享的语言动作思维链，将人类与机器人轨迹联合预训练，在真实世界任务中达到 80.1% 的平均进展，比替代动作表示提升 2.3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类自我中心数据有助于扩展机器人学习、摆脱昂贵的机器人演示，但具身差异使原始人类轨迹难以直接作为控制监督信号。作者的核心洞察是：虽然底层动作具有具身特异性，但其背后的运动意图可捕捉跨人类与机器人可迁移的任务相关结构。

Method: 提出 EgoLAP，一个 VLA 预训练框架，通过共享的、基于语言的动作思维链（action chain-of-thought）联合学习人类与机器人轨迹。该方法将运动意图表达为结构化、时间抽象的语言动作，并配以扎根于场景几何、物理和物体可供性的运动级推理。

Result: 在大量真实世界与仿真实验中，EgoLAP 比替代动作表示更有效地将人类经验迁移至机器人控制，达到 80.1% 的真实世界平均任务进展，性能提升 2.3 倍；运动级推理也优于结合子任务、物体框和视觉轨迹的复合推理格式。

Conclusion: 以语言动作思维链表达运动意图，可实现人类经验向机器人控制的有效迁移，优于其他动作表示与复合推理格式。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offer a path to scaling robot learning beyond costly robot demonstrations, yet the embodiment gap makes raw human trajectories a poor supervisory target for control. Our key insight is that, although low-level actions are embodiment-specific, their underlying motion intent can capture task-relevant structure that transfers across humans and robots. We introduce EgoLAP, a VLA pre-training framework that jointly learns from human and robot trajectories through a shared language-based action chain-of-thought. EgoLAP expresses motion intent as structured, temporally abstracted language actions and pairs them with motion-level reasoning grounded in scene geometry, physics, and object affordances. Across extensive real-world and simulated experiments, EgoLAP transfers human experience to robot control more effectively than alternative action representations and reaches 80.1% mean real-world task progress, a 2.3x performance gain over alternative action representations. Motion-level reasoning also outperforms a composite reasoning format that combines subtask, object-box, and visual-trace reasoning.

</details>


### [243] [Towards an Extensible Benchmark for Spoken Dialogue with Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08733)
*Casey Kennington,Ross Mead,Saad Elbeleidy,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个用于探索机器人与人类之间口语对话交互问题的基准测试，并建议使用实时通信框架Retico来支持该基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可作为人与机器人之间的即插即用接口，但在语音、对话、快速交互与协作等方面仍存在重要挑战，因此需要一个基准来系统性探索这些交互问题。

Method: 提出一个基准，用于探索机器人与人类之间常见的口语对话现象，包括请求澄清、打断、具身信号（如点头或面部线索）以及时间约束；并建议使用实时通信框架Retico来满足机器人实现口语对话能力所需的关键技术要求。

Result: 提出了该基准，并阐述了将其扩展到更广泛的人机交互方面的设想；同时提出使用Retico框架以支持该基准的实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为社区提供了一个探索人机口语对话交互的基准，并通过Retico框架满足实时对话的技术需求，未来可扩展到更多人机交互的重要方面。

Abstract: Language models provide a plug-and-play interface between humans and robots, but important challenges remain when speech, dialogue, fast interaction, and collaboration are required. We propose a benchmark for the community to use as a way to explore common spoken dialogue artifacts between robots and humans, including requests for clarification, interruptions, embodied signals (e.g., head nods or facial cues), and time constraints. We also explain our vision to extend the benchmark for other aspects of human-robot interaction that are important to the larger research community. To facilitate the benchmark, we further propose using \textit{Retico}, a real-time communication framework that fulfills important technical requirements to enable robots to have spoken dialogue capabilities.

</details>


### [244] [PhoneBot: A Low-Cost Open Humanoid Robot Platform Reusing Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08737)
*Ruochen Hou,Quanyou Wang,Daniel Koh,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhoneBot是一款以智能手机作为传感与计算核心的低成本开源人形机器人平台，通过模块化结构与实时通信框架实现稳定行走与多种交互功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人在教育科研中的普及受限于硬件成本高、传感系统复杂、算力需求大，亟需一种低成本、易复现的平台。

Method: 将商品智能手机复用为核心传感与计算单元，利用其IMU、摄像头、无线通信与本地算力，搭建由13个低成本执行器驱动的模块化下半身结构与躯干式手机安装设计，并配套机械结构、软件架构与实时通信框架以支持稳定运动与视觉跟随、对话交互、拍摄及移动呈现等功能。

Result: 实验表明机器人可实现可靠行走、感知驱动的交互，并能直接基于市售消费级智能手机完成部署。

Conclusion: PhoneBot以全开源软硬件设计提供了低成本、可复现的平台，适用于教育、科研与快速原型开发。

Abstract: The adoption of humanoid robots in education and research remains limited by high hardware costs, complex sensing systems, and substantial computational requirements. This paper presents PhoneBot, a low-cost, open-source humanoid robot platform that repurposes commodity smartphones as its primary sensing and computing unit. By using a smartphone's integrated inertial measurement unit (IMU), camera, wireless connectivity, and onboard processing capabilities, PhoneBot reduces hardware costs and simplifies the system architecture. The robot combines a modular lower-body structure driven by 13 low-cost actuators with a torso-mounted smartphone that supports perception, control computation, and user interaction. We describe the mechanical design, software architecture, and real-time communication framework that support stable locomotion and capabilities including vision-based human following, conversational interaction, filming, and mobile telepresence. Experimental evaluations demonstrate reliable walking, perception-driven interaction, and straightforward deployment using off-the-shelf consumer smartphones. With fully open-source hardware and software designs, PhoneBot provides an affordable, reproducible platform for education, research, and rapid prototyping. More details are available at https://phonebot.dev.

</details>


### [245] [LBA-CBF: Rapidly Adaptive Safety Filters via Parallel Dynamics Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08765)
*Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Timeea-Andreea Radu,Hassan Almubarak,Henry Z. Liao,Michael Görner,Francesco Maurelli,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF)，通过对有限候选动力学模型库进行基于近期预测误差的排序，并对最优模型容差范围内的所有模型施加高阶CBF条件，从而在突发、未测量的动态变化下保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF依赖假设的动力学模型来验证控制指令，因此当系统发生突发且未测量的动态模式变化时，CBF证书可能失效，而这恰恰是安全最关键的时刻。现有自适应或鲁棒方法要么需要切换模型或连续参数估计器，要么难以应对非线性参数依赖。

Method: LBA-CBF维护一个有限候选动力学模型库，用短回看窗口内的近期预测误差对模型排序，并对所有位于最优模型容差范围内的模型施加高阶CBF条件，从而在最佳拟合自适应与全库鲁棒滤波之间连续调节。方法允许动力学非线性依赖未知参数，无需切换模型或连续参数化估计器。理论证明：只要保留一个安全代表性候选模型，任何可行的滤波输入都满足真实CBF条件。

Result: 在四旋翼仿真中面对突发风向反转和未知负载，LBA-CBF在所有随机初始条件下均安全并到达目标，性能与oracle相当；而自适应和鲁棒基线成功率仅为0–88%。模型库规模可达250,000个并实时运行于控制回路。Crazyflie 2.1和F1TENTH实验验证了对风、负载释放和变化轮胎-路面摩擦的适应能力。

Conclusion: LBA-CBF通过回顾式模型筛选与容差鲁棒约束，在无需在线连续估计或切换模型的前提下，实现了对突发动态变化的安全适应，并在仿真与真实平台上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) certify commands through an assumed dynamics model, so an abrupt, unmeasured regime change can undermine the certificate exactly when safety matters most. We present Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF), which rank a finite bank of candidate dynamics by recent prediction error over a short look-back window and enforce the high-order CBF condition against every model within a tolerance of the best, spanning best-fit adaptation to full-bank robust filtering. The dynamics may depend nonlinearly on the unknown parameters, and no switching model or continuously parameterized estimator is required. We prove that any feasible filtered input satisfies the true CBF condition whenever a safety-representative candidate is retained. In quadrotor simulation with abrupt wind reversals and an unknown payload, LBA-CBF is safe and reaches the goal from all random initial conditions, matching an oracle, while adaptive and robust baselines achieve 0-88% success. Banks of up to 250,000 models run inside the control loop, and Crazyflie 2.1 and F1TENTH experiments demonstrate adaptation to wind, payload release, and varying tire-road friction. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [246] [DepthWorld: 3D World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08780)
*Jai Bardhan,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DROID-3D校准3D数据集与DepthWorld世界模型，通过联合预测多视角RGB与深度，实现几何一致的3D世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的世界模型仅用RGB训练，虽逐帧看似正确，但无法构成一致的3D世界，缺乏忠实的三维几何。这一差距需要两方面进展：大规模3D监督数据和能吸收该监督而不破坏预训练视频先验的架构。

Method: 提出校准流程：结合学习到的立体深度与联合因子图，汇集同一物理机器人采集的所有episode，恢复其共享运动学参数及各场景外参。应用于DROID数据集得到DROID-3D校准3D数据集，提供稠密度量深度与重校准多视角外参。随后训练DepthWorld，基于Stable Video Diffusion，通过空间潜变量分块联合预测多视角RGB与深度，保持预训练VAE不变。

Result: DROID-3D在90%的episode外部相机上实现<0.7 px重投影误差。DepthWorld在相同训练预算下，相比纯RGB基线RGB预测PSNR提升+1.48 dB，同时产出准确的度量深度用于下游几何推理。

Conclusion: 深度监督不仅提升RGB预测质量，还提供度量深度，使世界模型具备几何一致性，为机器人策略评估、改进与规划提供更忠实的3D基础。

Abstract: World models offer a data-driven alternative to traditional simulators for robotics, with applications spanning policy evaluation, improvement, and planning. All of these uses depend on faithful 3D geometry, yet current video-based world models are trained on RGB alone and produce rollouts that look correct frame-by-frame but do not compose into a consistent 3D world. Closing this gap requires progress on two fronts: large-scale 3D supervision for manipulation, and an architecture that can absorb it without disturbing strong pretrained video priors. We introduce a calibration pipeline that combines learned stereo depth with a joint factor graph, pooling all episodes collected from the same physical robot to recover its shared kinematic parameters alongside per-scene extrinsics. Applied to the DROID dataset, this yields DROID-3D, a calibrated 3D dataset providing dense metric depth and recalibrated multi-view extrinsics (achieving <0.7 px reprojection error on 90% of episodes for external cameras). We then train DepthWorld, a Stable Video Diffusion-based world model that jointly predicts multi-view RGB and depth via spatial latent tiling, leaving the pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) unchanged. Depth supervision improves RGB prediction itself by +1.48 dB PSNR over an identical RGB-only baseline at equal training budget, while simultaneously yielding accurate metric depth for downstream geometric reasoning.

</details>
